# Delivery notes — Ahd-e-Rishta front end

Companion to `README.md`. Everything below describes the state of the delivered code in this
workspace: what was built, every file, the assumptions behind it, and exactly what the client has to
replace before launch.

Build gates at hand-off:

| Gate          | Command                                   | Result                                    |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| TypeScript    | `npx tsc -b`                              | exit 0, no errors                         |
| Lint          | `npx eslint .`                            | 0 errors, 0 warnings                      |
| Format        | `npx prettier --check`                    | clean                                     |
| Build         | `npm run build`                           | success, `dist/` written (~20 s, 2 cores) |
| Accessibility | axe-core WCAG 2.0/2.1/2.2 A+AA, 20 routes | 0 violations                              |
| Console       | 24 routes × 3 viewports (360/768/1440)    | no errors, no page exceptions             |
| Overflow      | same sweep, `window.scrollX` after scroll | 0 px on every route at every width        |

Size: 142 TypeScript files, ~15 000 lines under `src/`.

---

## 1. Routes delivered

| Route                                  | Page                                | Notes                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `/`                                    | `HomePage`                          | 22 sections in the specified order                                                                                                              |
| `/profiles`                            | `ProfilesPage`                      | URL-synced filters, sort, chips, pagination, grid/list, skeletons, empty state, mobile filter sheet                                             |
| `/profiles/:id`                        | `ProfileDetailPage`                 | Breadcrumb, facts list, similar profiles, print stylesheet, no personal data in SEO head                                                        |
| `/register`                            | `RegisterPage`                      | 6-step wizard, benefits panel, "What happens next", thank-you screen with reference number                                                      |
| `/services`, `/services/:slug`         | `ServicesPage`, `ServiceDetailPage` | 6 services: `rishta-matching`, `personal-matchmaking`, `second-marriage`, `overseas-proposals`, `marriage-counselling`, `face-to-face-meetings` |
| `/packages`                            | `PackagesPage`                      | Essential / Select / Premium, horizontally scrollable comparison table, bank transfer + JazzCash + Easypaisa copy, refund note                  |
| `/how-it-works`                        | `HowItWorksPage`                    | 6-stage timeline                                                                                                                                |
| `/success-stories`                     | `SuccessStoriesPage`                | City + year filters, detail dialog, share, "sample stories" notice                                                                              |
| `/videos`                              | `VideosPage`                        | Topic chips, dialog player                                                                                                                      |
| `/about`, `/team`                      | `AboutPage`, `TeamPage`             | Founder message on the home page is mirrored in About                                                                                           |
| `/contact`                             | `ContactPage`                       | zod form, honeypot, map placeholder, office hours                                                                                               |
| `/faq`                                 | `FaqPage`                           | Search + grouped accordions                                                                                                                     |
| `/safety-tips`                         | `SafetyTipsPage`                    | Fake-matchmaker guidance + report CTA                                                                                                           |
| `/blog`, `/blog/:slug`                 | `BlogPage`, `BlogPostPage`          | 6 posts                                                                                                                                         |
| `/shortlist`                           | `ShortlistPage`                     | localStorage-backed, header count badge                                                                                                         |
| `/terms`, `/privacy`, `/refund-policy` | `LegalPage` (3 variants)            | Shared renderer, `legalContent.ts`                                                                                                              |
| `/login`                               | `LoginPage`                         | Form-only stub; clearly marked "coming soon" copy, no auth                                                                                      |
| `*`                                    | `NotFoundPage`                      | 404 with search + suggested links                                                                                                               |

Plus `ErrorBoundary` around the router and a route-level `Suspense` fallback (`PageSkeleton`) for
every lazy chunk.

---

## 2. File inventory — everything created

Only files listed here were created; nothing pre-existed in the workspace.

### Root / config (15)

| File                                                         | Purpose                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `package.json`                                               | deps + scripts (`dev`, `build`, `preview`, `lint`, `typecheck`, `format`)                                                 |
| `package-lock.json`                                          | lockfile                                                                                                                  |
| `vite.config.ts`                                             | react + tailwind v4 plugins, `@` → `./src`, dev/preview on `0.0.0.0` with `allowedHosts`, `manualChunks` (vendor, motion) |
| `tsconfig.json` / `tsconfig.app.json` / `tsconfig.node.json` | strict, `noUnusedLocals`, `@/*` paths                                                                                     |
| `eslint.config.js`                                           | flat config: typescript-eslint recommended, react-hooks, react-refresh, `no-console` (warn/error/info allowed)            |
| `.prettierrc`                                                | project formatting                                                                                                        |
| `.env` / `.env.example`                                      | API + WhatsApp variables                                                                                                  |
| `.gitignore`                                                 | node_modules, dist, env                                                                                                   |
| `components.json`                                            | shadcn/ui manifest (`@/components`, Tailwind v4, no config file)                                                          |
| `index.html`                                                 | lang="en", fonts preconnect-free, base meta, noscript                                                                     |
| `public/favicon.svg`                                         | monogram mark                                                                                                             |
| `public/robots.txt`                                          | allows all, points to sitemap                                                                                             |
| `public/sitemap.xml`                                         | all static routes, `http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9`                                                          |
| `README.md`, `DELIVERY.md`                                   | this documentation                                                                                                        |

### Application shell (5)

`src/main.tsx` (QueryClient, i18n, router, Sonner, ErrorBoundary) · `src/App.tsx` (23 lazy routes under `Layout`) ·
`src/index.css` (Tailwind v4 `@theme` tokens, base layer, RTL/Urdu rules, print rules, `--animate-*`) ·
`src/vite-env.d.ts` · `src/types/index.ts` (shared view-model types).

### `src/lib/` (10)

| File                     | Purpose                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `site.ts`                | `siteConfig`: name, tagline, phone, email, address, office hours, social links, `appLinks` (empty → app section hides)                            |
| `env.ts`                 | zod-validated env with demo fallbacks                                                                                                             |
| `whatsapp.ts`            | `buildWhatsAppUrl({ context, profileId, plan })` — every wa.me link goes through this                                                             |
| `analytics.ts`           | `trackEvent()` no-op placeholder with one documented integration point                                                                            |
| `filterOptions.ts`       | Cities, countries, sects, castes, education, occupation, income bands (country-state-city for the country list)                                   |
| `filterChips.ts`         | Active-filter chip descriptors + removal mapping                                                                                                  |
| `homeContent.ts`         | Trust badges, services summary, stats, cities, proposals tabs, how-it-works steps, membership per-band copy, safety tips, why-choose-us, app copy |
| `legalContent.ts`        | Terms, privacy, refund policy documents                                                                                                           |
| `utils.ts`               | `cn()`                                                                                                                                            |
| `animations/variants.ts` | fade/slide/stagger variants honouring reduced motion                                                                                              |

### `src/schemas/` (6)

`common.schema.ts` (Pakistani mobile regex, email, name, `shortText` helper) · `profile.schema.ts` ·
`matchFilter.schema.ts` (the URL filter contract, all fields optional + coercion) · `register.schema.ts`
(the 6-step wizard) · `inquiry.schema.ts` (contact form + honeypot) · `content.schema.ts` (stories,
videos, posts, plans, services, FAQs, consultants, stats).

### `src/api/` (19)

`http.ts` (fetch wrapper, timeout, error normalising, zod parse) · `mock/delay.ts` (artificial latency

- abort support) · `mock/profiles.mock.ts` (96 profiles) · `mock/profiles.query.ts` (filter/sort/paginate) ·
  `mock/content.mock.ts` (services, plans, FAQs, consultants, stories, videos, stats) · `mock/posts.mock.ts` (6 posts) ·
  and one thin API module per resource: `profiles`, `services`, `plans`, `faqs`, `consultants`,
  `successStories`, `videos`, `posts`, `stats`, `heroSlides`, `inquiries`, `registrations`, `newsletter`.

**Endpoint contract to implement server-side** (`VITE_USE_MOCK_API=false`):

```
GET  /profiles?...filters&page&pageSize   -> { items, page, pageSize, total, totalPages }
GET  /profiles/:id                        -> Profile
GET  /profiles?ids=AER-1045,AER-1061      -> Profile[]
GET  /services | /services/:slug          -> Service[] | Service
GET  /plans | /faqs | /consultants | /stats | /hero-slides
GET  /success-stories | /videos | /posts | /posts/:slug
POST /inquiries | /registrations | /newsletter  -> { id }
```

### `src/hooks/` (6)

`useShortlist.ts` (`ids, count, has, toggle, add, remove, clear`; storage key `ahd-e-rishta.shortlist.v1`) ·
`useProfileFilters.ts` (URL ↔ zod filter object, `activeCount`, `removeFilter`, `clearAll`) ·
`useApiQueries.ts` (TanStack Query wrappers incl. `useProfilesByIds`) · `useSeo.ts` (title, meta, OG,
canonical, JSON-LD) · `useMediaQuery.ts` (`useIsDesktop` = ≥1024px) · `useDebouncedValue.ts` (350 ms).

### `src/i18n/` (2)

`index.ts` (i18next init, language persistence, lazy Urdu font import, `html lang/dir` sync) ·
`resources.ts` (EN + UR namespaces: `nav`, `common`, `home`, `forms`, `errors`, `footer`, `language`).

### `src/components/ui/` (19)

`accordion` · `badge` · `breadcrumb` · `button` (variants incl. `whatsapp`, `peach`) · `card` ·
`carousel` (embla + autoplay) · `checkbox` · `dialog` · `input` · `label` · `option-select` (native
select styled to match) · `pagination` · `separator` · `sheet` · `skeleton` · `sonner` · `tabs` ·
`textarea` · `tooltip`.

### `src/components/common/` (10)

`SectionHeading` (eyebrow / heading / gradient bar / description) · `ScrollReveal` + `RevealItem` ·
`DataState` (loading / error / empty in one place) · `PageHero` · `PageSkeleton` · `Logo` · `DynamicIcon` ·
`VideoDialog` + `VideoCard` (youtube-nocookie, iframe only after a click) · `StoryCard` ·
`WhatsAppIcon`.

### `src/components/layout/` (9)

`Header` (announcement bar, mega-free desktop nav, language switcher, register + WhatsApp CTA,
shortlist badge, mobile sheet with phone fallback) · `AnnouncementBar` (dismissible, persisted) ·
`LanguageSwitcher` (EN / اردو) · `Footer` (4 columns, legal links, newsletter, contact, socials) ·
`FloatingActions` (WhatsApp pill + back-to-top, compact below `lg`) · `ScrollToTop` (route change) ·
`CookieConsent` (persisted) · `Layout` (skip link, header, `<main>`, footer, floating actions) ·
`ErrorBoundary` (friendly recovery screen).

### `src/components/forms/` (9)

`FormField.tsx` (label, hint, error, wires `aria-describedby`/`aria-invalid` via `cloneElement`) ·
`ComboboxField.tsx` (searchable select for city/caste/education) · `NewsletterForm.tsx` ·
`ContactForm.tsx` (zod + honeypot) · `RegisterForm.tsx` (wizard shell, step validation, review step) ·
`registerSteps.ts` (step definitions + per-step schemas) · `registerMessage.ts` (WhatsApp summary
text of a submission) · `RegisterSuccess.tsx` (thank-you screen, reference number, WhatsApp hand-off).

### `src/components/profiles/` (6)

`ProfileCard.tsx` (photo, name + last initial, ID, facts, shortlist button, no contact data) ·
`ProfilePhoto.tsx` (local SVG placeholder art, `aspect-square sm:aspect-4/5`) · `ShortlistButton.tsx`
(accessible toggle + toast) · `ShareButton.tsx` (Web Share + clipboard fallback) ·
`ProfileCarousel.tsx` (embla, keyboard accessible) · `FilterPanel.tsx` (all filters, `showTitle` prop
so the mobile sheet doesn't repeat the heading).

### `src/components/home/` (22)

`HeroCarousel` (3 slides with locally bundled photography, one `h1`) · `FindMatchSection` ·
`TrustStrip` · `ServicesSection` · `StatsSection` · `BrowseByCitySection` · `ProposalsTabsSection`
(Pakistan / Overseas tabs, 4 cards each) · `FeaturedProfilesSection` (Grooms / Brides in one `Tabs`
root) · `HowItWorksSection` · `SecondMarriageSection` (dark maroon band, divorced/widowed tabs) ·
`MembershipPlansSection` · `ConsultantsSection` · `FounderMessageSection` (Great Vibes quote marks +
signature) · `SafetySection` · `SuccessStoriesSection` · `VideosSection` · `WhyChooseUsSection` ·
`LatestProfilesSection` · `MobileAppSection` (renders only if `siteConfig.appLinks` is non-empty) ·
`FaqSection` (10 questions) · `NewsletterSection` · `CtaSection`.

### `src/pages/` (21 files, 23 routes)

`HomePage` · `ProfilesPage` · `ProfileDetailPage` · `RegisterPage` · `ServicesPage` ·
`ServiceDetailPage` · `PackagesPage` · `HowItWorksPage` · `SuccessStoriesPage` · `VideosPage` ·
`AboutPage` · `TeamPage` · `ContactPage` · `FaqPage` · `SafetyTipsPage` · `BlogPage` ·
`BlogPostPage` · `ShortlistPage` · `LegalPage` · `LoginPage` · `NotFoundPage`.

### `src/assets/photos/` (15)

- `hero-1.jpg`, `hero-2.jpg`, `hero-3.jpg` — the three home hero photographs (floral still life,
  hands over embroidery, candlelit celebration table). Generated for this project, bundled locally,
  and free of faces so no likeness is implied.
- `post-1..6.svg` — six on-brand abstract article covers (arches, lattice, rings, chevrons, dot grid,
  layered arcs) in the khaki/maroon palette, ~1 kB each and inlined by Vite.
- `female-1..3.svg`, `male-1..3.svg` — abstract, non-photographic placeholder profile portraits (no
  real people, so no likeness rights issue while the client sources consented photography).

`public/og-image.jpg` — 1200×630 social share image cropped from `hero-1.jpg`, referenced as the
default OG image by `useSeo`.

---

## 3. Assumptions made

1. **Language scope.** The i18n _infrastructure_ is complete and toggling to اردو flips direction,
   navigation, footer and shared UI strings. Page body copy (hero paragraphs, service descriptions,
   blog posts, legal text) is English-only in this delivery — a full Urdu translation pass is content
   work, not code work. The hero headline and description remain English when اردو is selected; the
   layout, fonts and direction switch correctly.
2. **Imagery is placeholder.** The three hero photographs and six article covers were produced for
   this build and ship inside the bundle; the profile portraits are abstract SVGs. Nothing is
   borrowed from a competitor or a stock library, so the client can swap in their own approved
   photography without a licensing trail. Video thumbnails are the only remote images (YouTube's
   CDN) and they degrade to a local arch pattern if the network blocks them.
3. **No real profiles.** All 96 profiles, 6 success stories, 6 videos, 6 blog posts and 4 consultants
   are invented. Photos are abstract SVGs rather than stock people, matching the brief's ban on copied
   competitor imagery. Video entries point at placeholder YouTube ids — the player uses
   `youtube-nocookie.com` and only mounts an iframe after a click.
4. **App section hidden.** `siteConfig.appLinks` is empty, so the mobile-app band is not rendered.
   Filling in the two store URLs makes it appear; nothing else changes.
5. **Payments are copy only.** Packages mention bank transfer, JazzCash and Easypaisa as _offline_
   steps with a refund note; there is deliberately no gateway, card field or "Pay now" flow.
6. **Bureau model.** There is no self-service messaging between members. Every contact path is a
   WhatsApp deep link with a context string, or the register/inquiry forms. The profile detail page
   withholds all direct contact data by design.
7. **Login is a stub.** `/login` renders a validated form and a "member area coming soon" notice, and
   submits nothing. Removing it is a single route deletion if the client prefers.
8. **Mock latency.** Mock services add 200–450 ms so loading skeletons are visible during review;
   set `VITE_USE_MOCK_API=false` to remove them entirely.
9. **Numbers are illustrative.** Stats such as "profiles reviewed" are round, obviously-placeholder
   figures and the UI never claims percentages, awards, registrations or celebrity endorsements.
10. **Fonts are self-hosted** through Fontsource exactly as instructed, so no third-party font request
    is made at runtime and the Urdu face is only fetched when the language is switched.
11. **Deployment target is a static host.** Sitemap/robots assume the site is served at the root of
    `siteConfig.url`; hash-free client routing means the host needs an SPA rewrite to `index.html`
    (`/profiles` must not 404). No SSR, no server functions.

---

## 4. Placeholders the client must replace

All of these carry the marker `// DUMMY DATA, replace with real data or API`.

**Brand and contact** — `src/lib/site.ts`: `name`, `legalName`, `tagline`, `phone` / `phoneDisplay`,
`email`, office `addressLines`, `officeHours`, `social` URLs, `url` (used for canonical + sitemap),
`appLinks.playStore` / `appLinks.appStore`.

**WhatsApp** — `.env`: `VITE_WHATSAPP_NUMBER` (currently `923001234567`) and `VITE_WHATSAPP_MESSAGE`.
This number appears in the header, footer, floating pill, every CTA band and the register hand-off.

**Imagery** — `src/assets/photos/`: replace `hero-1..3.jpg` with the bureau's approved photography
(keep the same filenames and the imports in `src/api/mock/content.mock.ts` stay valid), swap
`post-1..6.svg` for real article images if desired, and replace the six abstract profile portraits
with consented photographs from the office. Regenerate `public/og-image.jpg` (1200×630) to match the
final hero image. Consultant names, roles and the founder name live in
`src/api/mock/content.mock.ts` and `homeContent.ts`.

**Profiles** — `src/api/mock/profiles.mock.ts`: 96 sample profiles (IDs `AER-1001`…). Whether these
come from the API or SQL, the card contract is `first name + last initial + profile ID` only.

**Content collections** — `src/api/mock/content.mock.ts` (services, plans and pricing, FAQs,
consultants, success stories, videos, stats) and `src/api/mock/posts.mock.ts` (6 blog posts).

**Legal** — `src/lib/legalContent.ts`: terms, privacy policy, refund policy. These are structural
templates, not legal advice — a Pakistani lawyer should review them before launch.

**Page copy** — `src/lib/homeContent.ts` (trust badges, how-it-works steps, safety tips, why-choose-us,
membership band copy) and the JSX strings inside `src/pages/*` for the About/Team narrative.

**Analytics** — `src/lib/analytics.ts`: `trackEvent()` currently a no-op. Add the GA4/GTM/Meta Pixel
call in the single documented place.

**Map** — `src/pages/ContactPage.tsx`: styled map placeholder. Drop in a Google Maps embed or a
keyed Mapbox component where the placeholder sits.

**SEO assets** — `index.html`, `public/og-image.jpg`, `VITE_SITE_URL`, `public/sitemap.xml` (replace the demo domain and
re-generate when blog/service slugs are final), `src/lib/site.ts` `url`.

**Urdu translations** — `src/i18n/resources.ts`: fill the `ur` block per page; the plumbing already
handles direction, fonts and persistence.

---

## 5. Verified behaviour (manual + scripted QA)

- Filters on `/profiles` round-trip through the URL, restore on reload, render removable chips, and
  reset with "Clear all"; sort and grid/list toggle persist in the query string.
- Mobile filter sheet opens from the toolbar, applies and closes; results count updates.
- Register wizard: blocks on a missing required select with `role="alert"`, advances through all six
  steps, and a full submit reaches the thank-you screen with a generated reference number
  (`REG-…`) and no console output of submitted data.
- Shortlist: heart toggles without navigating, badge count in the header updates, `/shortlist`
  restores from localStorage after reload, discussion CTA opens WhatsApp with the shortlist context.
- Newsletter rejects a malformed address with the inline message "Enter a valid email address";
  contact form surfaces four field-level alerts and a honeypot that silently rejects bots.
- RTL: switching to اردو sets `<html lang="ur" dir="rtl">`, mirrors navigation, hero and footer, and
  loads the Nastaliq face; no layout breakage at 360 px.
- Print: `/profiles/:id` prints without chrome (header, footer and floating actions hidden), with the
  bureau contact block at the top.
- Reduced motion: all reveal/carousel motion collapses under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`.

---

## 6. Known limitations (by design, not bugs)

- Single-page app: needs an SPA fallback on the host for deep links.
- No authentication, no member area, no payment gateway, no admin — out of scope per the brief.
- Urdu covers navigation and shared UI strings only (see assumption 1).
- YouTube is the only remote dependency: thumbnails load from `i.ytimg.com` (with a local fallback
  pattern if blocked) and the privacy-enhanced player only mounts after a visitor clicks. In a
  network-isolated preview the player area will stay black — that is expected, and it works in a
  normal browser.
- `dist/` warns that the main chunk is ~568 kB before gzip because shadcn/Radix + motion are bundled;
  vendor and motion are already split into separate chunks and the page is route-lazy-loaded. Further
  trimming would mean dropping Radix primitives, which the stack requires.

========================================
FILE: README.md
========================================
# Ahd-e-Rishta — front end

A complete, production-quality **front end** for _Ahd-e-Rishta_, a bureau-managed Pakistani
matrimonial service. 23 routes, no admin panel, no backend: every data call goes through a typed
API layer that returns zod-validated mock data (switchable to a real API with one env flag).

```bash
npm install
cp .env.example .env      # then edit the WhatsApp number
npm run dev               # http://localhost:5173
npm run build             # tsc -b && vite build  ->  dist/
npm run preview           # serve dist/ on :4173
npm run lint              # eslint (flat config)
npm run typecheck         # tsc -b --noEmit style check
npm run format            # prettier --write
```

Node 20+, npm 10+. Fonts, hero photography and article art are all bundled locally — no Google Fonts
CDN and no external image host — so the built site renders fully inside a CSP-restricted preview
iframe. The only third-party requests happen after a deliberate click (YouTube player) or when a
YouTube thumbnail is available.

---

## Stack (as specified)

React 19 · TypeScript strict · Vite 7 · Tailwind CSS v4 (`@theme` tokens only, no `tailwind.config.js`) ·
shadcn/ui (Radix primitives) · react-router-dom v7 · react-hook-form + zod · motion/react ·
embla-carousel (shadcn `Carousel`) · lucide-react · country-state-city · TanStack Query · Fontsource.

---

## Environment

| Variable                | Purpose                                                                              |
| ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `VITE_API_URL`          | Base URL when `VITE_USE_MOCK_API=false`                                              |
| `VITE_USE_MOCK_API`     | `true` → in-memory mock services (default). `false` → `fetch` against `VITE_API_URL` |
| `VITE_WHATSAPP_NUMBER`  | Digits-only international number, e.g. `923001234567`                                |
| `VITE_WHATSAPP_MESSAGE` | Default prefilled WhatsApp message                                                   |

`src/lib/env.ts` validates these with zod at boot and falls back to safe demo values, so a missing
`.env` never breaks the build.

---

## Architecture

```
src/
  api/          typed client — http.ts + one *.api.ts per resource + mock/ implementations
  schemas/      zod contracts shared by forms, filters and API response validation
  hooks/        useShortlist, useProfileFilters, useApiQueries (TanStack), useSeo, media/debounce
  lib/          siteConfig, WhatsApp builder, analytics, filter options, page copy, animations
  components/
    ui/         shadcn primitives (locally owned, Tailwind v4 classes)
    common/     SectionHeading, ScrollReveal, DataState, PageHero, skeletons, dialogs
    layout/     Header, Footer, AnnouncementBar, FloatingActions, CookieConsent, ErrorBoundary
    forms/      FormField/ComboboxField wrappers + Newsletter, Contact, Register wizard
    profiles/   ProfileCard, ProfileCarousel, FilterPanel, ShortlistButton, ShareButton
    home/       the 22 home sections
  pages/        one file per route (default export, lazy-loaded)
  i18n/         react-i18next resources (EN + Urdu) — Urdu fonts loaded on demand
```

**Data layer rule:** a page never imports a mock directly. It calls `profilesApi.getProfiles(...)`
etc.; the module picks mock vs HTTP at runtime and validates the response with the same zod schema
the mock is typed against. Swapping in a real backend means implementing the documented endpoints
only — no component changes. See `DELIVERY.md` for the endpoint contract.

**State:** server state in TanStack Query (query keys are derived from the filter object),
URL state for `/profiles` filters (shareable links, back-button safe), localStorage for the
shortlist and language.

---

## Design system

All tokens live in `src/index.css` under `@theme`:

- **maroon** 50–950 (primary: 800/900, deepest 950 for the dark bands)
- **peach-400** — accent, used for the italic accent word and highlight rules only
- **cream** 50/100 — page and section backgrounds; cards are white
- **khaki** #C3B091 — hairline ornaments, rules, dividers
- **green-800/900** — _only_ the WhatsApp affordances, never decoration

Type: `font-heading` Fraunces (600, tracking-tight), `font-sans` Plus Jakarta Sans, `font-script`
Great Vibes (owner signature + quote marks only), `font-urdu` Noto Nastaliq Urdu applied via
`:lang(ur)` at line-height 2.1 and lazy-loaded the first time the user picks اردو.
Body copy is 16px/1.7, nothing below 14px, measure capped near 65 characters.

Motion is centralised in `src/lib/animations/variants.ts` and wrapped by `ScrollReveal` /
`RevealItem`; every variant collapses to a no-op under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`.

Accessibility target is WCAG 2.2 AA — audited with axe-core on 20 routes, 0 violations. Skip link,
visible focus rings on maroon, one `h1` per page, labelled controls, `role="alert"` form errors,
keyboard-operable carousels/sheets/dialogs, and 44px minimum touch targets.

---

## Privacy by design

Profile cards and detail pages show **first name + last initial + profile ID** only. No phone
numbers, no street addresses, no guardian contact, no exact dates — contact is always routed through
the bureau (WhatsApp / register form). Profile detail pages also omit personal data from the SEO
head so nothing is indexed.

---

## Where the content lives

Every piece of copy that a client would own is in one of two places, each marked
`// DUMMY DATA, replace with real data or API`:

- `src/lib/*.ts` — site config, home page copy, legal text, service/plan/FAQ/safety content
- `src/api/mock/*.ts` — profiles, success stories, videos, blog posts, consultants

See `DELIVERY.md` → _Placeholders the client must replace_ for the full checklist.

FILE: src\App.tsx
========================================
import { lazy } from "react";
import { Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { Layout } from "@/components/layout/Layout";
import { ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/layout/ErrorBoundary";

const HomePage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/HomePage"));
const ProfilesPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ProfilesPage"));
const ProfileDetailPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ProfileDetailPage"));
const RegisterPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/RegisterPage"));
const ServicesPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ServicesPage"));
const ServiceDetailPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ServiceDetailPage"));
const PackagesPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/PackagesPage"));
const HowItWorksPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/HowItWorksPage"));
const SuccessStoriesPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/SuccessStoriesPage"));
const VideosPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/VideosPage"));
const AboutPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/AboutPage"));
const TeamPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/TeamPage"));
const ContactPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ContactPage"));
const FaqPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/FaqPage"));
const SafetyTipsPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/SafetyTipsPage"));
const BlogPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/BlogPage"));
const BlogPostPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/BlogPostPage"));
const ShortlistPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/ShortlistPage"));
const LegalPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/LegalPage"));
const LoginPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/LoginPage"));
const NotFoundPage = lazy(() => import("@/pages/NotFoundPage"));

export default function App() {
  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
      <Routes>
        <Route element={<Layout />}>
          <Route index element={<HomePage />} />
          <Route path="profiles" element={<ProfilesPage />} />
          <Route path="profiles/:id" element={<ProfileDetailPage />} />
          <Route path="register" element={<RegisterPage />} />
          <Route path="services" element={<ServicesPage />} />
          <Route path="services/:slug" element={<ServiceDetailPage />} />
          <Route path="packages" element={<PackagesPage />} />
          <Route path="how-it-works" element={<HowItWorksPage />} />
          <Route path="success-stories" element={<SuccessStoriesPage />} />
          <Route path="videos" element={<VideosPage />} />
          <Route path="about" element={<AboutPage />} />
          <Route path="team" element={<TeamPage />} />
          <Route path="contact" element={<ContactPage />} />
          <Route path="faq" element={<FaqPage />} />
          <Route path="safety-tips" element={<SafetyTipsPage />} />
          <Route path="blog" element={<BlogPage />} />
          <Route path="blog/:slug" element={<BlogPostPage />} />
          <Route path="shortlist" element={<ShortlistPage />} />
          <Route path="terms" element={<LegalPage variant="terms" />} />
          <Route path="privacy" element={<LegalPage variant="privacy" />} />
          <Route path="refund-policy" element={<LegalPage variant="refund" />} />
          <Route path="login" element={<LoginPage />} />
          <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

FILE: src\main.tsx

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import "@fontsource-variable/fraunces";
import "@fontsource-variable/fraunces/wght-italic.css";
import "@fontsource-variable/plus-jakarta-sans";
import "@fontsource/great-vibes";
import "@/index.css";

import "@/i18n";
import App from "@/App";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/layout/ErrorBoundary";

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5,
      gcTime: 1000 * 60 * 30,
      retry: 1,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
    },
  },
});

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");
if (!rootElement) throw new Error("Root element #root was not found");

ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <ErrorBoundary>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <TooltipProvider delayDuration={200}>
            <App />
            <Toaster />
          </TooltipProvider>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  </React.StrictMode>,
);

}

========================================
FILE: src\api\http.ts
========================================
import type { z } from "zod";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly details?: unknown;

  constructor(message: string, status = 500, details?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.details = details;
  }
}

export interface RequestOptions<T> {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";
  body?: unknown;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  schema: z.ZodType<T>;
  /** Milliseconds before the request is aborted. */
  timeout?: number;
}

/**
 * Thin, typed fetch wrapper. Every response is validated with zod so a backend
 * change cannot silently break the UI. Swapping mocks for a real API only
 * touches the service files, never the components.
 */
export async function httpRequest<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions<T>): Promise<T> {
  const { method = "GET", body, signal, schema, timeout = 15000 } = options;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeout);

  const onAbort = () => controller.abort();
  signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${env.apiUrl.replace(/\/$/, "")}${path}`, {
      method,
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
        ...(body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      },
      body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      let details: unknown;
      try {
        details = await response.json();
      } catch {
        details = undefined;
      }
      throw new ApiError(
        response.status === 404
          ? "We could not find what you were looking for."
          : "Something went wrong while loading this section. Please try again.",
        response.status,
        details,
      );
    }

    const json: unknown = await response.json();
    const parsed = schema.safeParse(json);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "The server returned data we could not read.",
        502,
        parsed.error.flatten(),
      );
    }
    return parsed.data;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) throw error;
    if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === "AbortError") throw error;
    throw new ApiError("We could not reach the server. Please check your connection.", 0, error);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
  }
}

import { z } from "zod";
import { httpRequest } from "@/api/http";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { mockDelay } from "@/api/mock/delay";
import { contactInquirySchema, type ContactInquiry } from "@/schemas/inquiry.schema";

const submittedSchema = z.object({ id: z.string(), receivedAt: z.string() });

/** DUMMY: resolves locally. Replace the branch with the real POST when the API is ready. */
export async function submitContactInquiry(payload: ContactInquiry, signal?: AbortSignal) {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(600, signal);
    return { id: `INQ-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`, receivedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  }
  return httpRequest("/inquiries", {
    method: "POST",
    body: contactInquirySchema.parse(payload),
    schema: submittedSchema,
    signal,
  });
}

import { z } from "zod";
import { httpRequest } from "@/api/http";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { mockDelay } from "@/api/mock/delay";
import { blogPostSchema } from "@/schemas/content.schema";
import { dummyPosts } from "@/api/mock/posts.mock";

export async function getPosts(signal?: AbortSignal) {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(220, signal);
    return dummyPosts;
  }
  return httpRequest("/posts", { schema: z.array(blogPostSchema), signal });
}

export async function getPostBySlug(slug: string, signal?: AbortSignal) {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(240, signal);
    const post = dummyPosts.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
    if (!post) throw new Error("Post not found");
    return post;
  }
  return httpRequest(`/posts/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`, { schema: blogPostSchema, signal });
}

FILE: src\api\profiles.api.ts
========================================
import { z } from "zod";
import { httpRequest } from "@/api/http";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { mockDelay } from "@/api/mock/delay";
import { paginatedSchema } from "@/schemas/common.schema";
import { profileSchema, type Profile, type ProfileFilters } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import {
  featuredProfiles,
  findProfile,
  latestProfiles,
  profilesByLocation,
  queryProfiles,
  secondMarriageProfiles,
  similarProfiles,
} from "@/api/mock/profiles.query";

const paginatedProfilesSchema = paginatedSchema(profileSchema);

function toQueryString(filters: ProfileFilters): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(filters).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") return;
    params.set(key, String(value));
  });
  const query = params.toString();
  return query ? `?${query}` : "";
}

export interface ProfilesPage {
  items: Profile[];
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  total: number;
  totalPages: number;
}

export async function getProfiles(
  filters: ProfileFilters,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<ProfilesPage> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(360, signal);
    return queryProfiles(filters);
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles${toQueryString(filters)}`, {
    schema: paginatedProfilesSchema,
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getProfileById(id: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Profile> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(300, signal);
    const profile = findProfile(id);
    if (!profile) throw new Error("Profile not found");
    return profile;
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { schema: profileSchema, signal });
}

export async function getProfilesByIds(ids: string[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return [];
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(220, signal);
    return ids
      .map((id) => findProfile(id))
      .filter((profile): profile is Profile => Boolean(profile));
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/by-ids?ids=${encodeURIComponent(ids.join(","))}`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getSimilarProfiles(
  profile: Profile,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(300, signal);
    return similarProfiles(profile);
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/${encodeURIComponent(profile.id)}/similar`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getFeaturedProfiles(
  gender: "male" | "female",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(280, signal);
    return featuredProfiles(gender);
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/featured?gender=${gender}`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getLatestProfiles(
  gender: "male" | "female",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(280, signal);
    return latestProfiles(gender);
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/latest?gender=${gender}`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getSecondMarriageProfiles(
  maritalStatus: "divorced" | "widowed",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(280, signal);
    return secondMarriageProfiles(maritalStatus);
  }
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/second-marriage?maritalStatus=${maritalStatus}`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

export async function getProfilesByLocation(
  filter: { city?: string; country?: string },
  limit = 4,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Profile[]> {
  if (env.useMockApi) {
    await mockDelay(240, signal);
    return profilesByLocation(filter, limit);
  }
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ ...filter, limit: String(limit) });
  return httpRequest(`/profiles/by-location?${params.toString()}`, {
    schema: z.array(profileSchema),
    signal,
  });
}

========================================
FILE: src\api\mock\delay.ts
========================================
/** DUMMY DATA helper — simulates network latency so loading states are real. */
export function mockDelay(ms = 320, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
      resolve();
    }, ms);
    function onAbort() {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
    }
    signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
  });
}

        paragraphs: [
          "If a candidate goes quiet, do not fill the silence with assurances. Ask, privately, what they are thinking.",
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
];

  "Quiet, hardworking and family-first. Prefers a modest wedding and clear communication between both families.",

/** Deterministic pseudo-random generator so mock data never changes between builds. */
function createRandom(seed: number) {
  let state = seed;
  return () => {
    state = (state * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296;
    return state / 4294967296;
  };
}

const photoSets: Record<"male" | "female", string[]> = {
  male: [male1, male2, male3],
  female: [female1, female2, female3],
};

function pad(value: number, size = 4): string {
  return String(value).padStart(size, "0");
}

function buildProfiles(count: number): Profile[] {
  const random = createRandom(20261109);
  const profiles: Profile[] = [];

  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
    const gender: "male" | "female" = index % 2 === 0 ? "female" : "male";
    const isOverseas = random() < 0.22;
    const location = isOverseas
      ? overseasLocations[Math.floor(random() * overseasLocations.length)]
      : pakistanLocations[Math.floor(random() * pakistanLocations.length)];

    const ageRoll = random();
    const age = ageRoll < 0.55 ? 22 + Math.floor(random() * 8) : 30 + Math.floor(random() * 12);

    const maritalRoll = random();
    const maritalStatus =
      maritalRoll < 0.74 ? "never-married" : maritalRoll < 0.9 ? "divorced" : "widowed";

    const name =
      gender === "female"
        ? femaleNames[Math.floor(random() * femaleNames.length)]
        : maleNames[Math.floor(random() * maleNames.length)];
    const displayName = `${name} ${lastInitials[Math.floor(random() * lastInitials.length)]}`;

    const heightCm =
      gender === "female" ? 145 + Math.floor(random() * 23) : 163 + Math.floor(random() * 26);

    const photoConsent = random() < 0.55;
    const photoSet = photoSets[gender];

    const birthday = new Date();
    birthday.setFullYear(birthday.getFullYear() - age);
    birthday.setMonth(Math.floor(random() * 12));
    birthday.setDate(1 + Math.floor(random() * 27));

    const created = new Date();
    created.setDate(created.getDate() - Math.floor(random() * 90));

    const languageCount = 1 + Math.floor(random() * 3);
    const languages = Array.from(
      new Set([
        gender === "male" ? "English" : "Urdu",
        ...Array.from(
          { length: languageCount },
          () => tongues[Math.floor(random() * tongues.length)],
        ),
      ]),
    ).slice(0, 4);

    const cityIndexSeed = Math.floor(random() * 100);

    profiles.push({
      id: `AER-${pad(1041 + index)}`,
      displayName,
      gender,
      age,
      dateOfBirth: birthday.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      heightCm,
      maritalStatus,
      hasChildren: maritalStatus !== "never-married" && random() < 0.45,
      religion: "Islam",
      sect: random() < 0.82 ? "Sunni" : "Shia",
      caste: castes[cityIndexSeed % castes.length],
      education: educations[Math.floor(random() * educations.length)],
      occupation: occupationsByGender[gender][Math.floor(random() * 10)],
      income: incomes[Math.floor(random() * incomes.length)],
      city: location.city,
      country: location.country,
      languages,
      familyType: random() < 0.45 ? "joint" : "nuclear",
      about:
        gender === "female"
          ? aboutFemale[Math.floor(random() * aboutFemale.length)]
          : aboutMale[Math.floor(random() * aboutMale.length)],
      partnerDemands: {
        ageRange: `${Math.max(18, age - 4)} – ${age + 6} years`,
        heightRange: gender === "female" ? `5' 6" and above` : `5' 0" – 5' 8"`,
        maritalStatus:
          maritalStatus === "never-married" ? "Never married preferred" : "Open to all",
        education: "Bachelors or above preferred",
        occupation: "Any respectable profession",
        city: isOverseas ? "Pakistan or same country" : location.city,
        notes: "A family that values deen, education and kindness. Simple wedding preferred.",
      },
      photoConsent,
      photoUrl: photoConsent ? photoSet[index % photoSet.length] : null,
      verified: random() < 0.9,
      packageName: packageNames[Math.floor(random() * packageNames.length)],
      featured: index % 7 === 0 || index % 11 === 0,
      createdAt: created.toISOString(),
    });
  }

  return profiles.sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1));
}

export const dummyProfiles: Profile[] = buildProfiles(96);

FILE: src\api\mock\profiles.query.ts

/**
 * DUMMY DATA query helpers — mirrors the filtering rules the real API will use.
 */
import type { Profile, ProfileFilters, ProfileSort } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import type { Paginated } from "@/schemas/common.schema";
import { dummyProfiles } from "@/api/mock/profiles.mock";

function matchesFilters(profile: Profile, filters: ProfileFilters): boolean {
  const {
    gender,
    maritalStatus,
    ageFrom,
    ageTo,
    heightFrom,
    heightTo,
    religion,
    sect,
    caste,
    education,
    occupation,
    income,
    city,
    country,
    language,
    familyType,
    q,
    category,
  } = filters;

  if (gender && profile.gender !== gender) return false;
  if (maritalStatus && profile.maritalStatus !== maritalStatus) return false;
  if (ageFrom && profile.age < ageFrom) return false;
  if (ageTo && profile.age > ageTo) return false;
  if (heightFrom && profile.heightCm < heightFrom) return false;
  if (heightTo && profile.heightCm > heightTo) return false;
  if (religion && profile.religion !== religion) return false;
  if (sect && profile.sect !== sect) return false;
  if (caste && profile.caste !== caste) return false;
  if (education && profile.education !== education) return false;
  if (occupation && profile.occupation !== occupation) return false;
  if (income && profile.income !== income) return false;
  if (city && profile.city !== city) return false;
  if (country && profile.country !== country) return false;
  if (language && !profile.languages.includes(language)) return false;
  if (familyType && profile.familyType !== familyType) return false;

  if (category === "featured" && !profile.featured) return false;
  if (category === "latest") {
    /* no extra filter — ordering handles it */
  }
  if (category === "second-marriage" && profile.maritalStatus === "never-married") return false;

  if (q) {
    const needle = q.toLowerCase();
    const haystack = [
      profile.id,
      profile.displayName,
      profile.city,
      profile.country,
      profile.occupation,
    ]
      .join(" ")
      .toLowerCase();
    if (!haystack.includes(needle)) return false;
  }

  return true;
}

function sortProfiles(profiles: Profile[], sort: ProfileSort = "newest"): Profile[] {
  const sorted = [...profiles];
  switch (sort) {
    case "age-asc":
      return sorted.sort((a, b) => a.age - b.age);
    case "age-desc":
      return sorted.sort((a, b) => b.age - a.age);
    case "height-asc":
      return sorted.sort((a, b) => a.heightCm - b.heightCm);
    case "height-desc":
      return sorted.sort((a, b) => b.heightCm - a.heightCm);
    default:
      return sorted.sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1));
  }
}

export function queryProfiles(filters: ProfileFilters): Paginated<Profile> {
  const page = filters.page ?? 1;
  const pageSize = 12;
  const filtered = sortProfiles(
    dummyProfiles.filter((profile) => matchesFilters(profile, filters)),
    filters.sort,
  );
  const total = filtered.length;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const safePage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
  const start = (safePage - 1) * pageSize;

  return {
    items: filtered.slice(start, start + pageSize),
    page: safePage,
    pageSize,
    total,
    totalPages,
  };
}

export function findProfile(id: string): Profile | undefined {
  return dummyProfiles.find((profile) => profile.id.toLowerCase() === id.toLowerCase());
}

export function similarProfiles(profile: Profile, limit = 6): Profile[] {
  return dummyProfiles
    .filter(
      (candidate) =>
        candidate.id !== profile.id &&
        candidate.gender === profile.gender &&
        (candidate.city === profile.city || candidate.caste === profile.caste),
    )
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export function featuredProfiles(gender: "male" | "female", limit = 8): Profile[] {
  return dummyProfiles.filter((p) => p.featured && p.gender === gender).slice(0, limit);
}

export function latestProfiles(gender: "male" | "female", limit = 8): Profile[] {
  return sortProfiles(dummyProfiles.filter((p) => p.gender === gender)).slice(0, limit);
}

export function secondMarriageProfiles(
  maritalStatus: "divorced" | "widowed",
  limit = 8,
): Profile[] {
  return dummyProfiles.filter((p) => p.maritalStatus === maritalStatus).slice(0, limit);
}

export function profilesByLocation(
  filter: { city?: string; country?: string; region?: string },
  limit = 4,
): Profile[] {
  return dummyProfiles
    .filter((profile) => {
      if (filter.city) return profile.city === filter.city;
      if (filter.country) return profile.country === filter.country;
      return true;
    })
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export function profilesByCategory(category: "grooms" | "brides", limit = 8): Profile[] {
  return dummyProfiles
    .filter((p) => p.gender === (category === "grooms" ? "male" : "female"))
    .slice(0, limit);
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Inbox, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function SectionSkeleton({ className, count = 3 }: { className?: string; count?: number }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn("grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3", className)}
      aria-busy="true"
      aria-live="polite"
    >
      <span className="sr-only">Loading content…</span>
      {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, index) => (
        <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5">
          <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full rounded-2xl" />
          <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-5 w-2/3" />
          <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-1/2" />
          <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-9 w-full rounded-full" />
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ErrorState({
  title = "We could not load this section",
  description = "Please check your connection and try again. If it keeps happening, message the office on WhatsApp.",
  onRetry,
  className,
}: {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50/60 px-6 py-10 text-center",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700">
        <AlertTriangle className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <h3 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">{title}</h3>
      <p className="max-w-md text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
      {onRetry ? (
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onRetry} className="mt-1">
          <RefreshCw aria-hidden="true" /> Try again
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({
  title,
  description,
  children,
  className,
  icon,
}: {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  children?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-khaki-300 bg-cream-100/60 px-6 py-12 text-center",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-full bg-white text-maroon-700 shadow-sm">
        {icon ?? <Inbox className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />}
      </span>
      <h3 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">{title}</h3>
      {description ? (
        <p className="max-w-md text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>
      ) : null}
      {children ? (
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">{children}</div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  BadgeCheck,
  Camera,
  FileText,
  Globe,
  HeartCrack,
  HeartHandshake,
  Lock,
  MessageCircle,
  MessagesSquare,
  Phone,
  Search,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserRoundSearch,
  Users,
  UsersRound,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

const icons: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  "shield-check": ShieldCheck,
  lock: Lock,
  users: Users,
  "message-circle": MessageCircle,
  "badge-check": BadgeCheck,
  "heart-handshake": HeartHandshake,
  "heart-crack": HeartCrack,
  globe: Globe,
  "messages-square": MessagesSquare,
  "user-round-search": UserRoundSearch,
  "users-round": UsersRound,
  "file-text": FileText,
  search: Search,
  phone: Phone,
  camera: Camera,
};

export function DynamicIcon({ name, className }: { name: string; className?: string }) {
  const Icon = icons[name] ?? HeartHandshake;
  return <Icon className={className} aria-hidden="true" />;
}

          Trusted Rishta Service

FILE: src\components\common\PageHero.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Breadcrumb,
  BreadcrumbItem,
  BreadcrumbList,
  BreadcrumbPage,
  BreadcrumbSeparator,
} from "@/components/ui/breadcrumb";

export interface PageHeroProps {
  eyebrow?: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  breadcrumb?: { label: string; href?: string }[];
  children?: ReactNode;
  tone?: "cream" | "maroon";
  className?: string;
}

/** Consistent inner-page hero used by every secondary page. */
export function PageHero({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  breadcrumb,
  children,
  tone = "cream",
  className,
}: PageHeroProps) {
  return (
    <section
      className={cn(
        "relative overflow-hidden border-b border-border",
        tone === "maroon" ? "bg-maroon-950 text-cream-50" : "bg-cream-100",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <div aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 opacity-60">
        <div className="absolute -end-24 -top-24 size-72 rounded-full bg-peach-300/25 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute -start-16 bottom-0 size-56 rounded-full bg-khaki-400/20 blur-3xl" />
      </div>
      <div className="relative mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 sm:py-14 lg:py-16">
        {breadcrumb?.length ? (
          <Breadcrumb className="mb-5">
            <BreadcrumbList>
              {breadcrumb.map((crumb, index) => {
                const isLast = index === breadcrumb.length - 1;
                return (
                  <BreadcrumbItem key={`${crumb.label}-${index}`}>
                    {isLast || !crumb.href ? (
                      <BreadcrumbPage className={tone === "maroon" ? "text-cream-100" : undefined}>
                        {crumb.label}
                      </BreadcrumbPage>
                    ) : (
                      <Link
                        to={crumb.href}
                        className={cn(
                          "rounded-sm transition-colors hover:underline",
                          tone === "maroon"
                            ? "text-cream-200/80 hover:text-cream-50"
                            : "hover:text-maroon-800",
                        )}
                      >
                        {crumb.label}
                      </Link>
                    )}
                    {!isLast ? <BreadcrumbSeparator /> : null}
                  </BreadcrumbItem>
                );
              })}
            </BreadcrumbList>
          </Breadcrumb>
        ) : null}
        {eyebrow ? (
          <p
            className={cn(
              "font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.3em]",
              tone === "maroon" ? "text-peach-300" : "text-maroon-600",
            )}
          >
            {eyebrow}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <h1
          className={cn(
            "mt-2 max-w-3xl font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-balance sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl",
            tone === "maroon" ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-800",
          )}
        >
          {title}
        </h1>
        {description ? (
          <p
            className={cn(
              "mt-4 max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 sm:text-base",
              tone === "maroon" ? "text-cream-200/90" : "text-muted-foreground",
            )}
          >
            {description}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {children ? <div className="mt-7">{children}</div> : null}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

        ))}
      </div>

  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\common\ScrollReveal.tsx
========================================
import type { ElementType, ReactNode } from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { fadeIn, fadeUp, staggerParent } from "@/lib/animations/variants";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface ScrollRevealProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  delay?: number;
  stagger?: boolean;
  as?: ElementType;
  amount?: number;
}

/** Reveals content once as it scrolls into view. Honors prefers-reduced-motion. */
export function ScrollReveal({
  children,
  className,
  delay = 0,
  stagger = false,
  as = "div",
  amount = 0.2,
}: ScrollRevealProps) {
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const MotionTag = motion[as as keyof typeof motion] as typeof motion.div;

  if (reducedMotion) {
    const Tag = as as ElementType;
    return <Tag className={className}>{children}</Tag>;
  }

  return (
    <MotionTag
      className={className}
      variants={stagger ? staggerParent : fadeIn}
      initial="hidden"
      whileInView="visible"
      viewport={{ once: true, amount }}
      transition={{ delay }}
    >
      {children}
    </MotionTag>
  );
}

export interface RevealItemProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  as?: ElementType;
}

/** Child item for a staggered ScrollReveal. */
export function RevealItem({ children, className, as = "div" }: RevealItemProps) {
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const MotionTag = motion[as as keyof typeof motion] as typeof motion.div;

  if (reducedMotion) {
    const Tag = as as ElementType;
    return <Tag className={className}>{children}</Tag>;
  }

  return (
    <MotionTag className={cn(className)} variants={fadeUp}>
      {children}
    </MotionTag>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface SectionHeadingProps {
  eyebrow?: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  description?: ReactNode;
  align?: "center" | "start";
  tone?: "light" | "dark";
  className?: string;
  id?: string;
  as?: "h1" | "h2" | "h3";
}

export function SectionHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  align = "center",
  tone = "light",
  className,
  id,
  as: Heading = "h2",
}: SectionHeadingProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-col gap-3",
        align === "center" ? "items-center text-center" : "items-start text-start",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {eyebrow ? (
        <p
          className={cn(
            "font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.3em]",
            tone === "dark" ? "text-peach-300" : "text-maroon-600",
          )}
        >
          {eyebrow}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <Heading
        id={id}
        className={cn(
          "font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight leading-tight text-balance sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl",
          tone === "dark" ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-700",
        )}
      >
        {title}
      </Heading>
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "h-1 w-16 rounded-full bg-linear-to-r",
          tone === "dark" ? "from-peach-300 to-cream-200" : "from-maroon-700 to-peach-400",
        )}
      />
      {description ? (
        <p
          className={cn(
            "max-w-[62ch] font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 sm:text-base",
            tone === "dark" ? "text-cream-200/90" : "text-muted-foreground",
          )}
        >
          {description}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { MapPin, Quote, Share2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { SuccessStory } from "@/schemas/content.schema";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";

interface StoryCardProps {
  story: SuccessStory;
  className?: string;
}

export function StoryCard({ story, className }: StoryCardProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  const share = async () => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/success-stories#${story.id}`;
    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share({ title: story.coupleLabel, url });
        return;
      }
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      toast.success("Link copied.");
    } catch {
      toast.info("Press and hold the address bar to copy this link.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <article
        className={cn(
          "flex h-full flex-col gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10",
          className,
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <Quote className="size-6 text-peach-400" aria-hidden="true" />
          {story.isSample ? (
            <Badge variant="outline" className="font-normal">
              Sample story
            </Badge>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <blockquote className="font-heading text-lg italic leading-snug text-maroon-900">
          &ldquo;{story.quote}&rdquo;
        </blockquote>

        <div className="mt-auto flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
            <MapPin className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            {story.city}
          </span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>{story.year}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>{story.serviceUsed}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3">
          <p className="font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-800">{story.coupleLabel}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
              Read story
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon-sm"
              onClick={() => void share()}
              aria-label={`Share story from ${story.city}`}
            >
              <Share2 aria-hidden="true" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </article>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>{story.coupleLabel}</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              {story.city} · {story.year} · {story.serviceUsed}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <p className="font-heading text-lg italic leading-snug text-maroon-900">
            &ldquo;{story.quote}&rdquo;
          </p>
          <p className="font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 text-muted-foreground">{story.story}</p>
          {story.isSample ? (
            <p className="rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 p-3 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/70">
              This is a sample story used to show the layout. Real stories will be published only
              with the written consent of both families.
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button asChild>
              <a href="/register">Start your own search</a>
            </Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => void share()}>
              <Share2 aria-hidden="true" /> Share
            </Button>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

import { Play } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { VideoItem } from "@/schemas/content.schema";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogTitle, DialogDescription } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";

interface VideoCardProps {
  video: VideoItem;
  className?: string;
  /** Compact card used in the home page section. */
  priority?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Thumbnail card that opens a dialog with a privacy-enhanced YouTube embed.
 * The iframe is only mounted after the visitor clicks, so nothing loads up front.
 */
export function VideoCard({ video, className, priority = false }: VideoCardProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [thumbFailed, setThumbFailed] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          setOpen(true);
          trackEvent("video_open", { videoId: video.id });
        }}
        className="relative block aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden bg-maroon-900 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none"
        aria-label={`Play video: ${video.title}`}
      >
        {thumbFailed ? (
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-br from-maroon-800 via-maroon-900 to-maroon-950"
          >
            <svg
              viewBox="0 0 480 270"
              preserveAspectRatio="none"
              className="size-full text-khaki-400/25"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <g fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                <path d="M60 270V150a60 75 0 0 1 120 0v120" />
                <path d="M180 270V150a60 75 0 0 1 120 0v120" />
                <path d="M300 270V150a60 75 0 0 1 120 0v120" />
              </g>
            </svg>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <img
            src={`https://i.ytimg.com/vi/${video.youtubeId}/hqdefault.jpg`}
            alt=""
            width={480}
            height={360}
            loading={priority ? "eager" : "lazy"}
            decoding="async"
            onError={() => setThumbFailed(true)}
            className="size-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
          />
        )}
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-t from-maroon-950/70 via-maroon-950/10 to-transparent"
        />
        <span className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center">
          <span className="grid size-14 place-items-center rounded-full bg-cream-50/95 text-maroon-800 shadow-lg transition-transform group-hover:scale-110">
            <Play className="ms-0.5 size-5 fill-current" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
        </span>
        <span className="absolute bottom-2 end-2 rounded-full bg-maroon-950/80 px-2 py-0.5 font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-cream-100">
          {video.durationLabel}
        </span>
      </button>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1.5 p-5">
        <p className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-maroon-600">
          {video.category === "guidance"
            ? "Guidance"
            : video.category === "bureau"
              ? "Our bureau"
              : "For families"}
        </p>
        <h3 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold leading-snug text-maroon-900">
          {video.title}
        </h3>
        <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{video.description}</p>
      </div>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="max-w-3xl p-4 sm:p-6">
          <DialogTitle className="pe-8 font-heading text-xl">{video.title}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="sr-only">
            Video player. Press Escape to close.
          </DialogDescription>
          {open ? (
            <div className="aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-black">
              <iframe
                src={`https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/${video.youtubeId}?autoplay=1&rel=0`}
                title={video.title}
                allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                allowFullScreen
                loading="lazy"
                className="size-full"
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}
          <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{video.description}</p>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}
