========================================
FILE: src\components\forms\ComboboxField.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Check, ChevronDown, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { controlClass } from "@/components/forms/FormField";

export interface ComboboxOption {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}

export interface ComboboxFieldProps {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  options: ComboboxOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  id?: string;
  className?: string;
  /** Allows a value that is not in the option list. */
  allowCustom?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Accessible searchable select (WAI-ARIA combobox pattern) with full keyboard
 * support: Arrow keys, Home/End, Enter, Escape and Tab.
 */
export function ComboboxField({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  placeholder = "Select an option",
  error,
  hint,
  required,
  disabled,
  id,
  className,
  allowCustom = false,
}: ComboboxFieldProps) {
  const generatedId = useId();
  const inputId = id ?? `combobox-${generatedId}`;
  const listId = `${inputId}-listbox`;
  const errorId = error ? `${inputId}-error` : undefined;
  const hintId = hint ? `${inputId}-hint` : undefined;

  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);

  const selected = options.find((option) => option.value === value);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!needle) return options;
    return options.filter((option) => option.label.toLowerCase().includes(needle));
  }, [options, query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onPointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!containerRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
        setQuery("");
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", onPointerDown);
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const active = listRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-index="${activeIndex}"]`);
    active?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
  }, [activeIndex, open]);

  const commit = (nextValue: string) => {
    onChange(nextValue);
    setOpen(false);
    setQuery("");
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;

    if (event.key === "ArrowDown") {
      event.preventDefault();
      if (!open) {
        setOpen(true);
        return;
      }
      setActiveIndex((index) => Math.min(index + 1, filtered.length - 1));
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "ArrowUp") {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((index) => Math.max(index - 1, 0));
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "Home" && open) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex(0);
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "End" && open) {
      event.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex(Math.max(filtered.length - 1, 0));
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "Enter") {
      if (open && filtered[activeIndex]) {
        event.preventDefault();
        commit(filtered[activeIndex].value);
      } else if (allowCustom && query.trim()) {
        event.preventDefault();
        commit(query.trim());
      }
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      setOpen(false);
      setQuery("");
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === "Tab") {
      setOpen(false);
      setQuery("");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-1.5", className)} ref={containerRef}>
      <label htmlFor={inputId} className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900">
        {label}
        {required ? (
          <span className="ms-1 text-maroon-600" aria-hidden="true">
            *
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </label>

      <div className="relative">
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          id={inputId}
          role="combobox"
          type="text"
          autoComplete="off"
          aria-expanded={open}
          aria-controls={listId}
          aria-autocomplete="list"
          aria-describedby={[hintId, errorId].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || undefined}
          aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
          disabled={disabled}
          className={cn(controlClass, "pe-10")}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          value={open ? query : (selected?.label ?? value)}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setQuery(event.target.value);
            setActiveIndex(0);
            if (!open) setOpen(true);
          }}
          onClick={() => {
            if (!disabled) {
              setOpen(true);
              setQuery("");
            }
          }}
          onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          tabIndex={-1}
          aria-hidden="true"
          onClick={() => {
            if (disabled) return;
            setOpen((value) => !value);
            inputRef.current?.focus();
          }}
          className="absolute end-2 top-1/2 grid size-7 -translate-y-1/2 place-items-center rounded-full text-maroon-800/60 hover:bg-cream-100"
        >
          <ChevronDown className="size-4" />
        </button>

        {open ? (
          <ul
            ref={listRef}
            id={listId}
            role="listbox"
            aria-label={label}
            className="absolute z-40 mt-1.5 max-h-64 w-full overflow-y-auto scroll-area rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-1.5 shadow-xl shadow-maroon-900/10"
          >
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <li
                className="px-3 py-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground"
                role="option"
                aria-selected="false"
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Search className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  No matches
                  {allowCustom && query.trim() ? ` — press Enter to use “${query.trim()}”` : ""}
                </span>
              </li>
            ) : (
              filtered.map((option, index) => (
                <li key={option.value}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    data-index={index}
                    role="option"
                    aria-selected={option.value === value}
                    onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(index)}
                    onClick={() => commit(option.value)}
                    className={cn(
                      "flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-start font-sans text-sm",
                      index === activeIndex ? "bg-cream-100 text-maroon-900" : "text-foreground",
                    )}
                  >
                    <span>{option.label}</span>
                    {option.value === value ? (
                      <Check className="size-4 text-maroon-700" aria-hidden="true" />
                    ) : null}
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))
            )}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {hint && !error ? (
        <p id={hintId} className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
          {hint}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {error ? (
        <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-xs font-medium leading-5 text-red-700">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\forms\ContactForm.tsx
========================================
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Loader2, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  CONTACT_TOPICS,
  contactInquirySchema,
  type ContactInquiry,
} from "@/schemas/inquiry.schema";
import { submitContactInquiry } from "@/api/inquiries.api";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { cityOptions } from "@/lib/filterOptions";
import { ComboboxField } from "@/components/forms/ComboboxField";
import { FormField, NativeSelect, controlClass } from "@/components/forms/FormField";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const topicOptions = CONTACT_TOPICS.map((topic) => ({ label: topic, value: topic }));

interface ContactFormProps {
  initialTopic?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export function ContactForm({ initialTopic, className }: ContactFormProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  const defaultTopic = (CONTACT_TOPICS as readonly string[]).includes(initialTopic ?? "")
    ? (initialTopic as (typeof CONTACT_TOPICS)[number])
    : CONTACT_TOPICS[0];

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    watch,
    setValue,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<ContactInquiry>({
    resolver: zodResolver(contactInquirySchema),
    mode: "onTouched",
    defaultValues: {
      name: "",
      phone: "",
      city: "",
      topic: defaultTopic,
      message: "",
      company: "",
    },
  });

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (values: ContactInquiry) => submitContactInquiry(values),
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success(t("forms.contactSuccess"));
      trackEvent("contact_submit");
      reset({ ...valuesForReset(), topic: defaultTopic });
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error(t("forms.contactError"));
    },
  });

  function valuesForReset(): Partial<ContactInquiry> {
    return {
      name: "",
      phone: "",
      city: "",
      message: "",
      company: "",
    };
  }

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit((values) => mutation.mutate(values));

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={onSubmit}
      noValidate
      className={cn(
        "rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 sm:p-7",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <FormField
          label={t("forms.name")}
          htmlFor="contact-name"
          required
          error={errors.name?.message}
        >
          <input
            id="contact-name"
            type="text"
            autoComplete="name"
            maxLength={80}
            className={controlClass}
            placeholder="e.g. Ayesha Khan"
            aria-invalid={errors.name ? true : undefined}
            {...register("name")}
          />
        </FormField>

        <FormField
          label={t("forms.phone")}
          htmlFor="contact-phone"
          required
          error={errors.phone?.message}
          hint={t("forms.phoneHint")}
        >
          <input
            id="contact-phone"
            type="tel"
            inputMode="tel"
            autoComplete="tel"
            className={controlClass}
            placeholder="0300 1234567"
            aria-invalid={errors.phone ? true : undefined}
            {...register("phone")}
          />
        </FormField>

        <ComboboxField
          label={t("forms.city")}
          required
          value={watch("city")}
          onChange={(value) => setValue("city", value, { shouldValidate: true })}
          options={cityOptions}
          placeholder="Search or type your city"
          allowCustom
          error={errors.city?.message}
        />

        <FormField
          label={t("forms.topic")}
          htmlFor="contact-topic"
          required
          error={errors.topic?.message}
        >
          <NativeSelect
            id="contact-topic"
            options={topicOptions}
            aria-invalid={errors.topic ? true : undefined}
            {...register("topic")}
          />
        </FormField>

        <FormField
          label={t("forms.message")}
          htmlFor="contact-message"
          required
          error={errors.message?.message}
          className="sm:col-span-2"
          hint="Please do not include identity documents or bank details in this message."
        >
          <textarea
            id="contact-message"
            rows={5}
            maxLength={1200}
            className={cn(controlClass, "h-auto py-3")}
            placeholder="Tell us briefly what you need help with."
            aria-invalid={errors.message ? true : undefined}
            {...register("message")}
          />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      {/* Honeypot — must stay empty */}
      <div className="absolute h-0 w-0 overflow-hidden opacity-0" aria-hidden="true">
        <label htmlFor="contact-company">Company</label>
        <input id="contact-company" tabIndex={-1} autoComplete="off" {...register("company")} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <p className="font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
          We reply within one working day. For urgent matters, WhatsApp is fastest.
        </p>
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={mutation.isPending} className="sm:w-auto">
          {mutation.isPending ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" /> {t("forms.sending")}
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              {t("forms.send")}
              <Send aria-hidden="true" />
            </>
          )}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\forms\FormField.tsx
========================================
import { cloneElement, isValidElement, type ReactNode, type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

/** Shared control styling so every input in the app looks identical. */
export const controlClass =
  "h-11 w-full min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-input bg-white px-4 font-sans text-base text-foreground shadow-xs transition-colors placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700/20 focus-visible:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-cream-100 disabled:opacity-70 aria-invalid:border-red-600 aria-invalid:ring-2 aria-invalid:ring-red-600/15";

export interface FormFieldProps {
  label: string;
  htmlFor: string;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  optional?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  /** Adds the error styling hook to custom controls. */
  invalid?: boolean;
}

export function FormField({
  label,
  htmlFor,
  error,
  hint,
  required,
  optional,
  className,
  children,
}: FormFieldProps) {
  const hintId = hint ? `${htmlFor}-hint` : undefined;
  const errorId = error ? `${htmlFor}-error` : undefined;
  const describedBy = [hintId, errorId].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || undefined;

  // Wires the control to its hint/error text so screen readers announce both.
  const control = isValidElement<Record<string, unknown>>(children)
    ? cloneElement(children, {
        "aria-describedby": describedBy,
        "aria-invalid": error ? true : undefined,
      })
    : children;

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-1.5", className)}>
      <label htmlFor={htmlFor} className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900">
        {label}
        {required ? (
          <span className="ms-1 text-maroon-600" aria-hidden="true">
            *
          </span>
        ) : null}
        {optional ? (
          <span className="ms-1.5 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">(optional)</span>
        ) : null}
      </label>
      {control}
      {hint && !error ? (
        <p id={hintId} className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
          {hint}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {error ? (
        <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-xs font-medium leading-5 text-red-700">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface NativeSelectProps extends SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
  options: { label: string; value: string }[];
  placeholder?: string;
}

/** Native select: fastest, most accessible option on mobile. */
export function NativeSelect({ options, placeholder, className, ...props }: NativeSelectProps) {
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <select className={cn(controlClass, "appearance-none pe-10", className)} {...props}>
        {placeholder ? <option value="">{placeholder}</option> : null}
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <ChevronDown
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute end-3.5 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-maroon-800/60"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\forms\NewsletterForm.tsx

import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Send } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  newsletterSubscriptionSchema,
  type NewsletterSubscription,
} from "@/schemas/inquiry.schema";
import { subscribeToNewsletter } from "@/api/newsletter.api";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

interface NewsletterFormProps {
  tone?: "light" | "dark";
  className?: string;
}

export function NewsletterForm({ tone = "light", className }: NewsletterFormProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<NewsletterSubscription>({
    resolver: zodResolver(newsletterSubscriptionSchema),
    defaultValues: { email: "", company: "" },
  });

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (values: NewsletterSubscription) => subscribeToNewsletter(values),
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success(t("forms.newsletterSuccess"));
      trackEvent("newsletter_submit");
      reset();
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error("We could not subscribe you right now. Please try again in a moment.");
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit((values) => mutation.mutate(values));

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className={cn("w-full max-w-md", className)} noValidate>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <label htmlFor="newsletter-email" className="sr-only">
            {t("forms.email")}
          </label>
          <input
            id="newsletter-email"
            type="email"
            inputMode="email"
            autoComplete="email"
            placeholder="you@example.com"
            aria-invalid={errors.email ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={errors.email ? "newsletter-email-error" : undefined}
            className={cn(
              "h-11 w-full rounded-full px-4 font-sans text-base outline-none transition-colors",
              tone === "dark"
                ? "border border-cream-200/25 bg-white/5 text-cream-50 placeholder:text-cream-200/40 focus-visible:border-peach-300 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-peach-300/30"
                : "border border-input bg-white text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700/20",
              errors.email && "border-red-500",
            )}
            {...register("email")}
          />
          {/* Honeypot — hidden from humans and assistive tech */}
          <div className="absolute h-0 w-0 overflow-hidden opacity-0" aria-hidden="true">
            <label htmlFor="newsletter-company">Company</label>
            <input
              id="newsletter-company"
              tabIndex={-1}
              autoComplete="off"
              {...register("company")}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant={tone === "dark" ? "peach" : "default"}
          disabled={mutation.isPending}
          className="shrink-0"
        >
          {mutation.isPending ? t("common.submitting") : t("forms.newsletterCta")}
          <Send aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
        </Button>
      </div>
      {errors.email ? (
        <p id="newsletter-email-error" role="alert" className="mt-2 font-sans text-xs text-red-400">
          {errors.email.message}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <p
          className={cn(
            "mt-2 font-sans text-xs",
            tone === "dark" ? "text-cream-200/50" : "text-muted-foreground",
          )}
        >
          No forwarded messages. Unsubscribe any time.
        </p>
      )}
    </form>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\forms\RegisterForm.tsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Check, Loader2, Lock } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  registerSchema,
  isoDateYearsAgo,
  type RegistrationFormValues,
} from "@/schemas/register.schema";
import {
  casteOptions,
  cityOptions,
  countryOptions,
  educationOptions,
  familyTypeOptions,
  genderOptions,
  heightOptions,
  incomeOptions,
  languageOptions,
  maritalStatusOptions,
  occupationOptions,
  religionOptions,
  sectOptions,
} from "@/lib/filterOptions";
import { submitRegistration } from "@/api/registrations.api";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { formatHeight } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { registerSteps } from "@/components/forms/registerSteps";
import { buildRegistrationMessage } from "@/components/forms/registerMessage";
import { RegisterSuccess } from "@/components/forms/RegisterSuccess";
import { ComboboxField } from "@/components/forms/ComboboxField";
import { FormField, NativeSelect, controlClass } from "@/components/forms/FormField";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";

const STORAGE_KEY = "ahd-e-rishta.last-registration";

const defaultValues: RegistrationFormValues = {
  registeredBy: "self",
  gender: "female",
  maritalStatus: "never-married",
  dateOfBirth: isoDateYearsAgo(25),
  heightCm: 165,
  religion: "Islam",
  sect: "Sunni",
  caste: "",
  familyType: "nuclear",
  languages: ["Urdu"],
  siblingsTotal: 0,
  siblingsMarried: 0,
  hasChildren: false,
  education: "",
  occupation: "",
  income: "",
  city: "",
  country: "Pakistan",
  partnerAgeFrom: 24,
  partnerAgeTo: 34,
  partnerHeightFrom: 160,
  partnerHeightTo: 190,
  partnerMaritalStatus: "any",
  partnerEducation: "",
  partnerCity: "",
  partnerNotes: "",
  contactName: "",
  relation: "",
  phone: "",
  email: "",
  bestTimeToCall: "anytime",
  // Literal `true` fields cannot be defaulted; they start unchecked and are validated on submit.
  consentBureau: undefined as unknown as true,
  confirmTruth: undefined as unknown as true,
  agreeTerms: undefined as unknown as true,
  company: "",
};

const registeredByOptions = [
  { label: "I am registering for myself", value: "self" },
  { label: "Parent", value: "parent" },
  { label: "Sibling", value: "sibling" },
  { label: "Other relative", value: "relative" },
];

const bestTimeOptions = [
  { label: "Morning (10 AM – 1 PM)", value: "morning" },
  { label: "Afternoon (1 PM – 4 PM)", value: "afternoon" },
  { label: "Evening (4 PM – 7 PM)", value: "evening" },
  { label: "Any time", value: "anytime" },
];

const partnerMaritalOptions = [{ label: "No preference", value: "any" }, ...maritalStatusOptions];

interface RegisterFormProps {
  onDone?: () => void;
}

export function RegisterForm({ onDone }: RegisterFormProps) {
  const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{
    reference: string;
    values: RegistrationFormValues;
  } | null>(null);

  const form = useForm<RegistrationFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(registerSchema),
    mode: "onTouched",
    defaultValues,
  });

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    trigger,
    watch,
    setValue,
    setFocus,
    formState: { errors },
  } = form;

  const step = registerSteps[stepIndex];
  const values = watch();
  const isLastStep = stepIndex === registerSteps.length - 1;
  const maritalStatus = watch("maritalStatus");
  const selectedLanguages = watch("languages") ?? [];

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (payload: RegistrationFormValues) => submitRegistration(payload),
    onSuccess: (response, payload) => {
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(
          STORAGE_KEY,
          JSON.stringify({ reference: response.reference, submittedAt: response.receivedAt }),
        );
      } catch {
        /* storage is optional */
      }
      trackEvent("register_submit", { reference: response.reference, gender: payload.gender });
      toast.success("Registration received. The office will call you back.");
      setResult({ reference: response.reference, values: payload });
      // Hand off to WhatsApp with the same summary — the user can also press the button on the
      // success screen if the browser blocks this automatic tab.
      window.open(buildRegistrationMessage(payload), "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error(
        "We could not submit the form. Please try again, or message the office on WhatsApp.",
      );
    },
  });

  const goNext = async () => {
    const valid = await trigger(step.fields, { shouldFocus: true });
    if (!valid) {
      const firstError = step.fields.find((field) => errors[field]);
      if (firstError) setFocus(firstError);
      toast.error("Please check the highlighted fields before continuing.");
      return;
    }
    trackEvent("register_step", { step: step.id, index: stepIndex + 1 });
    setStepIndex((index) => Math.min(index + 1, registerSteps.length - 1));
  };

  const goBack = () => setStepIndex((index) => Math.max(index - 1, 0));

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit((formValues) => mutation.mutate(formValues));

  if (result) {
    return (
      <RegisterSuccess
        reference={result.reference}
        values={result.values}
        whatsappUrl={buildRegistrationMessage(result.values)}
        onDone={onDone}
      />
    );
  }

  const toggleLanguage = (language: string) => {
    const next = selectedLanguages.includes(language)
      ? selectedLanguages.filter((item) => item !== language)
      : [...selectedLanguages, language];
    setValue("languages", next, { shouldValidate: true });
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 sm:p-7">
      {/* Stepper */}
      <ol className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1">
        {registerSteps.map((item, index) => {
          const state = index === stepIndex ? "current" : index < stepIndex ? "done" : "upcoming";
          return (
            <li key={item.id} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (index < stepIndex) setStepIndex(index);
                }}
                disabled={index > stepIndex}
                aria-current={state === "current" ? "step" : undefined}
                className={cn(
                  "flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-2 py-1.5 text-start transition-colors",
                  index < stepIndex ? "hover:bg-cream-100" : "cursor-default",
                )}
              >
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "grid size-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full font-sans text-xs font-bold",
                    state === "current" && "bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50",
                    state === "done" && "bg-green-800 text-white",
                    state === "upcoming" &&
                      "border border-khaki-300 bg-cream-100 text-maroon-800/60",
                  )}
                >
                  {state === "done" ? <Check className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> : index + 1}
                </span>
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "hidden truncate font-sans text-xs font-semibold sm:block",
                    state === "upcoming" ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-maroon-900",
                  )}
                >
                  {item.title}
                </span>
              </button>
              {index < registerSteps.length - 1 ? (
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-px w-3 shrink-0 bg-khaki-300 sm:w-4" />
              ) : null}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>

      <div className="mt-5 border-t border-border pt-5">
        <p className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-maroon-600">
          Step {stepIndex + 1} of {registerSteps.length}
        </p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800">{step.title}</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">{step.description}</p>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="mt-5 space-y-4" noValidate>
        {/* Honeypot */}
        <div className="absolute h-0 w-0 overflow-hidden opacity-0" aria-hidden="true">
          <label htmlFor="register-company">Company</label>
          <input id="register-company" tabIndex={-1} autoComplete="off" {...register("company")} />
        </div>

        {step.id === "proposal" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <FormField
              label="Who is registering?"
              htmlFor="registeredBy"
              required
              error={errors.registeredBy?.message}
            >
              <NativeSelect
                id="registeredBy"
                options={registeredByOptions}
                {...register("registeredBy")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="This proposal is for a"
              htmlFor="gender"
              required
              error={errors.gender?.message}
            >
              <NativeSelect id="gender" options={genderOptions} {...register("gender")} />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Marital status"
              htmlFor="maritalStatus"
              required
              error={errors.maritalStatus?.message}
            >
              <NativeSelect
                id="maritalStatus"
                options={maritalStatusOptions}
                {...register("maritalStatus")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Date of birth"
              htmlFor="dateOfBirth"
              required
              error={errors.dateOfBirth?.message}
              hint="Age must be between 18 and 70 years."
            >
              <input
                id="dateOfBirth"
                type="date"
                min={isoDateYearsAgo(70)}
                max={isoDateYearsAgo(18)}
                className={controlClass}
                aria-invalid={errors.dateOfBirth ? true : undefined}
                {...register("dateOfBirth")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Height"
              htmlFor="heightCm"
              required
              error={errors.heightCm?.message}
              hint={"Between 4'0\" and 7'0\"."}
            >
              <NativeSelect id="heightCm" options={heightOptions} {...register("heightCm")} />
            </FormField>

            <div className="flex items-end">
              <p className="rounded-2xl bg-cream-100/70 p-3 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/70">
                We never publish a full name, phone number or address. Public listings show a first
                name with a last initial and a profile ID only.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {step.id === "family" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">

              label="Monthly income"
              htmlFor="income"
              required

            >
              <NativeSelect

              />

            <ComboboxField
              label="City"
              required
              value={values.city}
              onChange={(value) => setValue("city", value, { shouldValidate: true })}
              options={cityOptions}
              placeholder="Search or type your city"
              allowCustom
              error={errors.city?.message}
            />

            <ComboboxField
              label="Country"
              required
              value={values.country}
              onChange={(value) => setValue("country", value, { shouldValidate: true })}
              options={countryOptions}
              placeholder="Select a country"
              allowCustom
              error={errors.country?.message}
            />
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {step.id === "preferences" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <FormField
              label="Preferred age from"
              htmlFor="partnerAgeFrom"
              required
              error={errors.partnerAgeFrom?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="partnerAgeFrom"
                type="number"
                min={18}
                max={70}
                inputMode="numeric"
                className={controlClass}
                {...register("partnerAgeFrom")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Preferred age to"
              htmlFor="partnerAgeTo"
              required
              error={errors.partnerAgeTo?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="partnerAgeTo"
                type="number"
                min={18}
                max={70}
                inputMode="numeric"
                className={controlClass}
                {...register("partnerAgeTo")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Preferred height from"
              htmlFor="partnerHeightFrom"
              error={errors.partnerHeightFrom?.message}
            >
              <NativeSelect
                id="partnerHeightFrom"
                options={heightOptions}
                {...register("partnerHeightFrom")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Preferred height to"
              htmlFor="partnerHeightTo"
              error={errors.partnerHeightTo?.message}
            >
              <NativeSelect
                id="partnerHeightTo"
                options={heightOptions}
                {...register("partnerHeightTo")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField label="Marital status preference" htmlFor="partnerMaritalStatus">
              <NativeSelect
                id="partnerMaritalStatus"
                options={partnerMaritalOptions}
                {...register("partnerMaritalStatus")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Preferred education"
              htmlFor="partnerEducation"
              required
              error={errors.partnerEducation?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="partnerEducation"
                type="text"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="e.g. Graduate, practising professional"
                {...register("partnerEducation")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Preferred city or country"
              htmlFor="partnerCity"
              required
              error={errors.partnerCity?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="partnerCity"
                type="text"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="e.g. Lahore or United Kingdom"
                {...register("partnerCity")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Anything else the consultant should know"
              htmlFor="partnerNotes"
              optional
              error={errors.partnerNotes?.message}
              className="sm:col-span-2"
            >
              <textarea
                id="partnerNotes"
                rows={4}
                maxLength={600}
                className={cn(controlClass, "h-auto py-3")}
                placeholder="Family expectations, dietary preferences, living arrangements…"
                {...register("partnerNotes")}
              />
            </FormField>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {step.id === "contact" ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <FormField
              label="Contact person's name"
              htmlFor="contactName"
              required
              error={errors.contactName?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="contactName"
                type="text"
                autoComplete="name"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="e.g. Muhammad Aslam"
                {...register("contactName")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Relation to the proposal"
              htmlFor="relation"
              required
              error={errors.relation?.message}
            >
              <input
                id="relation"
                type="text"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="e.g. Father, brother, self"
                {...register("relation")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField
              label="Mobile number"
              htmlFor="phone"
              required
              error={errors.phone?.message}
              hint="Pakistani mobile number, e.g. 0300 1234567 or +92 300 1234567."
            >
              <input
                id="phone"
                type="tel"
                inputMode="tel"
                autoComplete="tel"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="0300 1234567"
                aria-invalid={errors.phone ? true : undefined}
                {...register("phone")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField label="Email" htmlFor="email" optional error={errors.email?.message}>
              <input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                inputMode="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                className={controlClass}
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                {...register("email")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField label="Best time to call" htmlFor="bestTimeToCall" required>
              <NativeSelect
                id="bestTimeToCall"
                options={bestTimeOptions}
                {...register("bestTimeToCall")}
              />
            </FormField>

            <div className="sm:col-span-2 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100/70 p-3">
              <Lock className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-maroon-700" aria-hidden="true" />
              <p className="font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/75">
                Your number is visible only to the office. It is never published and never shared
                without your permission.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {step.id === "consent" ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-cream-100/60 p-4">
              <h3 className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-maroon-900">Summary</h3>
              <dl className="mt-2 grid gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 font-sans text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
                {[
                  ["Proposal for", values.gender === "male" ? "Groom" : "Bride"],
                  ["Marital status", values.maritalStatus],
                  ["Date of birth", values.dateOfBirth],
                  ["Height", formatHeight(Number(values.heightCm))],
                  ["Religion", `${values.religion} · ${values.sect}`],
                  ["Caste", values.caste || "—"],
                  ["Education", values.education || "—"],
                  ["Occupation", values.occupation || "—"],
                  ["Location", `${values.city || "—"}, ${values.country || "—"}`],
                  ["Languages", selectedLanguages.join(", ") || "—"],
                  ["Contact person", values.contactName || "—"],
                  ["Phone", values.phone || "—"],
                ].map(([label, value]) => (
                  <div
                    key={label}
                    className="flex justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border/60 py-1 last:border-0"
                  >
                    <dt className="text-muted-foreground">{label}</dt>
                    <dd className="text-end font-medium text-maroon-900">{value}</dd>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </dl>
              <p className="mt-2 font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                You can ask the office to correct any detail after submission.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <Checkbox
                  label="I allow Ahd-e-Rishta to review these details and share them with suitable families after speaking to us."
                  {...register("consentBureau")}
                />
                {errors.consentBureau ? (
                  <p role="alert" className="mt-1 font-sans text-xs text-red-700">
                    {errors.consentBureau.message}
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </div>

              <div>
                <Checkbox
                  label="I confirm the information is accurate and that the candidate has agreed to this search."
                  {...register("confirmTruth")}
                />
                {errors.confirmTruth ? (
                  <p role="alert" className="mt-1 font-sans text-xs text-red-700">
                    {errors.confirmTruth.message}
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </div>

              <div>
                <Checkbox
                  label="I accept the terms of service and the privacy policy."
                  {...register("agreeTerms")}
                />
                {errors.agreeTerms ? (
                  <p role="alert" className="mt-1 font-sans text-xs text-red-700">
                    {errors.agreeTerms.message}
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Step controls */}
        <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 border-t border-border pt-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="ghost"
            onClick={goBack}
            disabled={stepIndex === 0 || mutation.isPending}
            className="sm:w-auto"
          >
            <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
            Back
          </Button>

          {isLastStep ? (
            <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={mutation.isPending} className="sm:w-auto">
              {mutation.isPending ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="animate-spin" aria-hidden="true" /> Submitting…
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  Submit registration
                  <Check aria-hidden="true" />
                </>
              )}
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button type="button" size="lg" onClick={() => void goNext()} className="sm:w-auto">
              Continue
              <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\forms\registerMessage.ts
========================================
import { formatHeight } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { ageFromDate, type RegistrationFormValues } from "@/schemas/register.schema";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

/** Builds the WhatsApp hand-off message sent after the wizard is submitted. */
export function buildRegistrationMessage(values: RegistrationFormValues): string {
  const age = ageFromDate(values.dateOfBirth);
  return buildWhatsAppUrl({
    message: [
      "New registration from the website",
      `Proposal: ${values.gender === "male" ? "Groom" : "Bride"}`,
      `Marital status: ${values.maritalStatus}`,
      `Age: ${age} years · Height: ${formatHeight(values.heightCm)}`,
      `Religion: ${values.religion} (${values.sect}) · Caste: ${values.caste}`,
      `Education: ${values.education} · Occupation: ${values.occupation}`,
      `Location: ${values.city}, ${values.country}`,
      `Family: ${values.familyType}, siblings ${values.siblingsMarried}/${values.siblingsTotal} married`,
      `Contact person: ${values.contactName} (${values.relation})`,
      `Preferred call time: ${values.bestTimeToCall}`,
    ].join("\n"),
  });
}

interface RegisterSuccessProps {

export function RegisterSuccess({ reference, values, whatsappUrl, onDone }: RegisterSuccessProps) {

        hours, expect a call the next working day.

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight, Building2, Globe2, MapPin } from "lucide-react";
import { cityTiles } from "@/lib/homeContent";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ScrollReveal, RevealItem } from "@/components/common/ScrollReveal";

export function BrowseByCitySection() {
  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="browse-city-heading"
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 lg:py-20"
    >
      <SectionHeading
        id="browse-city-heading"
        eyebrow="Browse"
        title="Proposals by city and category"
        description="Start with the region you know, or look at overseas proposals. Every list is curated by the office, not published automatically."
      />

      <ScrollReveal stagger className="mt-10 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {cityTiles.map((tile) => {
          const isOverseas = tile.label === "Overseas";
          return (
            <RevealItem key={tile.label}>
              <Link
                to={tile.href}
                className={
                  isOverseas
                    ? "group flex h-full items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-maroon-800/15 bg-linear-to-br from-maroon-800 to-maroon-950 p-5 text-cream-50 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/15 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none"
                    : "group flex h-full items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-maroon-200 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none"
                }
              >
                <span
                  className={
                    isOverseas
                      ? "grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-white/10 text-peach-300"
                      : "grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 text-maroon-700"
                  }
                >
                  {isOverseas ? (
                    <Globe2 className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : tile.label === "Karachi" || tile.label === "Lahore" ? (
                    <Building2 className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : (
                    <MapPin className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  )}
                </span>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <span className="block font-heading text-lg font-semibold">{tile.label}</span>
                  <span
                    className={
                      isOverseas
                        ? "block truncate font-sans text-xs text-cream-200/75"
                        : "block truncate font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground"
                    }
                  >
                    {tile.count}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <ArrowRight
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={
                    isOverseas
                      ? "size-4 shrink-0 text-peach-300 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 rtl:rotate-180"
                      : "size-4 shrink-0 text-maroon-600 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 rtl:rotate-180"
                  }
                />
              </Link>
            </RevealItem>
          );
        })}
        <RevealItem>
          <Link
            to="/profiles?category=featured"
            className="group flex h-full items-center gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-khaki-400 bg-cream-100/70 p-5 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none"
          >
            <span className="grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-maroon-700">
              <ArrowRight className="size-5 -rotate-45" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <span className="block font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                All proposals
              </span>
              <span className="block font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Browse the full screened list
              </span>
            </span>
          </Link>
        </RevealItem>
      </ScrollReveal>
    </section>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\home\ConsultantsSection.tsx
========================================
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { ArrowRight, Star } from "lucide-react";
import { useConsultants } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import { ScrollReveal, RevealItem } from "@/components/common/ScrollReveal";

export function ConsultantsSection() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useConsultants();
  const consultants = data?.slice(0, 4) ?? [];

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="consultants-heading"
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 lg:py-20"
    >
      <SectionHeading
        id="consultants-heading"
        eyebrow="The people you will speak to"
        title={t("home.consultantsTitle")}
        description={t("home.consultantsDesc")}
      />

      {isError ? (
        <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} className="mt-10" />
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4" aria-busy="true">
          <span className="sr-only">Loading consultants…</span>
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5">
              <Skeleton className="size-16 rounded-full" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-5 w-2/3" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-1/2" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-10 w-full rounded-full" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ScrollReveal stagger className="mt-10 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {consultants.map((consultant) => (
            <RevealItem key={consultant.id} className="h-full">
              <article className="flex h-full flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-shadow hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10">
                <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-3">
                  <span
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className="grid size-14 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-linear-to-br from-cream-100 to-peach-200 font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900"
                  >
                    {consultant.initials}
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h3 className="truncate font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                      {consultant.name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="truncate font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      {consultant.role}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1">
                    <Star className="size-3 fill-current text-amber-500" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {consultant.rating.toFixed(1)}
                    <span className="sr-only"> out of 5 from {consultant.reviews} families</span>
                  </Badge>
                  <Badge variant="muted">{consultant.experience}</Badge>
                </div>

                <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
                  {consultant.bio}
                </p>

                <p className="font-sans text-xs text-maroon-800/70">
                  <span className="font-semibold">Languages:</span>{" "}
                  {consultant.languages.join(", ")}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-auto w-full pt-2">
                  <Button
                    asChild
                    variant="whatsapp"
                    size="sm"
                    className="w-full"
                    onClick={() =>
                      trackEvent("whatsapp_click", {
                        source: "consultant_card",
                        consultant: consultant.name,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    <a
                      href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                        message: `I would like to speak with consultant ${consultant.name}.`,
                      })}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    >
                      <WhatsAppIcon /> Request a call
                    </a>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </article>
            </RevealItem>
          ))}
        </ScrollReveal>
      )}

      <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
        <Button asChild variant="outline">
          <Link to="/team">
            Meet the full team
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\home\FaqSection.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useFaqs } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionContent,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/accordion";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";

export function FaqSection() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useFaqs();
  const faqs = data?.slice(0, 10) ?? [];

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="faq-heading" className="bg-cream-100">
      <div className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-7xl gap-8 px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-12 lg:py-20">
        <div className="lg:col-span-4">
          <SectionHeading
            align="start"
            id="faq-heading"
            eyebrow="Questions"
            title={t("home.faqTitle")}
            description={t("home.faqDesc")}
          />
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button
              asChild
              variant="whatsapp"
              onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "faq_section" })}
            >
              <a
                href={buildWhatsAppUrl({ message: "I have a question about the rishta process." })}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
              >
                <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask on WhatsApp
              </a>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild variant="outline">
              <Link to="/faq">
                All questions
                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-8">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading questions…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                <Skeleton key={index} className="h-14 w-full rounded-2xl" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Accordion
              type="single"
              collapsible
              className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white px-5 sm:px-6"
            >
              {faqs.map((faq, index) => (
                <AccordionItem key={faq.id} value={faq.id}>
                  <AccordionTrigger>{faq.question}</AccordionTrigger>
                  <AccordionContent>
                    {faq.answer}
                    {index === faqs.length - 1 ? (
                      <span className="mt-3 block">
                        <Link
                          to="/faq"
                          className="font-semibold text-maroon-800 underline underline-offset-4"
                        >
                          See every question
                        </Link>
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                  </AccordionContent>
                </AccordionItem>
              ))}
            </Accordion>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";

type FeaturedTab = "brides" | "grooms";

export function FeaturedProfilesSection() {

      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-4 sm:px-6"

            />
          </TabsContent>

FILE: src\components\home\FindMatchSection.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { ChevronDown, Hash, Search, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  buildProfilesQuery,
  matchFilterSchema,
  type MatchFilterValues,
} from "@/schemas/matchFilter.schema";
import {
  casteOptions,
  cityOptions,
  countryOptions,
  educationOptions,
  genderOptions,
  maritalStatusOptions,
  religionOptions,
  sectOptions,
} from "@/lib/filterOptions";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormField, NativeSelect, controlClass } from "@/components/forms/FormField";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";

export function FindMatchSection() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [showAdvanced, setShowAdvanced] = useState(false);
  const [idQuery, setIdQuery] = useState("");

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
  } = useForm<MatchFilterValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(matchFilterSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      gender: "",
      maritalStatus: "",
      city: "",
      country: "",
      religion: "",
      sect: "",
      education: "",
      caste: "",
      q: "",
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit((values) => {
    trackEvent("search_submit", { source: "home_find_match" });
    const { ageFrom, ageTo, ...rest } = values;
    navigate(
      buildProfilesQuery({
        ...rest,
        ageFrom: ageFrom ? String(ageFrom) : undefined,
        ageTo: ageTo ? String(ageTo) : undefined,
      }),
    );
  });

  const onIdSearch = (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = idQuery.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    trackEvent("search_submit", { source: "home_profile_id" });
    if (/^AER-\d{3,6}$/i.test(trimmed)) {
      navigate(`/profiles/${trimmed.toUpperCase()}`);
      return;
    }
    navigate(buildProfilesQuery({ q: trimmed }));
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="find-match-heading" className="relative z-10 -mt-10 sm:-mt-14">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="rounded-3xl border border-khaki-300/60 bg-white p-5 shadow-xl shadow-maroon-900/10 sm:p-7">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-maroon-600">
                Begin here
              </p>
              <h2
                id="find-match-heading"
                className="mt-1.5 font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800 sm:text-3xl"
              >
                {t("home.findMatchTitle")}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 max-w-xl font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
                {t("home.findMatchDesc")}
              </p>
            </div>
            <a
              href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                message: "I would like help choosing the right filters for my family.",
              })}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "find_match_card" })}
              className="hidden items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-green-800 px-4 py-2 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-green-900 sm:inline-flex"
            >
              <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask a consultant
            </a>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="mt-5" noValidate>
            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
              <FormField label="Looking for" htmlFor="fm-gender" error={errors.gender?.message}>
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-gender"
                  options={genderOptions}
                  placeholder="Bride or groom"
                  {...register("gender")}
                />
              </FormField>

              <FormField
                label="Marital status"
                htmlFor="fm-marital"
                error={errors.maritalStatus?.message}
              >
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-marital"
                  options={maritalStatusOptions}
                  placeholder="Any status"
                  {...register("maritalStatus")}
                />
              </FormField>

              <FormField label="City" htmlFor="fm-city" error={errors.city?.message}>
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-city"
                  options={cityOptions}
                  placeholder="Any city"
                  {...register("city")}
                />
              </FormField>

              <FormField label="Country" htmlFor="fm-country" error={errors.country?.message}>
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-country"
                  options={countryOptions}
                  placeholder="Any country"
                  {...register("country")}
                />
              </FormField>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowAdvanced((value) => !value)}
                aria-expanded={showAdvanced}
                aria-controls="fm-advanced"
                className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-2 py-1.5 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-800 transition-colors hover:bg-cream-100 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none"
              >
                <SlidersHorizontal className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                More filters
                <ChevronDown
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={cn("size-4 transition-transform", showAdvanced && "rotate-180")}
                />
              </button>

              <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting} className="w-full sm:w-auto">
                <Search aria-hidden="true" />
                Show matches
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div
              id="fm-advanced"
              hidden={!showAdvanced}
              className="mt-4 grid gap-3 border-t border-border pt-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"
            >
              <FormField label="Religion" htmlFor="fm-religion">
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-religion"
                  options={religionOptions}
                  placeholder="Any religion"
                  {...register("religion")}
                />
              </FormField>
              <FormField label="Sect" htmlFor="fm-sect">
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-sect"
                  options={sectOptions}
                  placeholder="Any sect"
                  {...register("sect")}
                />
              </FormField>
              <FormField label="Education" htmlFor="fm-education">
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-education"
                  options={educationOptions}
                  placeholder="Any education"
                  {...register("education")}
                />
              </FormField>
              <FormField label="Caste / biradari" htmlFor="fm-caste">
                <NativeSelect
                  id="fm-caste"
                  options={casteOptions}
                  placeholder="Any caste"
                  {...register("caste")}
                />
              </FormField>

              <FormField label="Age from" htmlFor="fm-age-from" error={errors.ageFrom?.message}>
                <input
                  id="fm-age-from"
                  type="number"
                  min={18}
                  max={80}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  placeholder="18"
                  className={controlClass}
                  {...register("ageFrom")}
                />
              </FormField>
              <FormField label="Age to" htmlFor="fm-age-to" error={errors.ageTo?.message}>
                <input
                  id="fm-age-to"
                  type="number"
                  min={18}
                  max={80}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  placeholder="45"
                  className={controlClass}
                  {...register("ageTo")}
                />
              </FormField>
              <FormField label="Name or profile ID" htmlFor="fm-q" className="sm:col-span-2">
                <input
                  id="fm-q"
                  type="search"
                  placeholder="e.g. Ayesha or AER-1042"
                  className={controlClass}
                  {...register("q")}
                />
              </FormField>
            </div>
          </form>

          <form
            onSubmit={onIdSearch}
            className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-cream-100/80 p-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
          >
            <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/80 sm:flex-1">
              <Hash className="size-4 shrink-0 text-maroon-600" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>
                <span className="font-semibold">Have a profile ID?</span> Enter it here — e.g.
                AER-1042 from a card shared by the office.
              </span>
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <label htmlFor="fm-profile-id" className="sr-only">
                Profile ID
              </label>
              <input
                id="fm-profile-id"
                value={idQuery}
                onChange={(event) => setIdQuery(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="AER-1042"
                className={cn(controlClass, "h-10 w-full sm:w-40")}
              />
              <Button type="submit" variant="secondary" size="sm" className="shrink-0">
                Find
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
