========================================
FILE: src\components\home\HeroCarousel.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import Autoplay from "embla-carousel-autoplay";
import { useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { HeroSlide } from "@/schemas/content.schema";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import {
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselItem,
  useCarousel,
} from "@/components/ui/carousel";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";

export interface HeroCarouselProps {
  slides?: HeroSlide[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  isError?: boolean;
  onRetry?: () => void;
}

function HeroNav({ count }: { count: number }) {
  const { api, scrollPrev, scrollNext } = useCarousel();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!api) return;
    const onSelect = () => setSelected(api.selectedScrollSnap());
    onSelect();
    api.on("select", onSelect);
    api.on("reInit", onSelect);
    return () => {
      api.off("select", onSelect);
      api.off("reInit", onSelect);
    };
  }, [api]);

  if (count < 2) return null;

  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-4 z-20">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-1.5"
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Hero slides"
        >
          {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={selected === index}
              aria-label={`Go to slide ${index + 1}`}
              onClick={() => api?.scrollTo(index)}
              className={cn(
                "h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-peach-300 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-maroon-950 focus-visible:outline-none",
                selected === index
                  ? "w-8 bg-peach-300"
                  : "w-4 bg-cream-100/50 hover:bg-cream-100/80",
              )}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="pointer-events-auto flex gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"
            size="icon"
            aria-label="Previous slide"
            onClick={scrollPrev}
            className="border border-cream-100/30 bg-white/10 text-cream-50 hover:bg-white/20"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4 rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"
            size="icon"
            aria-label="Next slide"
            onClick={scrollNext}
            className="border border-cream-100/30 bg-white/10 text-cream-50 hover:bg-white/20"
          >
            <ArrowRight className="size-4 rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function HeroHeading({ slide }: { slide: HeroSlide }) {
  if (!slide.heading) return null;
  const accent = slide.accentWord;
  if (!accent || !slide.heading.toLowerCase().includes(accent.toLowerCase())) {
    return <>{slide.heading}</>;
  }
  const index = slide.heading.toLowerCase().indexOf(accent.toLowerCase());
  const before = slide.heading.slice(0, index);
  const match = slide.heading.slice(index, index + accent.length);
  const after = slide.heading.slice(index + accent.length);

  return (
    <>
      {before}
      <em className="font-heading italic text-peach-300">{match}</em>
      {after}
    </>
  );
}

export function HeroCarousel({
  slides = [],
  isLoading = false,
  isError = false,
  onRetry,
}: HeroCarouselProps) {
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();

  const plugins = useMemo(() => {
    if (reducedMotion || slides.length < 2) return [];
    return [Autoplay({ delay: 4000, stopOnInteraction: false, stopOnMouseEnter: true })];
  }, [reducedMotion, slides.length]);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <section aria-busy="true" className="relative">
        <span className="sr-only">Loading featured message…</span>
        <Skeleton className="h-[62svh] min-h-[460px] w-full rounded-none" />
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (isError) {
    return (
      <section
        aria-label="Welcome to Ahd-e-Rishta"
        className="relative flex min-h-[380px] items-center bg-maroon-950"
      >
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-16 text-center sm:px-6">
          <h1 className="font-heading text-3xl font-semibold text-cream-50 sm:text-5xl">
            Where families meet with <em className="italic text-peach-300">honour</em>
          </h1>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-xl font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-cream-100/80">
            The banner could not load, but the service is running. Browse proposals or message the
            office directly.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            <Button asChild variant="peach">
              <Link to="/profiles">Browse proposals</Link>
            </Button>
            {onRetry ? (
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onRetry}>
                Try again
              </Button>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (slides.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section aria-roledescription="carousel" aria-label="Welcome to Ahd-e-Rishta">
      <Carousel
        plugins={plugins}
        opts={{ loop: slides.length > 1, align: "start" }}
        className="relative w-full"
      >
        <CarouselContent className="ms-0">
          {slides.map((slide, index) => (
            <CarouselItem
              key={slide.id}
              className="basis-full ps-0"
              aria-roledescription="slide"
              aria-label={`${index + 1} of ${slides.length}`}
            >
              <div className="relative flex h-[62svh] min-h-[460px] max-h-[740px] w-full items-end overflow-hidden bg-maroon-950 sm:items-center">
                <img
                  src={slide.image}
                  alt={slide.alt}
                  width={1600}
                  height={900}
                  loading={index === 0 ? "eager" : "lazy"}
                  fetchPriority={index === 0 ? "high" : "auto"}
                  decoding="async"
                  className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover"
                />
                <div
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-t from-maroon-950/92 via-maroon-950/60 to-maroon-950/25 sm:bg-linear-to-r sm:from-maroon-950/94 sm:via-maroon-950/70 sm:to-maroon-950/15"
                />
                <div className="relative mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 pb-20 pt-20 sm:px-6 sm:pb-20">
                  <div className="max-w-2xl">
                    <p className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-cream-100/25 bg-white/5 px-3.5 py-1.5 font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-cream-100">
                      <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5 text-peach-300" aria-hidden="true" />
                      Bureau verified
                    </p>
                    {index === 0 ? (
                      <h1 className="mt-4 font-heading text-4xl font-semibold leading-[1.08] tracking-tight text-cream-50 text-balance sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl">
                        <HeroHeading slide={slide} />
                      </h1>
                    ) : (
                      <h2 className="mt-4 font-heading text-4xl font-semibold leading-[1.08] tracking-tight text-cream-50 text-balance sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl">
                        <HeroHeading slide={slide} />
                      </h2>
                    )}
                    {slide.subHeading ? (
                      <p className="mt-4 max-w-xl font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 text-cream-100/85 sm:text-base">
                        {slide.subHeading}
                      </p>
                    ) : null}
                    <div className="mt-7 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                      {slide.ctaLabel && slide.ctaHref ? (
                        <Button asChild size="lg" variant="peach">
                          <Link to={slide.ctaHref}>
                            {slide.ctaLabel}
                            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
                          </Link>
                        </Button>
                      ) : null}
                      <Button asChild size="lg" variant="whatsapp">
                        <a
                          href={buildWhatsAppUrl()}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "hero" })}
                        >
                          <WhatsAppIcon /> Talk to a consultant
                        </a>
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </CarouselItem>
          ))}
        </CarouselContent>

        <HeroNav count={slides.length} />
      </Carousel>
    </section>
  );
}

export function MembershipPlansSection() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = usePlans();

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="plans-heading"

      />

      {isError ? (
        <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} className="mt-10" />
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
          <span className="sr-only">Loading plans…</span>
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (

              <Skeleton className="h-6 w-24" />

              <Skeleton className="mt-6 h-4 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-5/6" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-6 h-11 w-full rounded-full" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ScrollReveal stagger className="mt-10 grid items-stretch gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {(data ?? []).map((plan) => (
            <RevealItem key={plan.id} className="h-full">
              <div
                className={cn(
                  "relative flex h-full flex-col rounded-3xl border p-6 shadow-sm transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1",
                  plan.highlight
                    ? "border-maroon-800/30 bg-linear-to-b from-maroon-900 to-maroon-950 text-cream-100 shadow-lg shadow-maroon-900/20"
                    : "border-border bg-white shadow-maroon-900/5",
                )}
              >
                {plan.badge ? (
                  <span className="absolute -top-3 start-6 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-peach-400 px-3 py-1 font-sans text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-maroon-950">
                    <Sparkles className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    {plan.badge}
                  </span>
                ) : null}

                <h3
                  className={cn(
                    "font-heading text-2xl font-semibold",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-900",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.name}
                </h3>
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "mt-1 font-sans text-xs",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-200/75" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.audience}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-4">
                  <p
                    className={cn(
                      "font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums",
                      plan.highlight ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-800",
                    )}
                  >
                    {plan.priceLabel}
                  </p>
                  <p
                    className={cn(
                      "mt-0.5 font-sans text-xs",
                      plan.highlight ? "text-cream-200/70" : "text-muted-foreground",
                    )}
                  >
                    {plan.priceNote}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "mt-4 font-sans text-sm leading-7",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-100/85" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.description}
                </p>

                <ul className="mt-5 space-y-2">
                  {plan.features.map((feature) => (
                    <li
                      key={feature.label}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex items-start gap-2 font-sans text-sm leading-6",
                        feature.included
                          ? plan.highlight
                            ? "text-cream-100/90"
                            : "text-maroon-900/85"
                          : plan.highlight
                            ? "text-cream-200/60"
                            : "text-muted-foreground",
                      )}
                    >
                      {feature.included ? (
                        <Check
                          className={cn(
                            "mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0",
                            plan.highlight ? "text-peach-300" : "text-green-800",

                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <Minus className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                      <span>
                        {feature.label}
                        {feature.note ? (
                          <span className="block text-xs opacity-80">{feature.note}</span>
                        ) : null}
                        <span className="sr-only">
                          {feature.included ? " — included" : " — not included"}
                        </span>
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>

                <div className="mt-auto space-y-2 pt-6">
                  <Button
                    asChild
                    variant="whatsapp"
                    className="w-full"
                    onClick={() =>
                      trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "plan_card", plan: plan.name })
                    }
                  >
                    <a href={whatsappForPlan(plan.name)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                      <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask about {plan.name}
                    </a>
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    asChild
                    variant={plan.highlight ? "secondary" : "outline"}
                    className="w-full"
                  >
                    <Link to="/register">Register first</Link>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </RevealItem>
          ))}
        </ScrollReveal>
      )}

      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <Button asChild variant="outline">
          <Link to="/packages#compare">Compare plans</Link>
        </Button>
        <Button asChild variant="ghost">
          <Link to="/packages">All plans and payment methods</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: src\components\home\ProposalsTabsSection.tsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { homeCityTabs, homeOverseasTabs, overseasTabQuery } from "@/lib/filterOptions";
import { useProfilesByLocation } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ScrollReveal } from "@/components/common/ScrollReveal";
import { ProfileCard } from "@/components/profiles/ProfileCard";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";

type Region = "pakistan" | "overseas";

export function ProposalsTabsSection() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [region, setRegion] = useState<Region>("pakistan");
  const [city, setCity] = useState<string>(homeCityTabs[0]);
  const [overseas, setOverseas] = useState<string>(homeOverseasTabs[0]);

  const filter =
    region === "pakistan"
      ? { city }
      : { country: overseasTabQuery[overseas as keyof typeof overseasTabQuery] };
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useProfilesByLocation(filter, 4);

  const viewAllHref =
    region === "pakistan"
      ? `/profiles?city=${encodeURIComponent(city)}`
      : `/profiles?country=${encodeURIComponent(overseasTabQuery[overseas as keyof typeof overseasTabQuery])}`;

  const chips = region === "pakistan" ? homeCityTabs : homeOverseasTabs;
  const activeChip = region === "pakistan" ? city : overseas;
  const setChip = region === "pakistan" ? setCity : setOverseas;

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="proposals-heading"
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 lg:py-20"
    >
      <SectionHeading
        id="proposals-heading"
        eyebrow="Curated lists"
        title={t("home.proposalsTitle")}
        description={t("home.proposalsDesc")}
      />

      <Tabs value={region} onValueChange={(value) => setRegion(value as Region)} className="mt-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <TabsList aria-label="Choose a region">
            <TabsTrigger value="pakistan">Pakistan</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="overseas">{t("home.overseasTitle")}</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>

          <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Choose a city or country"
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 sm:justify-center"
          >
            {chips.map((chip) => (
              <button
                key={chip}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setChip(chip as string)}
                aria-pressed={activeChip === chip}
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
                  activeChip === chip
                    ? "border-maroon-800 bg-maroon-50 text-maroon-900"
                    : "border-khaki-300/70 bg-white text-maroon-800/80 hover:bg-cream-100",
                )}
              >
                {chip}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <TabsContent value={region} className="mt-7">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading proposals…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-3">
                  <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-5 w-2/3" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-1/2" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : !data || data.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No proposals listed here yet"
              description="Our consultants may still have suitable proposals that are not published online. Please ask the office."
            />
          ) : (
            <ScrollReveal stagger className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
              {data.map((profile) => (
                <ProfileCard key={profile.id} profile={profile} />
              ))}
            </ScrollReveal>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
            <Button asChild variant="outline">
              <Link to={viewAllHref}>
                View all in {activeChip}
                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
              </Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>
    </section>
  );
}

import { useStats } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { statsBand } from "@/lib/homeContent";
import { ScrollReveal, RevealItem } from "@/components/common/ScrollReveal";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

export function StatsSection() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useStats();
  const stats =
    data && data.length > 0
      ? data
      : statsBand.map((item, index) => ({ id: `local-${index}`, ...item }));

  return (
    <section aria-label="Service at a glance" className="bg-maroon-900">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        {isError ? (
          <ErrorState
            onRetry={() => void refetch()}
            className="border-cream-200/30 bg-maroon-950/40"
          />
        ) : isLoading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-4" aria-busy="true">
            <span className="sr-only">Loading statistics…</span>
            {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="space-y-2">
                <Skeleton className="h-10 w-24 bg-cream-200/20" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-32 bg-cream-200/15" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ScrollReveal stagger className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-8 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {stats.map((stat) => (
              <RevealItem key={stat.id}>
                <p className="font-heading text-3xl font-semibold text-cream-50 tabular-nums sm:text-4xl">
                  {stat.value}
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-peach-300">
                  {stat.label}
                </p>
                {stat.caption ? (
                  <p className="mt-1.5 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-cream-200/70">
                    {stat.caption}
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </RevealItem>
            ))}
          </ScrollReveal>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\home\SuccessStoriesSection.tsx
========================================
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import Autoplay from "embla-carousel-autoplay";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { useSuccessStories } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { StoryCard } from "@/components/common/StoryCard";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselControls,
  CarouselItem,
} from "@/components/ui/carousel";

export function SuccessStoriesSection() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useSuccessStories();

  const plugins = useMemo(() => {
    if (reducedMotion) return [];
    return [Autoplay({ delay: 7000, stopOnInteraction: true, stopOnMouseEnter: true })];
  }, [reducedMotion]);

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="stories-heading"
      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6 lg:py-20"
    >
      <SectionHeading
        id="stories-heading"
        eyebrow="In their words"
        title={t("home.storiesTitle")}
        description="Sample stories for now. Real stories will be published only with the written consent of both families."
      />

      {isError ? (
        <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} className="mt-10" />
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-10 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
          <span className="sr-only">Loading stories…</span>
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6">
              <Skeleton className="h-5 w-6" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-16 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-4 w-1/2" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Carousel
          plugins={plugins}
          opts={{ loop: (data?.length ?? 0) > 3 }}
          className="mt-10"
          aria-label="Success stories"
        >
          <CarouselContent>
            {(data ?? []).map((story) => (
              <CarouselItem key={story.id} className="basis-full sm:basis-1/2 lg:basis-1/3">
                <StoryCard story={story} className="h-full" />
              </CarouselItem>
            ))}
          </CarouselContent>
          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
              <Link to="/success-stories">
                All stories
                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
              </Link>
            </Button>
            <CarouselControls label="success stories" />
          </div>
        </Carousel>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\AnnouncementBar.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

const STORAGE_KEY = "ahd-e-rishta.announcement-dismissed";

export function AnnouncementBar() {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setVisible(window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) !== "1");
    } catch {
      setVisible(true);
    }
  }, []);

  if (!visible) return null;

  const dismiss = () => {
    setVisible(false);
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, "1");
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative bg-maroon-950 text-cream-100">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-center gap-2 px-10 py-2.5 text-center font-sans text-[13px] leading-5 sm:px-12">
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold text-peach-300">New:</span> Overseas proposals desk now
          replies on WhatsApp within one working day.
        </p>
        <a
          href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
            message: "I saw the announcement bar — please share the overseas desk details.",
          })}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "announcement_bar" })}
          className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-peach-300/40 px-3 py-1 font-semibold text-peach-300 transition-colors hover:bg-peach-300/10 sm:inline-flex"
        >
          <WhatsAppIcon className="size-3.5" />
          Message us
        </a>
        <Link
          to="/packages"
          className="hidden shrink-0 rounded-full text-cream-200 underline decoration-peach-300/50 underline-offset-4 hover:text-white md:inline"
        >
          View plans
        </Link>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={dismiss}
        aria-label="Dismiss announcement"
        className="absolute end-2 top-1/2 grid size-8 -translate-y-1/2 place-items-center rounded-full text-cream-200/70 transition-colors hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-peach-300 focus-visible:outline-none"
      >
        <X className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\CookieConsent.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

const STORAGE_KEY = "ahd-e-rishta.cookie-consent";

/**
 * Consent banner, ready to switch on. Rendered only when analytics is enabled —
 * no vendor is wired up yet, so nothing is collected today.
 */
export function CookieConsent() {
  const analyticsEnabled = false;
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!analyticsEnabled) return;
    try {
      setVisible(window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) === null);
    } catch {
      setVisible(false);
    }
  }, [analyticsEnabled]);

  if (!analyticsEnabled || !visible) return null;

  const decide = (value: "accepted" | "declined") => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, value);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
    setVisible(false);
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="region"
      aria-label="Cookie consent"
      className="fixed inset-x-3 bottom-3 z-50 rounded-3xl border border-khaki-300 bg-cream-50 p-4 shadow-xl shadow-maroon-900/15 sm:inset-x-auto sm:end-5 sm:bottom-20 sm:max-w-sm"
    >
      <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-maroon-900">
        We use privacy-friendly analytics to understand which pages help families most. Read our{" "}
        <Link to="/privacy" className="underline underline-offset-2">
          privacy policy
        </Link>
        .
      </p>
      <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => decide("accepted")}>
          Accept
        </Button>
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => decide("declined")}>
          Decline
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\ErrorBoundary.tsx
========================================
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { AlertOctagon } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
}

/** Route-level error boundary. Catches render errors so the shell stays usable. */
export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<ErrorBoundaryProps, ErrorBoundaryState> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { hasError: false };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return { hasError: true };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo): void {
    if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
      console.error("[error-boundary]", error, info.componentStack);
    }
    // Hook a monitoring service here later.
  }

  private handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  render() {
    if (!this.state.hasError) return this.props.children;

    return (
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[60vh] w-full max-w-2xl flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-4 py-16 text-center">
        <span className="grid size-14 place-items-center rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700">
          <AlertOctagon className="size-6" aria-hidden="true" />
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-heading text-3xl font-semibold text-maroon-800">
          Something went wrong
        </h1>
        <p className="max-w-md font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
          An unexpected error occurred while showing this page. Reloading usually fixes it. If it
          keeps happening, message the office and a consultant will help.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
          <Button onClick={this.handleReload}>Reload page</Button>
          <Button variant="outline" asChild>
            <a href="/">Back to home</a>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\FloatingActions.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowUp } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

export function FloatingActions() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [showTop, setShowTop] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setShowTop(window.scrollY > 600);
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  const scrollToTop = () => {
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "smooth" });
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed end-3 bottom-3 z-30 flex flex-col items-end gap-2 sm:end-5 sm:bottom-5"
      /* keeps the buttons off the very edge on iOS safe areas without covering content */
      style={{ paddingBottom: "env(safe-area-inset-bottom)" }}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={scrollToTop}
        aria-label={t("common.backToTop")}
        className={cn(
          "grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-full border border-khaki-300 bg-cream-50 text-maroon-800 shadow-md shadow-maroon-900/15 transition-all duration-300 hover:bg-white focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
          showTop
            ? "pointer-events-auto translate-y-0 opacity-100"
            : "pointer-events-none translate-y-2 opacity-0",
        )}
      >
        <ArrowUp className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>

      <a
        href={buildWhatsAppUrl()}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "floating_button" })}
        aria-label={`${t("common.whatsappUs")} — opens WhatsApp in a new tab`}
        className="group flex size-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-green-800 text-white shadow-lg shadow-green-900/30 transition-transform hover:scale-105 hover:bg-green-900 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-green-800 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none lg:h-12 lg:w-auto lg:px-4"
      >
        <WhatsAppIcon className="size-5" />
        <span className="hidden font-sans text-sm font-semibold lg:inline">
          {t("common.whatsapp")}
        </span>
      </a>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";

import { Heart, Menu, Phone } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";

import { LanguageSwitcher } from "@/components/layout/LanguageSwitcher";

function navLinkClass({ isActive }: { isActive: boolean }) {
  return cn(
    "relative whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-2.5 py-2 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors xl:px-3",
    isActive ? "text-maroon-900" : "text-maroon-800/75 hover:text-maroon-900 hover:bg-cream-100",
  );
}

function activeUnderline(isActive: boolean) {
  return isActive ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0";
}

export function Header() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { count } = useShortlist();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border/80 bg-cream-50/95 backdrop-blur supports-[backdrop-filter]:bg-cream-50/80">
      <AnnouncementBar />
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:h-18">
        <Logo />

        <nav aria-label="Main navigation" className="hidden items-center gap-0 xl:flex">
          {siteConfig.nav.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.href}
              to={item.href}
              end={item.href === "/"}
              className={navLinkClass}
            >
              {({ isActive }) => (
                <span className="relative inline-block">
                  {t(item.label)}
                  <span
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    className={cn(
                      "absolute inset-x-0 -bottom-1 mx-auto h-0.5 w-5 rounded-full bg-linear-to-r from-maroon-700 to-peach-400 transition-opacity",
                      activeUnderline(isActive),
                    )}
                  />
                </span>
              )}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          <LanguageSwitcher className="hidden md:flex" />

          <Link
            to="/shortlist"
            aria-label={`${t("nav.shortlist")} — ${count} ${count === 1 ? "profile" : "profiles"} saved`}
            className="relative hidden size-10 place-items-center rounded-full border border-khaki-300/80 text-maroon-800 transition-colors hover:bg-cream-100 sm:grid"
          >
            <Heart aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
            {count > 0 ? (
              <span className="absolute -end-1 -top-1 grid min-w-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-maroon-700 px-1 font-sans text-[11px] font-bold text-cream-50 tabular-nums">
                {count}
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </Link>

          <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="hidden lg:inline-flex">
            <Link to="/register">{t("nav.register")}</Link>
          </Button>

          <Button
            asChild

          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );

========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\LanguageSwitcher.tsx
========================================
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { changeLanguage, type SupportedLanguage } from "@/i18n";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface LanguageSwitcherProps {
  tone?: "light" | "dark";
  className?: string;
}

export function LanguageSwitcher({ tone = "light", className }: LanguageSwitcherProps) {
  const { i18n, t } = useTranslation();
  const current: SupportedLanguage = i18n.language === "ur" ? "ur" : "en";

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full border p-0.5",
        tone === "dark" ? "border-cream-200/25" : "border-khaki-300/80",
        className,
      )}
      role="group"
      aria-label={t("nav.language")}
    >
      {(["en", "ur"] as const).map((language) => (
        <button
          key={language}
          type="button"
          onClick={() => changeLanguage(language)}
          aria-pressed={current === language}
          lang={language}
          className={cn(
            "rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 font-sans text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
            current === language
              ? tone === "dark"
                ? "bg-cream-100 text-maroon-900"
                : "bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
              : tone === "dark"
                ? "text-cream-200 hover:bg-white/10"
                : "text-maroon-800/80 hover:bg-cream-100",
          )}
        >
          {language === "en" ? "EN" : "اردو"}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { Suspense } from "react";

export function Layout() {

        href="#main-content"
        className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:start-4 focus:top-4 focus:z-50 focus:rounded-full focus:bg-maroon-900 focus:px-5 focus:py-3 focus:font-sans focus:text-sm focus:font-semibold focus:text-cream-50"

        id="main-content"
        tabIndex={-1}
        className="flex-1 pb-16 focus-visible:outline-none sm:pb-0"

      <CookieConsent />

========================================
FILE: src\components\layout\ScrollToTop.tsx
========================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

/** Resets scroll position on route change (skipped for in-page hash links). */
export function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hash) {
      const target = document.querySelector(hash);
      if (target instanceof HTMLElement) {
        target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "auto", block: "start" });
        return;
      }
    }
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "instant" });
  }, [pathname, hash]);

  return null;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { RotateCcw, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { ProfileFilters } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import {
  casteOptions,
  cityOptions,
  countryOptions,
  educationOptions,
  familyTypeOptions,
  genderOptions,
  heightOptions,
  incomeOptions,
  languageOptions,
  maritalStatusOptions,
  occupationOptions,
  religionOptions,
  sectOptions,
} from "@/lib/filterOptions";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormField, NativeSelect, controlClass } from "@/components/forms/FormField";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";

export interface FilterPanelProps {
  filters: ProfileFilters;
  onChange: (patch: Record<string, string | number | undefined>) => void;
  onClearAll: () => void;
  activeCount: number;
  className?: string;
  /** The mobile sheet already renders a title, so the panel can skip its own. */
  showTitle?: boolean;
}

const numberOrUndefined = (value: string): number | undefined => {
  if (value.trim() === "") return undefined;
  const parsed = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : undefined;
};

/** All profile filters. Shared by the desktop sidebar and the mobile sheet. */
export function FilterPanel({
  filters,
  onChange,
  onClearAll,
  activeCount,
  className,
  showTitle = true,
}: FilterPanelProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(filters.q ?? "");

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery(filters.q ?? "");
  }, [filters.q]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const trimmed = query.trim();
      if (trimmed === (filters.q ?? "")) return;
      onChange({ q: trimmed || undefined });
    }, 400);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [query, filters.q, onChange]);

  return (
    <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-5", className)}>
      <div>
        <div
          className={
            showTitle
              ? "flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
              : "flex items-center justify-end gap-2"
          }
        >
          {showTitle ? (
            <h2 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">Filters</h2>
          ) : null}
          {activeCount > 0 ? (
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClearAll} className="text-maroon-700">
              <RotateCcw aria-hidden="true" /> Clear all
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
          Every list is reviewed by a consultant. Filters help us prepare the right proposals.
        </p>
      </div>

      <FormField label="Search by name or profile ID" htmlFor="filter-q">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="pointer-events-none absolute start-3.5 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-maroon-800/50"
          />
          <input
            id="filter-q"
            type="search"
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. Ayesha or AER-1042"
            className={cn(controlClass, "ps-10")}
          />
        </div>
      </FormField>

      <Separator />

      <FormField label="Looking for" htmlFor="filter-gender">
        <NativeSelect
          id="filter-gender"
          options={genderOptions}
          placeholder="Bride or groom"
          value={filters.gender ?? ""}
          onChange={(event) => onChange({ gender: event.target.value || undefined })}
        />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label="Marital status" htmlFor="filter-marital">
        <NativeSelect
          id="filter-marital"
          options={maritalStatusOptions}
          placeholder="Any status"
          value={filters.maritalStatus ?? ""}
          onChange={(event) => onChange({ maritalStatus: event.target.value || undefined })}
        />
      </FormField>

      <fieldset>
        <legend className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900">Age range</legend>
        <div className="mt-1.5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="filter-age-from" className="sr-only">
              Minimum age
            </label>
            <input
              id="filter-age-from"
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              min={18}
              max={80}
              placeholder="From 18"
              value={filters.ageFrom ?? ""}
              onChange={(event) => onChange({ ageFrom: numberOrUndefined(event.target.value) })}
              className={controlClass}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="filter-age-to" className="sr-only">
              Maximum age
            </label>
            <input
              id="filter-age-to"
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              min={18}
              max={80}
              placeholder="To 60"
              value={filters.ageTo ?? ""}
              onChange={(event) => onChange({ ageTo: numberOrUndefined(event.target.value) })}
              className={controlClass}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset>
        <legend className="font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900">Height range</legend>

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { BadgeCheck, Briefcase, GraduationCap, MapPin, Ruler, UserRound } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  formatHeight,
  genderLabel,
  maritalLabel,
  profileHref,
  type Profile,
} from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { ProfilePhoto } from "@/components/profiles/ProfilePhoto";
import { ShortlistButton } from "@/components/profiles/ShortlistButton";
import { ShareButton } from "@/components/profiles/ShareButton";

export interface ProfileCardProps {
  profile: Profile;
  className?: string;
  /** Horizontal layout used by the grid/list toggle and shortlist page. */
  layout?: "grid" | "list";
  priority?: boolean;
  /** Hides the shortlist/share buttons when the card is a preview. */
  showActions?: boolean;
}

function DetailRow({ icon: Icon, children }: { icon: typeof MapPin; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex items-center gap-2 text-maroon-900/80">
      <Icon className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-maroon-600" aria-hidden="true" />
      <span className="truncate">{children}</span>
    </li>
  );
}

/**
 * Whole-card link built with a stretched overlay so nested buttons stay valid
 * and keyboard focus stops exactly once per card.
 */
export function ProfileCard({
  profile,
  className,
  layout = "grid",
  priority = false,
  showActions = true,
}: ProfileCardProps) {
  const isList = layout === "list";

  return (
    <article
      className={cn(
        "group relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-all duration-300",
        "hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-maroon-200 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10",
        "focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-maroon-700 focus-within:ring-offset-2",
        isList && "sm:flex-row",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <div className={cn("relative", isList ? "sm:w-48 sm:shrink-0" : "")}>
        <ProfilePhoto
          profile={profile}
          priority={priority}
          className={cn("aspect-square w-full sm:aspect-4/5", isList && "sm:h-full sm:aspect-auto")}
          sizes={isList ? "(max-width: 640px) 90vw, 200px" : "(max-width: 640px) 90vw, 300px"}
        />

        <div className="absolute inset-x-3 top-3 z-20 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {profile.packageName ? (
              <Badge variant="peach" className="shadow-sm">
                {profile.packageName}
              </Badge>
            ) : null}
            {profile.verified ? (
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1 shadow-sm">
                <BadgeCheck className="size-3.5 text-green-800" aria-hidden="true" />
                Verified
              </Badge>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          {showActions ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <ShortlistButton profileId={profile.id} />
              <ShareButton
                title={`${profile.displayName} (${profile.id})`}
                path={profileHref(profile.id)}
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <p className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-3 bottom-3 z-10 font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-cream-50">
          {profile.id}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className={cn("flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:p-5", isList && "sm:justify-center")}>
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900">
            <Link
              to={profileHref(profile.id)}
              className="after:absolute after:inset-0 after:content-[''] focus-visible:outline-none"
            >
              {profile.displayName}
            </Link>
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {genderLabel(profile.gender)} · {profile.age} yrs · {formatHeight(profile.heightCm)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <ul className="grid gap-1.5 font-sans text-sm">
          <DetailRow icon={UserRound}>
            {maritalLabel(profile.maritalStatus)} · {profile.religion}
          </DetailRow>
          <DetailRow icon={GraduationCap}>{profile.education}</DetailRow>
          <DetailRow icon={Briefcase}>{profile.occupation}</DetailRow>
          <li className="flex items-center gap-2 text-maroon-900/80">
            <MapPin className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-maroon-600" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="truncate">
              {profile.city}, {profile.country}
            </span>
          </li>
          <DetailRow icon={Ruler}>{profile.caste}</DetailRow>
        </ul>

        <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
          <span className="font-sans text-xs font-semibold text-maroon-700 underline-offset-4 group-hover:underline">
            View profile
          </span>
          <span className="font-sans text-[11px] text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true">
            Contact via office
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\profiles\ProfileCarousel.tsx
========================================
import { useMemo } from "react";
import Autoplay from "embla-carousel-autoplay";
import { useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { Profile } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import {
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselControls,
  CarouselItem,
} from "@/components/ui/carousel";
import { ProfileCard } from "@/components/profiles/ProfileCard";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";

export interface ProfileCarouselProps {
  profiles: Profile[] | undefined;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  isError?: boolean;
  onRetry?: () => void;
  /** Accessible name, e.g. "Featured grooms". */
  ariaLabel: string;
  emptyTitle?: string;
  viewAllHref?: string;
  className?: string;
  tone?: "light" | "dark";
  priority?: boolean;
}

export function ProfileCarousel({
  profiles,
  isLoading = false,
  isError = false,
  onRetry,
  ariaLabel,
  emptyTitle = "No profiles to show yet",
  viewAllHref,
  className,
  tone = "light",
  priority = false,
}: ProfileCarouselProps) {
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();

  const plugins = useMemo(() => {
    if (reducedMotion) return [];
    return [Autoplay({ delay: 6000, stopOnInteraction: true, stopOnMouseEnter: true })];
  }, [reducedMotion]);

  if (isError) {
    return <ErrorState onRetry={onRetry} className={className} />;
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className={cn("grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4", className)} aria-busy="true">
        <span className="sr-only">Loading profiles…</span>
        {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-3">
            <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-5 w-2/3" />
            <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-1/2" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!profiles || profiles.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title={emptyTitle}
        description="New proposals are added by the office every week."
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <Carousel opts={{ loop: profiles.length > 4 }} plugins={plugins} aria-label={ariaLabel}>
        <CarouselContent>
          {profiles.map((profile, index) => (
            <CarouselItem
              key={profile.id}
              className="basis-full sm:basis-1/2 lg:basis-1/3 xl:basis-1/4"
            >
              <ProfileCard
                profile={profile}
                priority={priority && index === 0}
                className="h-full"
              />
            </CarouselItem>
          ))}
        </CarouselContent>

        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          {viewAllHref ? (
            <Button asChild variant={tone === "dark" ? "secondary" : "outline"} size="sm">
              <Link to={viewAllHref}>
                View all
                <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
              </Link>
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <span />
          )}
          <CarouselControls label={ariaLabel.toLowerCase()} />
        </div>
      </Carousel>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ImageOff } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { Profile } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";

export interface ProfilePhotoProps {
  profile: Pick<Profile, "photoUrl" | "photoConsent" | "displayName" | "gender">;
  className?: string;
  /** Set for above-the-fold images so they load eagerly. */
  priority?: boolean;
  sizes?: string;
}

/**
 * Renders a consenting profile photo or the "Photo on request" placeholder.
 * Never renders a real person's photograph: mock data uses abstract artwork.
 */
export function ProfilePhoto({ profile, className, priority = false, sizes }: ProfilePhotoProps) {
  const showPhoto = profile.photoConsent && Boolean(profile.photoUrl);

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "relative isolate overflow-hidden bg-linear-to-b from-cream-100 to-cream-200",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {showPhoto ? (
        <img
          src={profile.photoUrl ?? undefined}
          alt={`Profile illustration for ${profile.displayName}`}
          width={640}
          height={800}
          sizes={sizes ?? "(max-width: 640px) 90vw, (max-width: 1024px) 45vw, 300px"}
          loading={priority ? "eager" : "lazy"}
          decoding="async"
          fetchPriority={priority ? "high" : "auto"}
          className="size-full object-cover"
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex size-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 text-center">
          <span className="grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-full border border-khaki-300/70 bg-white/70 text-maroon-700">
            <ImageOff className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <p className="font-heading text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-800">Photo on request</p>
          <p className="max-w-40 font-sans text-[11px] leading-4 text-muted-foreground">
            Shared by the office only with the family&apos;s consent.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-16 bg-linear-to-t from-maroon-950/25 to-transparent"
      />
    </div>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\profiles\ShareButton.tsx
========================================
import { Share2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ShareButtonProps {
  title: string;
  className?: string;
  /** Path to share; defaults to the current URL. */
  path?: string;
}

export function ShareButton({ title, className, path }: ShareButtonProps) {
  const onClick = async (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    const url = path ? `${window.location.origin}${path}` : window.location.href;
    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share({ title, url });
        return;
      }
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      toast.success("Link copied. Share it with your family.");
    } catch {
      toast.info("Press and hold the address bar to copy this link.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={`Share ${title}`}
      title="Share"
      className={cn(
        "grid size-9 place-items-center rounded-full border border-khaki-300 bg-white/90 text-maroon-800 transition-colors hover:bg-cream-100 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <Share2 aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
    </button>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\components\profiles\ShortlistButton.tsx
========================================
import { Heart } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useShortlist } from "@/hooks/useShortlist";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";

interface ShortlistButtonProps {
  profileId: string;
  className?: string;
  /** Larger control for the detail page. */
  size?: "sm" | "md";
  withLabel?: boolean;
}

/** Saves a profile to the visitor's shortlist (localStorage). Never navigates. */
export function ShortlistButton({
  profileId,
  className,
  size = "sm",
  withLabel = false,
}: ShortlistButtonProps) {
  const { has, toggle } = useShortlist();
  const saved = has(profileId);

  const onClick = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    const next = toggle(profileId);
    trackEvent("shortlist_toggle", { profileId, saved: next });
    toast.success(
      next
        ? `Saved to your shortlist (${profileId}).`
        : `Removed ${profileId} from your shortlist.`,
    );
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-pressed={saved}
      aria-label={saved ? `Remove ${profileId} from shortlist` : `Save ${profileId} to shortlist`}
      title={saved ? "Remove from shortlist" : "Save to shortlist"}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
        size === "sm" ? "size-9" : "h-11 px-4",
        saved
          ? "border-maroon-700 bg-maroon-700 text-cream-50 hover:bg-maroon-800"
          : "border-khaki-300 bg-white/90 text-maroon-800 hover:bg-cream-100",
        className,
      )}
    >
      <Heart
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn("size-4", saved ? "fill-current" : undefined)}
        strokeWidth={2}
      />
      {withLabel ? (
        <span className="font-sans text-sm font-semibold">{saved ? "Saved" : "Save"}</span>
      ) : null}
    </button>
  );
}

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-3 py-1 font-sans text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors [&_svg]:size-3.5",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-transparent bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50",
        secondary: "border-khaki-300 bg-cream-100 text-maroon-900",
        outline: "border-maroon-800/20 bg-transparent text-maroon-800",
        peach: "border-transparent bg-peach-100 text-maroon-900",
        success: "border-green-700/20 bg-green-50 text-green-900",
        muted: "border-transparent bg-cream-200/70 text-maroon-800",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default" },
  },
);

function Badge({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants>) {
  return <span className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />;
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants };

function Breadcrumb({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"nav">) {
  return <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className={cn("text-sm", className)} {...props} />;
}

function BreadcrumbList({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"ol">) {
  return (
    <ol
      className={cn("flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function BreadcrumbItem({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"li">) {
  return <li className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5", className)} {...props} />;
}

function BreadcrumbSeparator({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      role="presentation"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={cn("inline-flex text-khaki-500", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      <ChevronRight className="size-3.5 rtl:rotate-180" />
    </span>
  );
}

function BreadcrumbPage({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span aria-current="page" className={cn("font-medium text-maroon-900", className)} {...props} />
  );
}

import * as React from "react";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full font-sans text-sm font-semibold transition-[background-color,color,box-shadow,transform] duration-200 outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-cream-50 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0 active:translate-y-px",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/15 hover:bg-maroon-900",
        secondary: "bg-cream-100 text-maroon-900 border border-khaki-300/70 hover:bg-cream-200",
        outline:
          "border border-maroon-800/25 bg-transparent text-maroon-800 hover:bg-maroon-50 hover:border-maroon-800/40",
        ghost: "text-maroon-800 hover:bg-maroon-50",
        link: "text-maroon-800 underline-offset-4 hover:underline rounded-md",
        peach: "bg-peach-400 text-maroon-950 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/10 hover:bg-peach-300",
        whatsapp: "bg-green-800 text-white shadow-sm shadow-green-900/20 hover:bg-green-900",
        destructive: "bg-red-700 text-white hover:bg-red-800",
      },
      size: {
        sm: "h-9 px-4 text-[13px]",
        default: "h-11 px-5",
        lg: "h-12 px-7 text-base",
        xl: "h-14 px-8 text-base",
        icon: "size-11",
        "icon-sm": "size-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default", size: "default" },
  },
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button";
    return (
      <Comp className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))} ref={ref} {...props} />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

export { Button, buttonVariants };

========================================
FILE: src\components\ui\carousel.tsx
========================================
import * as React from "react";
import useEmblaCarousel, { type UseEmblaCarouselType } from "embla-carousel-react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Pause, Play } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

type CarouselApi = UseEmblaCarouselType[1];
type UseCarouselParameters = Parameters<typeof useEmblaCarousel>;

export interface CarouselProps extends React.ComponentProps<"div"> {
  opts?: UseCarouselParameters[0];
  plugins?: UseCarouselParameters[1];
  setApi?: (api: CarouselApi) => void;
}

interface CarouselContextValue {
  carouselRef: UseEmblaCarouselType[0];
  api: CarouselApi;
  scrollPrev: () => void;
  scrollNext: () => void;
  canScrollPrev: boolean;
  canScrollNext: boolean;
  isAutoplayRunning: boolean;
  toggleAutoplay: () => void;
  hasAutoplay: boolean;
}

const CarouselContext = React.createContext<CarouselContextValue | null>(null);

function useCarousel(): CarouselContextValue {
  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />");
  return context;
}

export function Carousel({ opts, plugins, setApi, className, children, ...props }: CarouselProps) {
  const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel(
    { align: "start", containScroll: "trimSnaps", ...opts },
    plugins,
  );
  const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false);
  const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false);
  const [isAutoplayRunning, setIsAutoplayRunning] = React.useState(false);

  const autoplayPlugin = React.useMemo(() => {
    const found = plugins?.find((plugin) => (plugin as { name?: string }).name === "autoplay");
    return found as { stop: () => void; play: () => void; isPlaying: () => boolean } | undefined;
  }, [plugins]);

  const onSelect = React.useCallback((emblaApi: NonNullable<CarouselApi>) => {
    setCanScrollPrev(emblaApi.canScrollPrev());
    setCanScrollNext(emblaApi.canScrollNext());
  }, []);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!api) return;
    onSelect(api);
    api.on("select", onSelect);
    api.on("reInit", onSelect);
    if (autoplayPlugin) setIsAutoplayRunning(autoplayPlugin.isPlaying());
    return () => {
      api.off("select", onSelect);
      api.off("reInit", onSelect);
    };
  }, [api, onSelect, autoplayPlugin]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (setApi) setApi(api);
  }, [api, setApi]);

  const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => api?.scrollPrev(), [api]);
  const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => api?.scrollNext(), [api]);

  const toggleAutoplay = React.useCallback(() => {
    if (!autoplayPlugin) return;
    if (autoplayPlugin.isPlaying()) {
      autoplayPlugin.stop();
      setIsAutoplayRunning(false);
    } else {
      autoplayPlugin.play();
      setIsAutoplayRunning(true);
    }
  }, [autoplayPlugin]);

  const value = React.useMemo<CarouselContextValue>(
    () => ({
      carouselRef,
      api,
      scrollPrev,
      scrollNext,
      canScrollPrev,
      canScrollNext,
      isAutoplayRunning,
      toggleAutoplay,
      hasAutoplay: Boolean(autoplayPlugin),
    }),
    [
      carouselRef,
      api,
      scrollPrev,
      scrollNext,
      canScrollPrev,
      canScrollNext,
      isAutoplayRunning,
      toggleAutoplay,
      autoplayPlugin,
    ],
  );

  return (
    <CarouselContext.Provider value={value}>
      <div
        className={cn("relative", className)}
        role="region"
        aria-roledescription="carousel"
        {...props}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </CarouselContext.Provider>
  );
}

const CarouselContent = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.ComponentProps<"div">>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const { carouselRef } = useCarousel();
    return (
      <div ref={carouselRef} className="overflow-hidden">
        <div ref={ref} className={cn("-ms-4 flex touch-pan-y", className)} {...props} />
      </div>
    );
  },
);
CarouselContent.displayName = "CarouselContent";

const CarouselItem = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, React.ComponentProps<"div">>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      role="group"
      aria-roledescription="slide"
      className={cn("min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full ps-4", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  ),
);
CarouselItem.displayName = "CarouselItem";

function CarouselControls({ className, label }: { className?: string; label: string }) {
  const {
    scrollPrev,
    scrollNext,
    canScrollPrev,
    canScrollNext,
    hasAutoplay,
    isAutoplayRunning,
    toggleAutoplay,
  } = useCarousel();
  return (
    <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-2", className)}>
      {hasAutoplay ? (
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="icon-sm"
          onClick={toggleAutoplay}
          aria-label={isAutoplayRunning ? `Pause ${label}` : `Play ${label}`}
          aria-pressed={isAutoplayRunning}
        >
          {isAutoplayRunning ? <Pause aria-hidden="true" /> : <Play aria-hidden="true" />}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        size="icon-sm"
        onClick={scrollPrev}
        disabled={!canScrollPrev}
        aria-label={`Previous ${label}`}
      >
        <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
      </Button>
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        size="icon-sm"
        onClick={scrollNext}
        disabled={!canScrollNext}
        aria-label={`Next ${label}`}
      >
        <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

export { CarouselContent, CarouselItem, CarouselControls, useCarousel };

import * as React from "react";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface CheckboxProps extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "type"> {
  label?: React.ReactNode;
  description?: string;
}

const Checkbox = React.forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, CheckboxProps>(
  ({ className, label, description, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const generatedId = React.useId();
    const inputId = id ?? generatedId;
    return (
      <div className={cn("flex items-start gap-3", className)}>
        <span className="relative mt-0.5 grid size-5 shrink-0 place-items-center">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            id={inputId}
            type="checkbox"
            className="peer size-5 cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-md border border-khaki-400 bg-white transition-colors checked:border-maroon-800 checked:bg-maroon-800 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none aria-invalid:border-red-600"
            aria-describedby={description ? `${inputId}-description` : undefined}
            {...props}
          />
          <Check
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="pointer-events-none absolute size-3.5 text-cream-50 opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100"
            strokeWidth={3}
          />
        </span>
        {label ? (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-maroon-900">
            {label}
            {description ? (
              <span
                id={`${inputId}-description`}
                className="block text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground"
              >
                {description}
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </label>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  },
);
Checkbox.displayName = "Checkbox";

export { Checkbox };

import * as React from "react";

const Dialog = DialogPrimitive.Root;

  />
));

import * as React from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface SimpleOption {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}

export interface OptionSelectProps extends Omit<React.ComponentProps<"select">, "children"> {
  options: SimpleOption[];
  placeholder?: string;
}

/**
 * Styled native <select>. Native keeps mobile UX and keyboard behaviour flawless
 * while still matching the design system.
 */
const OptionSelect = React.forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, OptionSelectProps>(
  ({ className, options, placeholder, ...props }, ref) => (
    <div className="relative">
      <select
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "h-11 w-full appearance-none rounded-2xl border border-input bg-white ps-4 pe-10 font-sans text-base text-foreground shadow-xs transition-colors",
          "focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700/20 focus-visible:outline-none",
          "aria-invalid:border-red-600 aria-invalid:ring-2 aria-invalid:ring-red-600/15",
          "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70",
          className,
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {placeholder ? <option value="">{placeholder}</option> : null}
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <ChevronDown
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute end-3.5 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-maroon-800/60"
      />
    </div>
  ),
);
OptionSelect.displayName = "OptionSelect";

export { OptionSelect };

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, MoreHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

interface PaginationProps {
  page: number;
  totalPages: number;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
  className?: string;
}

function getPages(page: number, totalPages: number): (number | "ellipsis")[] {
  if (totalPages <= 7) return Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  const pages: (number | "ellipsis")[] = [1];
  const start = Math.max(2, page - 1);
  const end = Math.min(totalPages - 1, page + 1);
  if (start > 2) pages.push("ellipsis");
  for (let i = start; i <= end; i += 1) pages.push(i);
  if (end < totalPages - 1) pages.push("ellipsis");
  pages.push(totalPages);
  return pages;
}

export function Pagination({ page, totalPages, onPageChange, className }: PaginationProps) {
  if (totalPages <= 1) return null;

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Pagination"
      className={cn("flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5", className)}
    >
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        size="icon-sm"
        onClick={() => onPageChange(page - 1)}
        disabled={page <= 1}
        aria-label="Previous page"
      >
        <ChevronLeft aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
      </Button>
      {getPages(page, totalPages).map((item, index) =>
        item === "ellipsis" ? (
          <span
            key={`ellipsis-${index}`}
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="grid size-9 place-items-center text-khaki-500"
          >
            <MoreHorizontal className="size-4" />
          </span>
        ) : (
          <Button
            key={item}
            type="button"
            variant={item === page ? "default" : "outline"}
            size="icon-sm"
            onClick={() => onPageChange(item)}
            aria-label={`Page ${item}`}
            aria-current={item === page ? "page" : undefined}
            className="font-heading tabular-nums"
          >
            {item}
          </Button>
        ),
      )}
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        size="icon-sm"
        onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)}
        disabled={page >= totalPages}
        aria-label="Next page"
      >
        <ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" className="rtl:rotate-180" />
      </Button>
    </nav>
  );
}
