import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getHeroSlides } from "@/api/heroSlides.api";
import { getStats } from "@/api/stats.api";
import { getPlans } from "@/api/plans.api";
import { getConsultants } from "@/api/consultants.api";
import { getSuccessStories } from "@/api/successStories.api";
import { getVideos } from "@/api/videos.api";
import { getFaqs } from "@/api/faqs.api";
import { getPosts, getPostBySlug } from "@/api/posts.api";
import { getServices, getServiceBySlug } from "@/api/services.api";
import {
  getFeaturedProfiles,
  getLatestProfiles,
  getProfileById,
  getProfiles,
  getProfilesByIds,
  getProfilesByLocation,
  getSecondMarriageProfiles,
  getSimilarProfiles,
} from "@/api/profiles.api";
import type { Profile, ProfileFilters } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";

export const queryKeys = {
  heroSlides: ["hero-slides"] as const,
  stats: ["stats"] as const,
  plans: ["plans"] as const,
  consultants: ["consultants"] as const,
  successStories: ["success-stories"] as const,
  videos: ["videos"] as const,
  faqs: ["faqs"] as const,
  posts: ["posts"] as const,
  post: (slug: string) => ["posts", slug] as const,
  services: ["services"] as const,
  service: (slug: string) => ["services", slug] as const,
  profiles: (filters: ProfileFilters) => ["profiles", filters] as const,
  profile: (id: string) => ["profile", id] as const,
  similarProfiles: (id: string) => ["profiles", id, "similar"] as const,
  featured: (gender: "male" | "female") => ["profiles", "featured", gender] as const,
  latest: (gender: "male" | "female") => ["profiles", "latest", gender] as const,
  secondMarriage: (status: "divorced" | "widowed") =>
    ["profiles", "second-marriage", status] as const,
  byLocation: (key: string) => ["profiles", "location", key] as const,
  byIds: (ids: string[]) => ["profiles", "by-ids", ids.join(",")] as const,
};

export function useHeroSlides() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.heroSlides,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getHeroSlides(signal),
  });
}

export function useStats() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.stats, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getStats(signal) });
}

export function usePlans() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.plans, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getPlans(signal) });
}

export function useConsultants() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.consultants,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getConsultants(signal),
  });
}

export function useSuccessStories() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.successStories,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getSuccessStories(signal),
  });
}

export function useVideos() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.videos, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getVideos(signal) });
}

export function useFaqs() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.faqs, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getFaqs(signal) });
}

export function usePosts() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.posts, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getPosts(signal) });
}

export function usePost(slug: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.post(slug),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getPostBySlug(slug, signal),
    enabled: Boolean(slug),
  });
}

export function useServices() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.services, queryFn: ({ signal }) => getServices(signal) });
}

export function useService(slug: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.service(slug),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getServiceBySlug(slug, signal),
    enabled: Boolean(slug),
  });
}

export function useProfiles(filters: ProfileFilters) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.profiles(filters),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getProfiles(filters, signal),

  });
}

    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getProfileById(id, signal),

    retry: false,

    queryKey: queryKeys.similarProfiles(profile?.id ?? "none"),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getSimilarProfiles(profile as Profile, signal),
    enabled: Boolean(profile),

  return useQuery({

    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getFeaturedProfiles(gender, signal),
  });
}

  return useQuery({

    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getLatestProfiles(gender, signal),

}

    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getSecondMarriageProfiles(status, signal),
  });
}

export function useProfilesByIds(ids: string[]) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.byIds(ids),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => getProfilesByIds(ids, signal),
    enabled: ids.length > 0,
  });
}

export function useProfilesByLocation(filter: { city?: string; country?: string }, limit = 4) {
  const key = filter.city ?? filter.country ?? "all";

FILE: src\hooks\useDebouncedValue.ts
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function useDebouncedValue<T>(value: T, delay = 350): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [value, delay]);

  return debounced;
}

FILE: src\hooks\useMediaQuery.ts
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

/** SSR-safe media query hook. */
export function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState(() =>
    typeof window === "undefined" ? false : window.matchMedia(query).matches,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia(query);
    const onChange = (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => setMatches(event.matches);
    setMatches(mediaQuery.matches);
    mediaQuery.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => mediaQuery.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, [query]);

  return matches;
}

export function useIsDesktop(): boolean {
  return useMediaQuery("(min-width: 1024px)");
}


========================================
FILE: src\hooks\useProfileFilters.ts
========================================
import { useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { profileFiltersSchema, type ProfileFilters } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { PROFILE_FILTER_KEYS } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";

export interface ProfileFiltersApi {
  filters: ProfileFilters;
  setFilters: (patch: Record<string, string | number | undefined>) => void;
  removeFilter: (key: keyof ProfileFilters) => void;
  clearAll: () => void;
  activeCount: number;
}

const FILTER_DEFAULTS: ProfileFilters = { page: 1, sort: "newest", view: "grid" };

/** Keeps profile filters in the URL so links are shareable and back/forward works. */
export function useProfileFilters(): ProfileFiltersApi {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const filters = useMemo<ProfileFilters>(() => {
    const raw: Record<string, string> = {};
    PROFILE_FILTER_KEYS.forEach((key) => {
      const value = searchParams.get(key);
      if (value !== null && value !== "") raw[key] = value;
    });
    const parsed = profileFiltersSchema.safeParse(raw);
    if (!parsed.success) return { ...FILTER_DEFAULTS };
    return { ...FILTER_DEFAULTS, ...parsed.data };
  }, [searchParams]);

  const setFilters = useCallback(
    (patch: Record<string, string | number | undefined>) => {
      const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
      Object.entries(patch).forEach(([key, value]) => {
        if (value === undefined || value === "" || value === null) {
          next.delete(key);
        } else {
          next.set(key, String(value));
        }
      });
      const isPaging = Object.keys(patch).length === 1 && Object.keys(patch)[0] === "page";
      if (!isPaging) next.delete("page");
      setSearchParams(next, { replace: false });
    },
    [searchParams, setSearchParams],
  );

  const removeFilter = useCallback(
    (key: keyof ProfileFilters) => {
      const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
      next.delete(key);
      next.delete("page");
      setSearchParams(next);
    },
    [searchParams, setSearchParams],
  );

  const clearAll = useCallback(() => {
    const next = new URLSearchParams();
    const view = searchParams.get("view");
    if (view === "list") next.set("view", "list");
    setSearchParams(next);
  }, [searchParams, setSearchParams]);

  const activeCount = useMemo(
    () =>
      Array.from(searchParams.keys()).filter((key) => !["page", "sort", "view"].includes(key))
        .length,
    [searchParams],
  );

  return { filters, setFilters, removeFilter, clearAll, activeCount };
}


========================================
FILE: src\hooks\useSeo.ts
========================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";

interface SeoOptions {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  image?: string;
  type?: "website" | "article" | "profile";
  jsonLd?: Record<string, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>[];
  noIndex?: boolean;
  /** Path such as "/profiles" — used for the canonical link. */
  path?: string;
}

function upsertMeta(selector: string, attrs: Record<string, string>): void {
  let element = document.head.querySelector<HTMLMetaElement>(selector);
  if (!element) {
    element = document.createElement("meta");
    document.head.appendChild(element);
  }
  Object.entries(attrs).forEach(([key, value]) => element?.setAttribute(key, value));
}

function upsertLink(rel: string, href: string): void {
  let element = document.head.querySelector<HTMLLinkElement>(`link[rel="${rel}"]`);
  if (!element) {
    element = document.createElement("link");
    element.rel = rel;
    document.head.appendChild(element);
  }
  element.href = href;
}

const JSON_LD_ID = "ahd-e-rishta-jsonld";

/** Sets document title, description, Open Graph, canonical URL and optional JSON-LD. */
export function useSeo({
  title,
  description,
  image = `${env.siteUrl.replace(/\/$/, "")}/og-image.jpg`,
  type = "website",
  jsonLd,
  noIndex = false,
  path,
}: SeoOptions): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const fullTitle = title.includes(siteConfig.name) ? title : `${title} | ${siteConfig.name}`;
    document.title = fullTitle;

    upsertMeta('meta[name="description"]', { name: "description", content: description });
    upsertMeta('meta[name="robots"]', {
      name: "robots",
      content: noIndex ? "noindex, nofollow" : "index, follow",
    });
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:title"]', { property: "og:title", content: fullTitle });
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:description"]', {
      property: "og:description",
      content: description,
    });
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:type"]', { property: "og:type", content: type });
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:site_name"]', {
      property: "og:site_name",
      content: siteConfig.name,
    });
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:image"]', { property: "og:image", content: image });
    upsertMeta('meta[name="twitter:card"]', {
      name: "twitter:card",
      content: "summary_large_image",
    });
    upsertMeta('meta[name="twitter:title"]', { name: "twitter:title", content: fullTitle });
    upsertMeta('meta[name="twitter:description"]', {
      name: "twitter:description",
      content: description,
    });

    const canonicalPath = path ?? window.location.pathname;
    const canonical = `${env.siteUrl.replace(/\/$/, "")}${canonicalPath}`;
    upsertLink("canonical", canonical);
    upsertMeta('meta[property="og:url"]', { property: "og:url", content: canonical });

    document.getElementById(JSON_LD_ID)?.remove();
    if (jsonLd) {
      const script = document.createElement("script");
      script.type = "application/ld+json";
      script.id = JSON_LD_ID;
      script.textContent = JSON.stringify(jsonLd);
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    }

    return () => {
      document.getElementById(JSON_LD_ID)?.remove();
    };
  }, [title, description, image, type, jsonLd, noIndex, path]);
}


========================================
FILE: src\hooks\useShortlist.ts
========================================
import { useCallback, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "ahd-e-rishta.shortlist.v1";
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

let snapshot: string[] = [];

function readStorage(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string").slice(0, 200);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeStorage(ids: string[]): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(ids));
  } catch {
    /* storage may be unavailable (private mode / quota) — fail silently */
  }
}

function emit(): void {
  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}

function getSnapshot(): string[] {
  const stored = readStorage();
  const same =
    stored.length === snapshot.length && stored.every((id, index) => snapshot[index] === id);
  if (!same) snapshot = stored;
  return snapshot;
}

function getServerSnapshot(): string[] {
  return snapshot;
}

export interface ShortlistApi {
  ids: string[];
  count: number;
  has: (id: string) => boolean;
  toggle: (id: string) => boolean;
  add: (id: string) => void;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  clear: () => void;
}

/** Visitor shortlist persisted in localStorage. Shared by ProfileCard, detail page and header. */
export function useShortlist(): ShortlistApi {
  const ids = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot);

  const update = useCallback((next: string[]) => {
    writeStorage(next);
    snapshot = next;
    emit();
  }, []);

  const has = useCallback((id: string) => ids.includes(id), [ids]);

  const add = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      if (ids.includes(id)) return;
      update([id, ...ids]);
    },
    [ids, update],
  );

  const remove = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      update(ids.filter((item) => item !== id));
    },
    [ids, update],
  );

  const toggle = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      if (ids.includes(id)) {
        update(ids.filter((item) => item !== id));
        return false;
      }
      update([id, ...ids]);
      return true;
    },
    [ids, update],
  );

  const clear = useCallback(() => update([]), [update]);

  return { ids, count: ids.length, has, toggle, add, remove, clear };
}

FILE: src\i18n\index.ts
========================================
import i18n from "i18next";
import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";
import { en, ur } from "@/i18n/resources";

export const SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES = ["en", "ur"] as const;
export type SupportedLanguage = (typeof SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES)[number];

const STORAGE_KEY = "ahd-e-rishta.language";
export const URDU_FONT_EVENT = "ahd-e-rishta:urdu-font-loaded";

function readStoredLanguage(): SupportedLanguage {
  try {
    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (stored === "ur" || stored === "en") return stored;
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  return "en";
}

export function applyDocumentLanguage(language: SupportedLanguage): void {
  document.documentElement.lang = language;
  document.documentElement.dir = language === "ur" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
}

let urduFontPromise: Promise<unknown> | null = null;

/** Loads the Nastaliq font only when Urdu is actually selected. */
export function loadUrduFont(): Promise<unknown> {
  if (!urduFontPromise) {
    urduFontPromise = import("@fontsource-variable/noto-nastaliq-urdu")
      .then((mod) => {
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(URDU_FONT_EVENT));
        return mod;
      })
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }
  return urduFontPromise;
}

void i18n.use(initReactI18next).init({
  resources: {
    en: { translation: en },
    ur: { translation: ur },
  },
  lng: typeof window === "undefined" ? "en" : readStoredLanguage(),
  fallbackLng: "en",
  interpolation: { escapeValue: false },
  returnNull: false,
});

export function changeLanguage(language: SupportedLanguage): void {
  void i18n.changeLanguage(language);
  applyDocumentLanguage(language);
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, language);
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  if (language === "ur") void loadUrduFont();
}

if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
  const initial = (i18n.language === "ur" ? "ur" : "en") as SupportedLanguage;
  applyDocumentLanguage(initial);
  if (initial === "ur") void loadUrduFont();
}

export default i18n;

/**
 * Tiny vendor-free analytics placeholder. Swap the body of `trackEvent`
 * for a real provider later; call sites do not need to change.
 */
export type AnalyticsEvent =
  | "whatsapp_click"
  | "register_step"
  | "register_submit"
  | "search_submit"
  | "profile_view"
  | "shortlist_toggle"
  | "newsletter_submit"
  | "contact_submit"
  | "video_open"
  | "filter_change";

export interface AnalyticsPayload {
  [key: string]: string | number | boolean | undefined;
}

export function trackEvent(event: AnalyticsEvent, payload: AnalyticsPayload = {}): void {
  if (import.meta.env.DEV && import.meta.env.VITE_ANALYTICS_DEBUG === "true") {
    console.info("[analytics]", event, payload);
  }
  // Intentionally no-op in production until a provider is connected.
}

FILE: src\lib\env.ts

import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * Runtime-validated environment. Every variable is optional so the app can boot
 * in a preview environment without secrets; sensible placeholders are used.
 */
const envSchema = z.object({
  VITE_API_URL: z.string().url().default("http://localhost:4000/api"),
  VITE_USE_MOCK_API: z
    .enum(["true", "false"])
    .default("true")
    .transform((value) => value === "true"),
  VITE_WHATSAPP_NUMBER: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{10,15}$/, "WhatsApp number must be digits only, e.g. 923001234567")
    .default("923001234567"),
  VITE_WHATSAPP_MESSAGE: z
    .string()
    .min(1)
    .default("Assalam-o-Alaikum, I would like to know more about Ahd-e-Rishta."),
  VITE_SITE_URL: z.string().url().default("https://ahde-rishta.example.com"),
});

const parsed = envSchema.safeParse(import.meta.env);

if (!parsed.success && import.meta.env.DEV) {
  console.warn(
    "[env] Invalid environment variables, falling back to defaults.",
    parsed.error.flatten(),
  );
}

const safe = parsed.success
  ? parsed.data
  : {
      VITE_API_URL: "http://localhost:4000/api",
      VITE_USE_MOCK_API: true,
      VITE_WHATSAPP_NUMBER: "923001234567",
      VITE_WHATSAPP_MESSAGE: "Assalam-o-Alaikum, I would like to know more about Ahd-e-Rishta.",
      VITE_SITE_URL: "https://ahde-rishta.example.com",
    };

export const env = {
  apiUrl: safe.VITE_API_URL,
  useMockApi: safe.VITE_USE_MOCK_API,
  whatsappNumber: safe.VITE_WHATSAPP_NUMBER,
  whatsappMessage: safe.VITE_WHATSAPP_MESSAGE,
  siteUrl: safe.VITE_SITE_URL,
} as const;


========================================
FILE: src\lib\filterChips.ts
========================================
import type { ProfileFilters } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import {
  casteOptions,
  cityOptions,
  countryOptions,
  educationOptions,
  familyTypeOptions,
  heightOptions,
  incomeOptions,
  languageOptions,
  maritalStatusOptions,
  occupationOptions,
  religionOptions,
  sectOptions,
} from "@/lib/filterOptions";

export interface FilterChip {
  key: keyof ProfileFilters;
  label: string;
}

function labelOf(options: { label: string; value: string }[], value: string | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  return options.find((option) => option.value === value)?.label ?? value;
}

function heightLabel(value: number | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return "";
  return heightOptions.find((option) => Number(option.value) === value)?.label ?? `${value} cm`;
}

/** Human-readable chips for the active filters shown above the results. */
export function buildFilterChips(filters: ProfileFilters): FilterChip[] {
  const chips: FilterChip[] = [];

  if (filters.q) chips.push({ key: "q", label: `Search: ${filters.q}` });
  if (filters.category === "featured") chips.push({ key: "category", label: "Featured only" });
  if (filters.category === "second-marriage")
    chips.push({ key: "category", label: "Second marriage" });
  if (filters.gender) {
    chips.push({ key: "gender", label: filters.gender === "male" ? "Groom" : "Bride" });
  }
  if (filters.maritalStatus) {
    chips.push({
      key: "maritalStatus",
      label: labelOf(maritalStatusOptions, filters.maritalStatus),
    });
  }
  if (filters.ageFrom || filters.ageTo) {
    chips.push({
      key: filters.ageFrom ? "ageFrom" : "ageTo",
      label: `Age ${filters.ageFrom ?? 18}–${filters.ageTo ?? 80}`,
    });
  }
  if (filters.heightFrom || filters.heightTo) {
    chips.push({
      key: filters.heightFrom ? "heightFrom" : "heightTo",
      label: `Height ${heightLabel(filters.heightFrom) || "4' 0\""}–${
        heightLabel(filters.heightTo) || "7' 0\""
      }`,
    });
  }
  if (filters.city) chips.push({ key: "city", label: labelOf(cityOptions, filters.city) });
  if (filters.country)
    chips.push({ key: "country", label: labelOf(countryOptions, filters.country) });
  if (filters.education)
    chips.push({ key: "education", label: labelOf(educationOptions, filters.education) });
  if (filters.occupation)
    chips.push({ key: "occupation", label: labelOf(occupationOptions, filters.occupation) });
  if (filters.income) chips.push({ key: "income", label: labelOf(incomeOptions, filters.income) });
  if (filters.religion)
    chips.push({ key: "religion", label: labelOf(religionOptions, filters.religion) });
  if (filters.sect) chips.push({ key: "sect", label: labelOf(sectOptions, filters.sect) });
  if (filters.caste) chips.push({ key: "caste", label: labelOf(casteOptions, filters.caste) });
  if (filters.language)
    chips.push({ key: "language", label: labelOf(languageOptions, filters.language) });
  if (filters.familyType) {
    chips.push({ key: "familyType", label: labelOf(familyTypeOptions, filters.familyType) });
  }

  return chips;
}


========================================
FILE: src\lib\filterOptions.ts
========================================
/** DUMMY DATA, replace with real data or API. Option lists used across filters and forms. */

export interface Option {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}

export const genderOptions: Option[] = [
  { label: "Groom (male)", value: "male" },
  { label: "Bride (female)", value: "female" },
];

export const maritalStatusOptions: Option[] = [
  { label: "Never married", value: "never-married" },
  { label: "Divorced", value: "divorced" },
  { label: "Widowed", value: "widowed" },
];

export const familyTypeOptions: Option[] = [
  { label: "Nuclear family", value: "nuclear" },
  { label: "Joint family", value: "joint" },
];

export const religionOptions: Option[] = ["Islam", "Christianity", "Hinduism", "Sikhism"].map(
  (v) => ({
    label: v,
    value: v,
  }),
);

export const sectOptions: Option[] = [

export const heightOptions: Option[] = Array.from({ length: 19 }, (_, index) => {
  const totalInches = 48 + index; // 4'0" → 7'0"
  const feet = Math.floor(totalInches / 12);
  const inches = totalInches % 12;
  const cm = Math.round(totalInches * 2.54);
  return { label: `${feet}' ${inches}"`, value: String(cm) };
});

FILE: src\lib\utils.ts

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

/** Converts a string to a URL-safe slug. */
export function slugify(value: string): string {
  return value
    .toLowerCase()
    .trim()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s-]/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, "-")
    .replace(/-+/g, "-");
}

export function titleCase(value: string): string {
  return value
    .split(/[\s-]+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
    .join(" ");
}

export function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.min(Math.max(value, min), max);
}

export function formatCount(n: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PK").format(n);
}


========================================
FILE: src\lib\whatsapp.ts
========================================
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

interface WhatsAppOptions {
  /** Any extra context, e.g. profile ID, plan name or service name. */
  message?: string;
}

/**
 * Builds a wa.me link with a context-aware, prefilled message.
 * Always normalises the number to digits only.
 */
export function buildWhatsAppUrl(options: WhatsAppOptions = {}): string {
  const number = env.whatsappNumber.replace(/\D/g, "");
  const base = options.message?.trim();
  const text = base ? `${env.whatsappMessage}\n\n${base}` : env.whatsappMessage;
  return `https://wa.me/${number}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
}

export function whatsappForProfile(profileId: string, displayName: string): string {
  return buildWhatsAppUrl({
    message: `Profile ID: ${profileId}\nName: ${displayName}\nI would like to enquire about this proposal.`,
  });
}

export function whatsappForPlan(planName: string): string {
  return buildWhatsAppUrl({
    message: `Plan: ${planName}\nI would like to know more about this membership plan.`,
  });
}

export function whatsappForService(serviceName: string): string {
  return buildWhatsAppUrl({
    message: `Service: ${serviceName}\nPlease share the details and process.`,
  });
}

import type { Variants } from "motion/react";

const soft = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] as const;

  hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 24 },
  visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.55, ease: soft } },
};

export const fadeIn: Variants = {
  hidden: { opacity: 0 },
  visible: { opacity: 1, transition: { duration: 0.5, ease: soft } },
};

export const staggerParent: Variants = {
  hidden: {},
  visible: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.08, delayChildren: 0.05 } },
};

export const cardHover = {
  rest: { y: 0 },
  hover: { y: -4, transition: { duration: 0.25, ease: soft } },
} as const;

FILE: src\pages\BlogPage.tsx

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { usePosts } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const categories = [
  { value: "all", label: "All articles" },
  { value: "guidance", label: "Guidance" },
  { value: "families", label: "For families" },
  { value: "process", label: "The process" },
  { value: "safety", label: "Safety" },
] as const;

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
    day: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export default function BlogPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = usePosts();
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<(typeof categories)[number]["value"]>("all");

  useSeo({
    title: "Guidance blog",
    description:
      "Practical articles on rishta etiquette, family conversations, overseas proposals and avoiding matrimonial fraud.",
    path: "/blog",
  });

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => (data ?? []).filter((post) => category === "all" || post.category === category),
    [data, category],
  );

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Guidance"
        title="Notes for families"
        description="Short, practical articles written by our consultants. No hype, no promises — just what we have learned from the families we work with."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Blog" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <div
          className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5"
          role="group"
          aria-label="Article categories"
        >
          {categories.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.value}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCategory(item.value)}
              aria-pressed={category === item.value}
              className={cn(
                "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
                category === item.value
                  ? "border-maroon-800 bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
                  : "border-khaki-300/70 bg-white text-maroon-800 hover:bg-cream-100",
              )}
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading articles…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-4">
                  <Skeleton className="aspect-video w-full" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-5 w-3/4" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-full" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No articles in this category yet"
              description="New guidance is published every month."
            />
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid list-none gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {filtered.map((post, index) => (
                <li key={post.id}>
                  <article className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10">
                    <Link
                      to={`/blog/${post.slug}`}
                      aria-label={`Read: ${post.title}`}
                      className="block focus-visible:outline-none"
                    >
                      <img
                        src={post.imageUrl}
                        alt=""
                        width={1200}
                        height={675}
                        loading={index < 3 ? "eager" : "lazy"}
                        decoding="async"
                        className="aspect-video w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
                      />
                    </Link>
                    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 p-5">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <Badge variant="secondary">{post.category}</Badge>
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-sans text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                          <Clock className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" /> {post.readingMinutes} min
                          read
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h2 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold leading-snug text-maroon-900">
                        <Link
                          to={`/blog/${post.slug}`}
                          className="hover:underline underline-offset-4"
                        >
                          {post.title}
                        </Link>
                      </h2>
                      <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground">
                        {post.excerpt}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-auto pt-2 font-sans text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                        {post.author} · {formatDate(post.publishedAt)}
                      </p>
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-700">
                        Read article
                        <ArrowRight
                          aria-hidden="true"
                          className="size-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 rtl:rotate-180"
                        />
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\pages\BlogPostPage.tsx
========================================
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowLeft, Clock, Share2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { usePost, usePosts } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export default function BlogPostPage() {
  const { slug = "" } = useParams();
  const { data: post, isLoading, isError, refetch } = usePost(slug);
  const { data: posts } = usePosts();

  useSeo({
    title: post ? post.title : "Article",
    description: post ? post.excerpt : "Guidance from the Ahd-e-Rishta consultants.",
    path: `/blog/${slug}`,
    type: "article",
    image: post?.imageUrl,
    jsonLd: post
      ? {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "Article",
          headline: post.title,
          description: post.excerpt,
          datePublished: post.publishedAt,
          author: { "@type": "Organization", name: post.author },
          image: post.imageUrl,
        }
      : undefined,
  });

  const share = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share({ title: post?.title ?? "Article", url: window.location.href });
        return;
      }
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
      toast.success("Link copied.");
    } catch {
      toast.info("Press and hold the address bar to copy this link.");
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6" aria-busy="true">
        <span className="sr-only">Loading article…</span>
        <Skeleton className="h-5 w-24" />
        <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-10 w-3/4" />
        <Skeleton className="mt-6 aspect-video w-full rounded-3xl" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isError || !post) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6">
        <ErrorState
          title="We could not find that article"
          description="It may have been renamed or removed."
          onRetry={() => void refetch()}
        />
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-center">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link to="/blog">All articles</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const related = (posts ?? []).filter((item) => post.relatedIds.includes(item.id)).slice(0, 2);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow={post.category}
        title={post.title}
        description={post.excerpt}
        breadcrumb={[
          { label: "Home", href: "/" },
          { label: "Blog", href: "/blog" },
          { label: post.title },
        ]}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 font-sans text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          <span>{post.author}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>{formatDate(post.publishedAt)}</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
            <Clock className="size-3.5" aria-hidden="true" /> {post.readingMinutes} min read
          </span>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => void share()}>
            <Share2 aria-hidden="true" /> Share
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <article className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <img
          src={post.imageUrl}
          alt=""
          width={1200}
          height={675}
          loading="eager"
          decoding="async"
          className="aspect-video w-full rounded-3xl border border-border object-cover"
        />

        <div className="mt-8 space-y-8">
          {post.body.map((section) => (
            <section key={section.heading}>
              <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-800 sm:text-2xl">
                {section.heading}
              </h2>
              <div className="mt-2 space-y-3">
                {section.paragraphs.map((paragraph) => (
                  <p
                    key={paragraph.slice(0, 24)}
                    className="max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-base leading-7 text-maroon-900/85"
                  >
                    {paragraph}
                  </p>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-10 flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-3xl bg-cream-100 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-maroon-900/80">
            Have a question about this topic? A consultant will answer it personally.
          </p>
          <Button asChild variant="whatsapp" size="sm">
            <a
              href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                message: `I read your article "${post.title}" and have a question.`,
              })}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            >
              <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask on WhatsApp
            </a>
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8">
          <Link
            to="/blog"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-800 underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="size-4 rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" /> All articles
          </Link>
        </div>
      </article>

      {related.length > 0 ? (
        <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 pb-14 sm:px-6">
          <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-800">Related reading</h2>
          <ul className="mt-4 grid list-none gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {related.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.id}>
                <Link
                  to={`/blog/${item.slug}`}
                  className="flex h-full flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-cream-50"
                >
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="w-fit">
                    {item.category}
                  </Badge>
                  <p className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                    {item.title}
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                    {item.excerpt}
                  </p>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Clock, Mail, MapPin, MessageCircle, PhoneCall } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { ContactForm } from "@/components/forms/ContactForm";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

export default function ContactPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const initialTopic = searchParams.get("topic") ?? undefined;

  useSeo({
    title: "Contact the office",
    description:
      "Speak to the Ahd-e-Rishta office in Lahore. Call, email or message on WhatsApp — Monday to Saturday, 10:00 AM to 7:00 PM.",
    path: "/contact",
    jsonLd: {
      "@context": "https://schema.org",
      "@type": "LocalBusiness",
      name: siteConfig.name,
      telephone: siteConfig.phone,
      email: siteConfig.email,
      address: {
        "@type": "PostalAddress",
        streetAddress: siteConfig.address,
        addressLocality: siteConfig.city,
        addressCountry: "PK",
      },
      openingHours: "Mo-Sa 10:00-19:00",
    },
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Contact"
        title="Speak to the office"
        description="Ask a question, request a callback or report a suspicious profile. A consultant replies within one working day."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Contact" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="lg:col-span-7">
            <ContactForm initialTopic={initialTopic} />

            <div className="mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-cream-100/70 p-5">
              <h2 className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                Reporting a suspicious profile
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1.5 max-w-[62ch] font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-maroon-900/80">
                Send the profile ID and what happened. Please do not forward identity documents. We
                review every report within two working days and remove profiles that fail
                verification.
              </p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Button
                  asChild
                  variant="whatsapp"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "contact_report" })}
                >
                  <a
                    href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                      message: "I want to report a suspicious profile. Profile ID:",
                    })}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  >

              <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                {t("footer.contact")}

                      href={`tel:${siteConfig.phone}`}

========================================
FILE: src\pages\FaqPage.tsx
========================================
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { useFaqs } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionContent,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/accordion";
import { cn, titleCase } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { FaqCategory } from "@/schemas/content.schema";

const categoryLabels: Record<FaqCategory, string> = {
  general: "General",
  profiles: "Profiles",
  packages: "Packages & fees",
  privacy: "Privacy",
  process: "The process",
};

export default function FaqPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useFaqs();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<FaqCategory | "all">("all");

  useSeo({
    title: "Frequently asked questions",
    description:
      "Answers about registration, verification, privacy, photographs, membership plans and the rishta process at Ahd-e-Rishta.",
    path: "/faq",
    jsonLd: data
      ? {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "FAQPage",
          mainEntity: data.map((faq) => ({
            "@type": "Question",
            name: faq.question,
            acceptedAnswer: { "@type": "Answer", text: faq.answer },
          })),
        }
      : undefined,
  });

  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set((data ?? []).map((faq) => faq.category));
    return ["all", ...Array.from(set)] as (FaqCategory | "all")[];
  }, [data]);

  const filtered = (data ?? []).filter((faq) => {
    const matchesCategory = category === "all" || faq.category === category;
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const matchesQuery =
      !needle ||
      faq.question.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
      faq.answer.toLowerCase().includes(needle);
    return matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
  });

  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<FaqCategory, typeof filtered>();
    filtered.forEach((faq) => {
      const list = map.get(faq.category) ?? [];
      list.push(faq);
      map.set(faq.category, list);
    });
    return Array.from(map.entries());
  }, [filtered]);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Help centre"
        title="Frequently asked questions"
        description="Search the answers below, or message the office — a consultant replies personally, usually within one working day."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "FAQ" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-5xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <div className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-4 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5">
          <label htmlFor="faq-search" className="sr-only">
            Search questions
          </label>
          <div className="relative">
            <Search
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute start-4 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-maroon-800/50"
            />
            <input
              id="faq-search"
              type="search"
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search questions, e.g. photographs, refund, verification"
              className="h-11 w-full rounded-full border border-input bg-cream-50 ps-11 pe-4 font-sans text-base text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700/20 focus-visible:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5" role="group" aria-label="Filter by category">
            {categories.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCategory(item)}
                aria-pressed={category === item}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
                  category === item
                    ? "border-maroon-800 bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
                    : "border-khaki-300/70 bg-white text-maroon-800 hover:bg-cream-100",
                )}
              >
                {item === "all" ? "All" : categoryLabels[item]}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading questions…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, index) => (
                <Skeleton key={index} className="h-14 w-full rounded-2xl" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : grouped.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No questions match your search"
              description="Try a different word, or ask us directly — we answer every message."
            >
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery("");
                  setCategory("all");
                }}
              >
                Reset search
              </Button>
              <Button asChild variant="whatsapp">
                <a
                  href={buildWhatsAppUrl({ message: "I could not find my question in the FAQ." })}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                >
                  <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask on WhatsApp
                </a>
              </Button>
            </EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-8">
              {grouped.map(([groupCategory, items]) => (
                <section key={groupCategory} aria-labelledby={`faq-group-${groupCategory}`}>
                  <h2
                    id={`faq-group-${groupCategory}`}
                    className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
                  >
                    {categoryLabels[groupCategory] ?? titleCase(groupCategory)}
                  </h2>
                  <Accordion
                    type="single"
                    collapsible
                    className="mt-3 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white px-5"
                  >
                    {items.map((faq) => (
                      <AccordionItem key={faq.id} value={faq.id}>
                        <AccordionTrigger>{faq.question}</AccordionTrigger>
                        <AccordionContent>{faq.answer}</AccordionContent>
                      </AccordionItem>
                    ))}
                  </Accordion>
                </section>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-start gap-3 rounded-3xl bg-maroon-950 p-6 text-cream-100 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-cream-50">Still unsure?</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-sm text-cream-200/80">
              Message the office — no obligation, no sales script.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button asChild variant="whatsapp">
              <a
                href={buildWhatsAppUrl({ message: "I have a question about the service." })}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
              >
                <WhatsAppIcon /> WhatsApp
              </a>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild variant="secondary">
              <Link to="/contact">Contact form</Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useHeroSlides, useFaqs } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";
import { HeroCarousel } from "@/components/home/HeroCarousel";
import { FindMatchSection } from "@/components/home/FindMatchSection";
import { TrustStrip } from "@/components/home/TrustStrip";
import { ServicesSection } from "@/components/home/ServicesSection";
import { StatsSection } from "@/components/home/StatsSection";
import { BrowseByCitySection } from "@/components/home/BrowseByCitySection";
import { ProposalsTabsSection } from "@/components/home/ProposalsTabsSection";
import { FeaturedProfilesSection } from "@/components/home/FeaturedProfilesSection";
import { HowItWorksSection } from "@/components/home/HowItWorksSection";
import { SecondMarriageSection } from "@/components/home/SecondMarriageSection";
import { MembershipPlansSection } from "@/components/home/MembershipPlansSection";
import { ConsultantsSection } from "@/components/home/ConsultantsSection";
import { FounderMessageSection } from "@/components/home/FounderMessageSection";
import { SafetySection } from "@/components/home/SafetySection";
import { SuccessStoriesSection } from "@/components/home/SuccessStoriesSection";
import { VideosSection } from "@/components/home/VideosSection";
import { WhyChooseUsSection } from "@/components/home/WhyChooseUsSection";
import { LatestProfilesSection } from "@/components/home/LatestProfilesSection";
import { MobileAppSection } from "@/components/home/MobileAppSection";
import { FaqSection } from "@/components/home/FaqSection";
import { NewsletterSection } from "@/components/home/NewsletterSection";
import { CtaSection } from "@/components/home/CtaSection";

export default function HomePage() {
  const hero = useHeroSlides();
  const faqs = useFaqs();

  const jsonLd = useMemo(() => {
    const organization = {
      "@context": "https://schema.org",
      "@type": "Organization",
      name: siteConfig.name,
      alternateName: siteConfig.tagline,
      url: env.siteUrl,
      description: siteConfig.description,
      email: siteConfig.email,
      telephone: siteConfig.phone,
      address: {
        "@type": "PostalAddress",
        streetAddress: siteConfig.address,
        addressLocality: siteConfig.city,
        addressCountry: "PK",
      },
    };

    const website = {
      "@context": "https://schema.org",
      "@type": "WebSite",
      name: siteConfig.name,
      url: env.siteUrl,
      potentialAction: {
        "@type": "SearchAction",
        target: `${env.siteUrl}/profiles?q={search_term_string}`,
        "query-input": "required name=search_term_string",
      },
    };

    const faqPage = faqs.data?.length
      ? {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "FAQPage",
          mainEntity: faqs.data.slice(0, 8).map((faq) => ({
            "@type": "Question",
            name: faq.question,
            acceptedAnswer: { "@type": "Answer", text: faq.answer },
          })),
        }
      : null;

    return [organization, website, ...(faqPage ? [faqPage] : [])];
  }, [faqs.data]);

  useSeo({
    title: `${siteConfig.name} | ${siteConfig.tagline}`,
    description: siteConfig.description,
    path: "/",
    jsonLd,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <HeroCarousel
        slides={hero.data ?? []}
        isLoading={hero.isLoading}
        isError={hero.isError}
        onRetry={() => void hero.refetch()}
      />
      <FindMatchSection />
      <TrustStrip />
      <ServicesSection />
      <StatsSection />
      <BrowseByCitySection />
      <ProposalsTabsSection />
      <FeaturedProfilesSection />
      <HowItWorksSection />
      <SecondMarriageSection />
      <MembershipPlansSection />
      <ConsultantsSection />
      <FounderMessageSection />
      <SafetySection />
      <SuccessStoriesSection />
      <VideosSection />
      <WhyChooseUsSection />
      <LatestProfilesSection />
      <MobileAppSection />
      <FaqSection />
      <NewsletterSection />
      <CtaSection />
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\pages\LegalPage.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { AlertCircle, FileText } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { legalDocuments, type LegalVariant } from "@/lib/legalContent";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";

interface LegalPageProps {
  variant: LegalVariant;
}

export default function LegalPage({ variant }: LegalPageProps) {
  const document = legalDocuments[variant];

  useSeo({
    title: document.title,
    description: document.description,
    path: `/${variant === "refund" ? "refund-policy" : variant}`,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow={document.eyebrow}
        title={document.title}
        description={document.description}
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: document.title }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto grid w-full max-w-6xl gap-8 px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
        <article className="lg:col-span-8">
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-amber-300/60 bg-amber-50 p-4">
            <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 text-amber-700" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-amber-900">
              <span className="font-semibold">Placeholder text.</span> Replace this page with the
              bureau&rsquo;s approved wording before launch. Nothing here should be relied on as
              legal advice.
            </p>
          </div>

          <p className="font-sans text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground">
            {document.updated}
          </p>

          <div className="mt-6 space-y-8">
            {document.sections.map((section) => (
              <section key={section.heading} aria-labelledby={section.heading}>
                <h2
                  id={section.heading}
                  className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900 sm:text-2xl"
                >
                  {section.heading}
                </h2>
                <div className="mt-2 space-y-3">
                  {section.paragraphs.map((paragraph) => (
                    <p
                      key={paragraph.slice(0, 24)}
                      className="max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 text-maroon-900/80"
                    >
                      {paragraph}
                    </p>
                  ))}
                </div>
                {section.bullets ? (
                  <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                    {section.bullets.map((bullet) => (
                      <li
                        key={bullet}
                        className="flex gap-2 font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 text-maroon-900/80"
                      >
                        <span
                          aria-hidden="true"
                          className="mt-2.5 size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-maroon-600"
                        />
                        <span>{bullet}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                ) : null}
              </section>
            ))}
          </div>
        </article>

        <aside className="lg:col-span-4">
          <div className="sticky top-28 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-maroon-900">
              <FileText className="size-4 text-maroon-700" aria-hidden="true" />
              Other documents
            </h2>
            <Separator className="my-3" />
            <ul className="space-y-2">
              {[
                { label: "Terms of service", href: "/terms" },
                { label: "Privacy policy", href: "/privacy" },
                { label: "Refund policy", href: "/refund-policy" },
                { label: "Safety tips", href: "/safety-tips" },
                { label: "FAQ", href: "/faq" },
              ].map((link) => (
                <li key={link.href}>
                  <Link
                    to={link.href}
                    className="block rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5 font-sans text-sm text-maroon-800 transition-colors hover:bg-cream-100"
                  >
                    {link.label}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-cream-100 p-3 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/70">
              Questions about this document? Message the office and a consultant will explain it in
              plain language.
            </p>
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Check, CreditCard, Minus, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { useFaqs, usePlans } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { whatsappForPlan } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionContent,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/accordion";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const paymentMethods = [
  {
    name: "Bank transfer",
    detail: "Ask the office for the IBAN in writing. Always take a receipt.",
  },
  {
    name: "JazzCash",
    detail: "Payment only to the bureau's registered merchant number.",
  },
  {
    name: "Easypaisa",
    detail: "Payment only to the bureau's registered merchant number.",
  },
];

export default function PackagesPage() {
  const { data: plans, isLoading, isError, refetch } = usePlans();
  const { data: faqs } = useFaqs();
  const packageFaqs = (faqs ?? []).filter(
    (faq) => faq.category === "packages" || faq.category === "privacy",
  );

  useSeo({
    title: "Membership plans and fees",
    description:
      "Compare Ahd-e-Rishta membership plans, what each includes, payment methods and refund terms. All prices are placeholders until the bureau confirms its fee schedule.",
    path: "/packages",
  });

  const featureRows = (plans ?? []).reduce<string[]>((rows, plan) => {
    plan.features.forEach((feature) => {
      if (!rows.includes(feature.label)) rows.push(feature.label);
    });
    return rows;
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Membership"
        title="Plans and fees"
        description="Choose the level of guidance your family wants. Prices shown here are placeholders — the office will confirm the current fee schedule in writing before any payment."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Packages" }]}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link to="/register">Register first</Link>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="outline">
            <Link to="/faq">Read the FAQ</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:py-16">
        {isError ? (
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
        ) : isLoading ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
            <span className="sr-only">Loading plans…</span>
            {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6">
                <Skeleton className="h-7 w-28" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-10 w-36" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-6 h-24 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-6 h-11 w-full rounded-full" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid items-stretch gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {(plans ?? []).map((plan) => (
              <article
                key={plan.id}
                className={cn(
                  "relative flex flex-col rounded-3xl border p-6 shadow-sm",
                  plan.highlight
                    ? "border-maroon-800/30 bg-linear-to-b from-maroon-900 to-maroon-950 text-cream-100 shadow-lg shadow-maroon-900/20"
                    : "border-border bg-white shadow-maroon-900/5",
                )}
              >
                {plan.badge ? (
                  <Badge variant="peach" className="absolute -top-3 start-6">
                    {plan.badge}
                  </Badge>
                ) : null}
                <h2
                  className={cn(
                    "font-heading text-2xl font-semibold",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-900",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.name}
                </h2>
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "mt-1 font-sans text-xs",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-200/70" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.audience}
                </p>
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "mt-4 font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-50" : "text-maroon-800",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.priceLabel}
                </p>
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "font-sans text-xs",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-200/60" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.priceNote}
                </p>
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "mt-4 font-sans text-sm leading-7",
                    plan.highlight ? "text-cream-100/85" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  {plan.description}
                </p>

                <ul className="mt-5 space-y-2">
                  {plan.features.map((feature) => (
                    <li
                      key={feature.label}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex items-start gap-2 font-sans text-sm leading-6",
                        feature.included
                          ? plan.highlight
                            ? "text-cream-100/90"
                            : "text-maroon-900/85"
                          : plan.highlight
                            ? "text-cream-200/60"
                            : "text-muted-foreground",
                      )}
                    >
                      {feature.included ? (
                        <Check
                          className={cn(
                            "mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0",
                            plan.highlight ? "text-peach-300" : "text-green-800",
                          )}
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <Minus className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                      {feature.label}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>

                <div className="mt-auto space-y-2 pt-6">
                  <Button
                    asChild
                    variant="whatsapp"
                    className="w-full"
                    onClick={() =>
                      trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "packages_page", plan: plan.name })
                    }
                  >
                    <a href={whatsappForPlan(plan.name)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                      <WhatsAppIcon /> Enquire about {plan.name}
                    </a>
                  </Button>
                  <p
                    className={cn(
                      "text-center font-sans text-[11px]",
                      plan.highlight ? "text-cream-200/60" : "text-muted-foreground",
                    )}
                  >
                    No payment is taken through this website.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section id="compare" className="scroll-mt-28 bg-cream-100">
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-14 sm:px-6">
          <SectionHeading
            align="start"
            eyebrow="Side by side"
            title="Compare plans"
            description="Scroll the table sideways on a small screen. Every plan includes the same verification standard."
          />

          <div className="mt-8 overflow-x-auto rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white">
            <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] border-collapse text-start">
              <caption className="sr-only">Comparison of membership plan features</caption>
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-border bg-cream-50">
                  <th
                    scope="col"
                    className="p-4 text-start font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground"
                  >
                    Feature
                  </th>
                  {(plans ?? []).map((plan) => (
                    <th
                      key={plan.id}
                      scope="col"
                      className="p-4 text-center font-heading text-base font-semibold text-maroon-900"
                    >
                      {plan.name}
                      <span className="mt-0.5 block font-sans text-[11px] font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                        {plan.priceLabel}
                      </span>
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {featureRows.map((row) => (
                  <tr key={row} className="border-b border-border/70 last:border-0">
                    <th
                      scope="row"
                      className="p-4 text-start font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900"
                    >
                      {row}
                    </th>
                    {(plans ?? []).map((plan) => {
                      const feature = plan.features.find((item) => item.label === row);
                      return (
                        <td key={plan.id} className="p-4 text-center">
                          {feature?.included ? (
                            <>
                              <Check className="mx-auto size-4 text-green-800" aria-hidden="true" />
                              <span className="sr-only">Included</span>
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <Minus
                                className="mx-auto size-4 text-muted-foreground/50"
                                aria-hidden="true"
                              />
                              <span className="sr-only">Not included</span>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-14 sm:px-6">
        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900">
              <CreditCard className="size-5 text-maroon-700" aria-hidden="true" />
              Payment methods
            </h2>
            <ul className="mt-3 space-y-3">
              {paymentMethods.map((method) => (
                <li key={method.name} className="rounded-2xl bg-cream-100/70 p-3">
                  <p className="font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-900">{method.name}</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                    {method.detail}
                  </p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <p className="mt-3 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/70">
              We never accept payment to a personal mobile number, and we never ask for a top-up or
              gift card. If someone does, report it to the office immediately.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-3xl border border-amber-300/60 bg-amber-50 p-6">
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-amber-900">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />
              Refund note
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-2 font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-amber-900/90">
              This is a placeholder. The bureau&rsquo;s approved refund policy must be published
              here before any payment is accepted.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-amber-900/90">
              Ask for the written policy, including any administration charges, before you transfer
              money.
            </p>
            <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm" className="mt-3">
              <Link to="/refund-policy">Read the refund policy page</Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-10">
          <SectionHeading align="start" eyebrow="Questions" title="Plan questions" />
          <Accordion
            type="single"
            collapsible
            className="mt-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white px-5"
          >
            {packageFaqs.map((faq) => (
              <AccordionItem key={faq.id} value={faq.id}>
                <AccordionTrigger>{faq.question}</AccordionTrigger>
                <AccordionContent>{faq.answer}</AccordionContent>
              </AccordionItem>
            ))}
          </Accordion>
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\pages\ProfileDetailPage.tsx
