import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  BadgeCheck,
  Briefcase,
  Cake,
  GraduationCap,
  HeartHandshake,
  Info,
  Languages,
  MapPin,
  Printer,
  Ruler,
  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useProfile, useSimilarProfiles } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { whatsappForProfile } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { formatHeight, genderLabel, maritalLabel } from "@/schemas/profile.schema";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { ProfilePhoto } from "@/components/profiles/ProfilePhoto";
import { ProfileCarousel } from "@/components/profiles/ProfileCarousel";
import { ShortlistButton } from "@/components/profiles/ShortlistButton";
import { ShareButton } from "@/components/profiles/ShareButton";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

interface FactProps {
  icon: typeof Cake;
  label: string;
  value: string;
}

function Fact({ icon: Icon, label, value }: FactProps) {
  return (
    <li className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-3.5">
      <span className="grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-cream-100 text-maroon-700">
        <Icon className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
          {label}
        </p>
        <p className="mt-0.5 font-sans text-sm font-medium text-maroon-900">{value}</p>
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

function DetailSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6" aria-busy="true">
      <span className="sr-only">Loading profile…</span>
      <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-12">
        <div className="lg:col-span-4">
          <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full rounded-3xl" />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-4 lg:col-span-8">
          <Skeleton className="h-8 w-2/3" />
          <Skeleton className="h-4 w-1/3" />
          <Skeleton className="h-28 w-full rounded-3xl" />
          <Skeleton className="h-40 w-full rounded-3xl" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ProfileDetailPage() {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();
  const { data: profile, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useProfile(id);
  const similar = useSimilarProfiles(profile);

  const profileJsonLd = useMemo(() => {
    if (!profile) return undefined;
    // No personal data in structured data: no names, no birth dates, no contact details.
    return {
      "@context": "https://schema.org",
      "@type": "WebPage",
      name: `Rishta proposal ${profile.id}`,
      description: `A bureau-verified matrimonial proposal (${profile.id}) listed by Ahd-e-Rishta. Contact is handled by the office.`,
      url: `${env.siteUrl}/profiles/${profile.id}`,
      isPartOf: { "@type": "WebSite", name: "Ahd-e-Rishta", url: env.siteUrl },
    };
  }, [profile]);

  useSeo({
    title: profile ? `Proposal ${profile.id} — verified rishta profile` : "Proposal",
    description: profile
      ? `Bureau-verified matrimonial proposal ${profile.id}. ${genderLabel(profile.gender)}, ${profile.age} years, ${profile.city}. Contact only through the Ahd-e-Rishta office.`
      : "Verified rishta proposal listed by Ahd-e-Rishta.",
    path: `/profiles/${id}`,
    noIndex: true,
    jsonLd: profileJsonLd,
  });

  if (isLoading) return <DetailSkeleton />;

  if (isError || !profile) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6">
        <ErrorState
          title="This profile is no longer listed"
          description="It may have been matched, paused or removed at the family's request. Our consultants can suggest similar proposals."
          onRetry={() => void refetch()}
        />
        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link to="/profiles">Browse all proposals</Link>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="whatsapp">
            <a
              href={whatsappForProfile(id, "")}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "profile_error" })}
            >
              <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask about {id}
            </a>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const facts: FactProps[] = [
    { icon: Cake, label: "Age", value: `${profile.age} years` },
    { icon: Ruler, label: "Height", value: formatHeight(profile.heightCm) },
    { icon: HeartHandshake, label: "Marital status", value: maritalLabel(profile.maritalStatus) },
    {
      icon: Users,
      label: "Family type",
      value: profile.familyType === "joint" ? "Joint family" : "Nuclear family",
    },
    { icon: GraduationCap, label: "Education", value: profile.education },
    { icon: Briefcase, label: "Occupation", value: profile.occupation },
    { icon: MapPin, label: "Location", value: `${profile.city}, ${profile.country}` },
    { icon: Languages, label: "Languages", value: profile.languages.join(", ") },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow={`Proposal ${profile.id}`}
        title={profile.displayName}
        description={`${genderLabel(profile.gender)} · ${profile.age} years · ${profile.city}, ${profile.country}`}
        breadcrumb={[
          { label: "Home", href: "/" },
          { label: "Find a match", href: "/profiles" },
          { label: profile.id },
        ]}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2" data-print-hide>
          <Button
            asChild
            variant="whatsapp"
            onClick={() =>
              trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "profile_detail", profileId: profile.id })
            }
          >
            <a
              href={whatsappForProfile(profile.id, profile.displayName)}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            >
              <WhatsAppIcon /> Enquire about {profile.id}
            </a>
          </Button>
          <ShortlistButton profileId={profile.id} size="md" withLabel className="bg-white/90" />
          <ShareButton title={`${profile.displayName} (${profile.id})`} className="size-11" />
          <Button variant="secondary" size="default" onClick={() => window.print()}>
            <Printer aria-hidden="true" /> Print
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="lg:col-span-4">
            <div className="sticky top-28 space-y-4">
              <ProfilePhoto
                profile={profile}
                priority
                className="aspect-4/5 w-full rounded-3xl border border-border"
              />
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                <Badge variant="secondary" className="gap-1">
                  <BadgeCheck className="size-3.5 text-green-800" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {profile.verified ? "Office verified" : "Verification in progress"}
                </Badge>
                {profile.packageName ? (
                  <Badge variant="peach">{profile.packageName} plan</Badge>
                ) : null}
                <Badge variant="outline">ID {profile.id}</Badge>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-cream-100/70 p-4" data-print-hide>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                  <ShieldCheck className="size-4 text-green-800" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Contact through the office
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1.5 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-maroon-900/75">
                  We never publish phone numbers, addresses or full names. Message the office with
                  this profile ID and a consultant will arrange a respectful introduction.
                </p>
                <Button asChild variant="whatsapp" size="sm" className="mt-3 w-full">
                  <a
                    href={whatsappForProfile(profile.id, profile.displayName)}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  >
                    <WhatsAppIcon /> Message the office
                  </a>
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-8">
            <section aria-labelledby="about-heading">
              <h2
                id="about-heading"
                className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
              >
                About this proposal
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-[15px] leading-7 text-maroon-900/85">
                {profile.about}
              </p>
            </section>

            <Separator className="my-7" />

            <section aria-labelledby="facts-heading">
              <h2
                id="facts-heading"
                className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
              >
                Key details
              </h2>
              <ul className="mt-4 grid list-none gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {facts.map((fact) => (
                  <Fact key={fact.label} {...fact} />
                ))}
                <Fact
                  icon={Users}
                  label="Children"
                  value={
                    profile.maritalStatus === "never-married"
                      ? "Not applicable"
                      : profile.hasChildren
                        ? "Has children from a previous marriage"
                        : "No children"
                  }
                />
                <Fact icon={Info} label="Monthly income" value={profile.income} />
                <Fact
                  icon={ShieldCheck}
                  label="Religion & sect"
                  value={`${profile.religion} · ${profile.sect}`}
                />
                <Fact icon={Users} label="Caste / biradari" value={profile.caste} />
              </ul>
            </section>

            <Separator className="my-7" />

            <section
              aria-labelledby="demands-heading"
              className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 sm:p-6"
            >
              <h2
                id="demands-heading"
                className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
              >
                What the family is looking for
              </h2>
              <dl className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {[
                  ["Age preference", profile.partnerDemands.ageRange],
                  ["Height preference", profile.partnerDemands.heightRange],
                  ["Marital status", profile.partnerDemands.maritalStatus],
                  ["Education", profile.partnerDemands.education],
                  ["Occupation", profile.partnerDemands.occupation],
                  ["Location", profile.partnerDemands.city],
                ].map(([label, value]) => (
                  <div key={label} className="rounded-2xl bg-cream-100/60 p-3">
                    <dt className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground">
                      {label}
                    </dt>
                    <dd className="mt-0.5 font-sans text-sm text-maroon-900">{value}</dd>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </dl>
              <p className="mt-4 max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
                {profile.partnerDemands.notes}
              </p>
            </section>

            <div className="mt-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-amber-300/60 bg-amber-50 p-4">
              <Info className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-amber-700" aria-hidden="true" />
              <p className="font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-amber-900">
                This listing is prepared by the bureau from information supplied by the family. Our
                team checks identity, phone and family references before sharing, but families
                should still meet in person with a mahram before agreeing to anything.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-8" data-print-hide>
              <Button asChild variant="whatsapp" size="lg" className="w-full sm:w-auto">
                <a
                  href={whatsappForProfile(profile.id, profile.displayName)}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  onClick={() =>
                    trackEvent("whatsapp_click", {
                      source: "profile_detail_footer",
                      profileId: profile.id,
                    })
                  }
                >
                  <WhatsAppIcon /> Enquire about {profile.id}
                </a>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <section className="bg-cream-100" data-print-hide>
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-14 sm:px-6">
          <SectionHeading
            align="start"
            eyebrow="You may also consider"
            title="Similar proposals"
            description="Selected by the office based on city, caste and preferences."
          />
          <div className="mt-8">
            <ProfileCarousel
              ariaLabel="Similar proposals"
              profiles={similar.data}
              isLoading={similar.isLoading}
              isError={similar.isError}
              onRetry={() => void similar.refetch()}
              viewAllHref={
                profile.gender === "male" ? "/profiles?gender=male" : "/profiles?gender=female"
              }
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}


========================================
FILE: src\pages\ProfilesPage.tsx
========================================
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { LayoutGrid, List, SlidersHorizontal, X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useProfileFilters } from "@/hooks/useProfileFilters";
import { useProfiles } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { buildFilterChips } from "@/lib/filterChips";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { formatCount } from "@/lib/utils";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { ProfileCard } from "@/components/profiles/ProfileCard";
import { FilterPanel } from "@/components/profiles/FilterPanel";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Pagination } from "@/components/ui/pagination";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetTrigger, SheetClose } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Newest first", value: "newest" },
  { label: "Age: youngest first", value: "age-asc" },
  { label: "Age: oldest first", value: "age-desc" },
  { label: "Height: shortest first", value: "height-asc" },
  { label: "Height: tallest first", value: "height-desc" },
];

export default function ProfilesPage() {
  const { filters, setFilters, removeFilter, clearAll, activeCount } = useProfileFilters();
  const { data, isLoading, isError, isFetching, refetch } = useProfiles(filters);
  const [sheetOpen, setSheetOpen] = useState(false);

  const chips = useMemo(() => buildFilterChips(filters), [filters]);
  const view = filters.view ?? "grid";

  useSeo({
    title: "Find a match — verified rishta proposals",
    description:
      "Browse bureau-verified rishta proposals across Pakistan and overseas. Filter by city, caste, education, age and marital status. Contact only through the office.",
    path: "/profiles",
    noIndex: true,
  });

  const handleChange = (patch: Record<string, string | number | undefined>) => {
    trackEvent("filter_change", { ...patch });
    setFilters(patch);
  };

  const resultLabel =
    data === undefined
      ? "Loading proposals…"
      : `${formatCount(data.total)} ${data.total === 1 ? "proposal" : "proposals"}`;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Find a match"
        title="Browse verified proposals"
        description="Every profile below has been reviewed by our team. Contact details and photographs are shared only through the office, with the family's consent."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Find a match" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:py-12">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-12">
          {/* Desktop sidebar */}
          <aside aria-label="Profile filters" className="hidden lg:col-span-3 lg:block">
            <div className="sticky top-28 max-h-[calc(100dvh-8rem)] overflow-y-auto scroll-area rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5">
              <FilterPanel
                filters={filters}
                onChange={handleChange}
                onClearAll={clearAll}
                activeCount={activeCount}
              />
            </div>
          </aside>

          <div className="lg:col-span-9">
            {/* Toolbar */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-3.5 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Sheet open={sheetOpen} onOpenChange={setSheetOpen}>
                  <SheetTrigger asChild>
                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="lg:hidden">
                      <SlidersHorizontal aria-hidden="true" />
                      Filters
                      {activeCount > 0 ? (
                        <span className="grid size-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-maroon-800 text-[11px] font-bold text-cream-50">
                          {activeCount}
                        </span>
                      ) : null}
                    </Button>
                  </SheetTrigger>
                  <SheetContent side="start" title="Profile filters" className="p-0">
                    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
                      <div className="border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
                        <p className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                          Filters
                        </p>
                        <p className="font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">{resultLabel}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto scroll-area px-5 py-5">
                        <FilterPanel
                          filters={filters}
                          onChange={handleChange}
                          onClearAll={clearAll}
                          activeCount={activeCount}
                          showTitle={false}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div className="border-t border-border p-4">
                        <SheetClose asChild>
                          <Button className="w-full">
                            Show {data ? formatCount(data.total) : ""} results
                          </Button>
                        </SheetClose>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </SheetContent>
                </Sheet>

                <p aria-live="polite" className="font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                  {resultLabel}
                </p>
                {isFetching && !isLoading ? (
                  <span className="font-sans text-xs text-muted-foreground">Updating…</span>
                ) : null}
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="relative">
                  <label htmlFor="sort" className="sr-only">
                    Sort results
                  </label>
                  <select
                    id="sort"
                    value={filters.sort ?? "newest"}
                    onChange={(event) => handleChange({ sort: event.target.value })}
                    className="h-9 rounded-full border border-input bg-white ps-3 pe-8 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold text-maroon-900 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700/20 focus-visible:outline-none"
                  >
                    {SORT_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
                      <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                        {option.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-input p-0.5"
                  role="group"
                  aria-label="View mode"
                >
                  {(
                    [
                      { value: "grid", label: "Grid view", Icon: LayoutGrid },
                      { value: "list", label: "List view", Icon: List },
                    ] as const
                  ).map((option) => (
                    <button
                      key={option.value}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setFilters({ view: option.value })}
                      aria-pressed={view === option.value}
                      aria-label={option.label}
                      className={cn(
                        "grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none",
                        view === option.value
                          ? "bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
                          : "text-maroon-800/70 hover:bg-cream-100",
                      )}
                    >
                      <option.Icon className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Active filter chips */}
            {chips.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
                  Active
                </span>
                {chips.map((chip) => (
                  <button
                    key={`${chip.key}-${chip.label}`}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => removeFilter(chip.key)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-maroon-200 bg-maroon-50 px-3 py-1 font-sans text-xs font-semibold text-maroon-900 transition-colors hover:bg-maroon-100 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none"
                  >
                    {chip.label}
                    <X className="size-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span className="sr-only">Remove filter</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={clearAll} className="text-maroon-700">
                  Clear all
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : null}

            {/* Results */}
            <div className="mt-6">
              {isError ? (
                <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
              ) : isLoading ? (
                <div
                  className={cn(
                    "grid gap-4",
                    view === "grid" ? "sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3" : "grid-cols-1",
                  )}
                  aria-busy="true"
                >
                  <span className="sr-only">Loading proposals…</span>
                  {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                    <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-3">
                      <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full" />
                      <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-5 w-2/3" />
                      <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-1/2" />
                      <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-9 w-full rounded-full" />
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                  title="No proposals match these filters"
                  description="Try widening the age or height range, or removing a city filter. Our consultants may also have suitable proposals that are not published online."
                >
                  <Button onClick={clearAll} variant="secondary">
                    Clear filters
                  </Button>
                  <Button asChild>
                    <Link to="/register">Register a proposal</Link>
                  </Button>
                  <Button asChild variant="whatsapp">
                    <a
                      href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                        message: "I could not find a match on the website. Can you help?",
                      })}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      onClick={() =>
                        trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "profiles_empty_state" })
                      }
                    >
                      <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask a consultant
                    </a>
                  </Button>
                </EmptyState>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <ul
                    className={cn(
                      "grid list-none gap-4",
                      view === "grid" ? "sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3" : "grid-cols-1",
                    )}
                  >
                    {data.items.map((profile, index) => (
                      <li key={profile.id}>
                        <ProfileCard
                          profile={profile}
                          layout={view === "list" ? "list" : "grid"}
                          priority={index < 3}
                          className="h-full"
                        />
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>

                  <Pagination
                    className="mt-8"
                    page={data.page}
                    totalPages={data.totalPages}
                    onPageChange={(page) => {
                      setFilters({ page });
                      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
                    }}
                  />
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-10 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-cream-100/70 p-5">
              <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-maroon-900/80">
                <span className="font-semibold">A note on privacy:</span> profiles show a first name
                with a last initial and a profile ID. Phone numbers and addresses are never
                published — every enquiry goes through the office, so both families are protected.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\pages\RegisterPage.tsx

import { CheckCircle2, Clock3, Lock, MessageCircle, PhoneCall, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { RegisterForm } from "@/components/forms/RegisterForm";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

const benefits = [
  "A named consultant for your family — one person to call.",
  "Your details are never published. Photos only with written consent.",
  "Identity, phone and family references checked before any introduction.",
  "The first consultation is free, with no obligation to continue.",
];

export default function RegisterPage() {

        eyebrow="Registration"

                Privacy promise
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1.5 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-cream-200/80">
                Full names, phone numbers and addresses are never published. Contact happens only
                through the office.
              </p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
                <a
                  href={buildWhatsAppUrl({ message: "I would like help registering a proposal." })}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"

                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-green-800 px-4 py-2 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-green-900"

                Prefer to talk first?
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 font-sans text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                Many families call before filling anything in. Our consultants are happy to explain

              <a
                href={buildWhatsAppUrl({ message: "Please explain the registration process." })}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"

                <MessageCircle className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" /> Ask a question

          </aside>

          <div className="min-w-0 lg:col-span-8">

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, PhoneCall, ShieldCheck, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { safetyTips } from "@/lib/homeContent";
import { siteConfig } from "@/lib/site";

import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { SectionHeading } from "@/components/common/SectionHeading";

import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

const redFlags = [
  "Asking for money, mobile top-ups or gift cards before any meeting.",
  "Refusing to meet with a mahram or insisting on meeting alone.",
  "Requests for private photographs, CNIC scans or bank details early on.",
  "Pressure to decide within days, or threats if you slow down.",
  "A profile that cannot be verified by the bureau when you ask.",
  "Contact from a personal number that does not match the office.",
];

const neverAsks = [
  "Your bank account, card details or OTP codes.",
  "Payment to an individual's mobile number instead of the office.",
  "Private photographs, or photographs without consent.",
  "A decision on the first meeting.",
  "Your CNIC or passport scan before both families have met.",
  "Anything at all through a personal social media account.",
];

const reportSteps = [
  {
    title: "Stop the conversation",
    description: "Do not send more messages, documents or money. Take screenshots first.",
  },
  {
    title: "Note the profile ID",
    description: "Every listing has an ID such as AER-1042. Share it with the office.",
  },
  {
    title: "Contact the office directly",
    description:
      "Use the official number published on this site. Do not use a number the other party gave you.",
  },
  {
    title: "We review within two working days",
    description: "Profiles that fail verification are removed and, where relevant, reported.",
  },
];

export default function SafetyTipsPage() {
  useSeo({
    title: "Safety tips and fraud prevention",
    description:
      "How to avoid fake matchmakers: warning signs, what a genuine bureau will never ask for, and how to report a suspicious profile to Ahd-e-Rishta.",
    path: "/safety-tips",
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Safety"

            asChild

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { useService, useServices } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { whatsappForService } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { DynamicIcon } from "@/components/common/DynamicIcon";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { CtaSection } from "@/components/home/CtaSection";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionContent,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/accordion";

export default function ServiceDetailPage() {
  const { slug = "" } = useParams();
  const { data: service, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useService(slug);
  const { data: allServices } = useServices();

  useSeo({
    title: service ? service.title : "Service",
    description: service ? service.shortDescription : "A bureau-managed rishta service.",
    path: `/services/${slug}`,
    jsonLd: service
      ? {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "Service",
          name: service.title,
          description: service.shortDescription,
          provider: { "@type": "Organization", name: "Ahd-e-Rishta" },
          areaServed: "Pakistan",
        }
      : undefined,
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-5xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6" aria-busy="true">
        <span className="sr-only">Loading service…</span>
        <Skeleton className="h-6 w-32" />
        <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-12 w-2/3" />
        <Skeleton className="mt-6 h-40 w-full rounded-3xl" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isError || !service) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 sm:px-6">
        <ErrorState
          title="We could not find that service"
          description="It may have been renamed. Browse all services instead."
          onRetry={() => void refetch()}
        />
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-center">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link to="/services">All services</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const related = (allServices ?? []).filter((item) => item.slug !== service.slug).slice(0, 3);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Service"
        title={service.title}
        description={service.shortDescription}
        breadcrumb={[
          { label: "Home", href: "/" },
          { label: "Services", href: "/services" },
          { label: service.title },
        ]}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            asChild
            variant="whatsapp"
            onClick={() =>
              trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "service_hero", service: service.title })
            }
          >
            <a href={whatsappForService(service.title)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
              <WhatsAppIcon /> Ask about this service
            </a>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="outline">
            <Link to="/register">Register a proposal</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:py-16">
        <div className="grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="lg:col-span-8">
            <span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-cream-100 text-maroon-800">
              <DynamicIcon name={service.icon} className="size-5" />
            </span>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-[65ch] font-sans text-base leading-7 text-maroon-900/85">
              {service.intro}
            </p>

            <section aria-labelledby="benefits-heading" className="mt-10">
              <h2
                id="benefits-heading"
                className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
              >
                What you can expect
              </h2>
              <ul className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {service.benefits.map((benefit) => (
                  <li
                    key={benefit}
                    className="flex gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-3.5"
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2
                      className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-green-800"
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    />
                    <span className="font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-maroon-900/85">
                      {benefit}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>

            <section aria-labelledby="process-heading" className="mt-10">
              <h2
                id="process-heading"
                className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-maroon-800"
              >
                How the process works
              </h2>
              <ol className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                {service.process.map((step, index) => (
                  <li
                    key={step.title}
                    className="flex gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-4"
                  >
                    <span className="grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-maroon-800 font-sans text-xs font-bold text-cream-50">
                      {index + 1}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { useServices } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { DynamicIcon } from "@/components/common/DynamicIcon";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { ScrollReveal, RevealItem } from "@/components/common/ScrollReveal";
import { CtaSection } from "@/components/home/CtaSection";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";

export default function ServicesPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useServices();

  useSeo({
    title: "Our rishta services",
    description:
      "Six bureau-managed matrimonial services: rishta matching, personal matchmaking, second marriage, overseas proposals, marriage counselling and face-to-face family meetings.",
    path: "/services",
    jsonLd: data
      ? {
          "@context": "https://schema.org",
          "@type": "ItemList",
          itemListElement: data.map((service, index) => ({
            "@type": "ListItem",
            position: index + 1,
            name: service.title,
            description: service.shortDescription,
          })),
        }
      : undefined,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Services"
        title="Guidance for every stage of the search"
        description="Whether you are beginning the search, returning to it after a loss, or coordinating between two countries, one of these services will fit your family."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Services" }]}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button asChild>
            <Link to="/register">Register a proposal</Link>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="outline">
            <Link to="/packages">See membership plans</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PageHero>

      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:py-16">
        {isError ? (
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
        ) : isLoading ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
            <span className="sr-only">Loading services…</span>
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6">
                <Skeleton className="size-12 rounded-2xl" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-6 w-2/3" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-4 w-5/6" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ScrollReveal stagger className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {(data ?? []).map((service) => (
              <RevealItem key={service.id} className="h-full">
                <Link
                  to={`/services/${service.slug}`}
                  className="group flex h-full flex-col gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-maroon-200 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-maroon-900/10 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none"
                >
                  <span className="grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-linear-to-br from-cream-100 to-peach-100 text-maroon-800 transition-colors group-hover:from-maroon-800 group-hover:to-maroon-900 group-hover:text-cream-50">
                    <DynamicIcon name={service.icon} className="size-5" />
                  </span>
                  <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                    {service.title}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
                    {service.shortDescription}
                  </p>
                  <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {service.suitedFor.slice(0, 2).map((item) => (
                      <li
                        key={item}
                        className="rounded-full bg-cream-100 px-2.5 py-1 font-sans text-[11px] font-medium text-maroon-800"
                      >
                        {item}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                  <span className="mt-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-2 font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-700">

            ))}

        )}

FILE: src\pages\ShortlistPage.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Heart, LayoutGrid, List, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useShortlist } from "@/hooks/useShortlist";
import { useProfilesByIds } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { buildWhatsAppUrl } from "@/lib/whatsapp";
import { trackEvent } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { ProfileCard } from "@/components/profiles/ProfileCard";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { WhatsAppIcon } from "@/components/common/WhatsAppIcon";

export default function ShortlistPage() {
  const { ids, clear } = useShortlist();
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useProfilesByIds(ids);
  const [view, setView] = useState<"grid" | "list">("grid");

  useSeo({
    title: "My shortlist",
    description: "Profiles you have saved while browsing Ahd-e-Rishta.",
    path: "/shortlist",
    noIndex: true,
  });

  const handleClear = () => {
    clear();
    trackEvent("shortlist_toggle", { action: "clear_all" });
    toast.success("Shortlist cleared.");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Saved profiles"
        title="My shortlist"
        description="Saved on this device only — nothing is sent to the office unless you message us. Share the profile IDs with your consultant to discuss them."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Shortlist" }]}
      >
        {ids.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Button asChild variant="whatsapp">
              <a
                href={buildWhatsAppUrl({
                  message: `I would like to discuss these profiles from my shortlist: ${ids.join(", ")}`,
                })}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                onClick={() => trackEvent("whatsapp_click", { source: "shortlist_page" })}
              >
                <WhatsAppIcon /> Discuss my shortlist
              </a>
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleClear}>
              <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" /> Clear shortlist
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </PageHero>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        {ids.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            title="Your shortlist is empty"
            description="Tap the heart on any profile card to save it here. Your shortlist stays on this device."
            icon={<Heart className="size-5" aria-hidden="true" />}
          >
            <Button asChild>
              <Link to="/profiles">Browse proposals</Link>
            </Button>
            <Button asChild variant="outline">
              <Link to="/register">Register a proposal</Link>
            </Button>
          </EmptyState>
        ) : isError ? (
          <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
        ) : isLoading ? (
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
            <span className="sr-only">Loading saved profiles…</span>
            {Array.from({ length: Math.min(ids.length, 6) }).map((_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-3">
                <Skeleton className="aspect-4/5 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="mt-3 h-5 w-2/3" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <p aria-live="polite" className="font-sans text-sm font-semibold text-maroon-900">
                {data?.length ?? 0} saved {data?.length === 1 ? "profile" : "profiles"}
              </p>
              <div
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-input p-0.5"
                role="group"
                aria-label="View mode"
              >
                {(
                  [
                    { value: "grid", label: "Grid view", Icon: LayoutGrid },
                    { value: "list", label: "List view", Icon: List },
                  ] as const
                ).map((option) => (
                  <button
                    key={option.value}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setView(option.value)}
                    aria-pressed={view === option.value}
                    aria-label={option.label}
                    className={cn(
                      "grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none",
                      view === option.value
                        ? "bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
                        : "text-maroon-800/70 hover:bg-cream-100",
                    )}
                  >
                    <option.Icon className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <ul
              className={cn(
                "grid list-none gap-4",
                view === "grid" ? "sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" : "grid-cols-1",
              )}
            >
              {(data ?? []).map((profile) => (
                <li key={profile.id}>
                  <ProfileCard
                    profile={profile}
                    layout={view === "list" ? "list" : "grid"}
                    className="h-full"
                  />
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>

            {data && data.length < ids.length ? (
              <p className="mt-4 font-sans text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Some saved profiles ({ids.length - data.length}) are no longer listed. They may have
                been matched or paused by the family.
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\pages\SuccessStoriesPage.tsx

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useSuccessStories } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { StoryCard } from "@/components/common/StoryCard";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export default function SuccessStoriesPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useSuccessStories();
  const [city, setCity] = useState("all");
  const [year, setYear] = useState("all");

  useSeo({
    title: "Success stories",
    description:
      "Sample stories from families who used the Ahd-e-Rishta service. Real stories are published only with the written consent of both families.",
    path: "/success-stories",
  });

  const cities = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set((data ?? []).map((story) => story.city));
    return ["all", ...Array.from(set).sort()];
  }, [data]);

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set((data ?? []).map((story) => story.year));
    return [
      "all",
      ...Array.from(set)
        .sort((a, b) => Number(b) - Number(a))
        .map(String),
    ];
  }, [data]);

  const filtered = (data ?? []).filter(
    (story) =>
      (city === "all" || story.city === city) && (year === "all" || String(story.year) === year),
  );

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Success stories"
        title="Families we have served"
        description="These are sample stories used to show the layout. Real stories will appear only when both families give written consent — and their names will still never be published."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Success stories" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-4 shadow-sm shadow-maroon-900/5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="story-city"
                className="font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground"
              >
                City
              </label>
              <select
                id="story-city"
                value={city}
                onChange={(event) => setCity(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 block h-10 rounded-full border border-input bg-white px-3 pe-8 font-sans text-sm text-maroon-900 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none"
              >
                {cities.map((option) => (
                  <option key={option} value={option}>
                    {option === "all" ? "All cities" : option}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="story-year"
                className="font-sans text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground"
              >
                Year
              </label>
              <select
                id="story-year"
                value={year}
                onChange={(event) => setYear(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 block h-10 rounded-full border border-input bg-white px-3 pe-8 font-sans text-sm text-maroon-900 focus-visible:border-maroon-700 focus-visible:outline-none"
              >
                {years.map((option) => (
                  <option key={option} value={option}>
                    {option === "all" ? "Any year" : option}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p aria-live="polite" className={cn("font-sans text-sm text-muted-foreground")}>
            {filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? "story" : "stories"}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading stories…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-6">
                  <Skeleton className="h-5 w-6" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-16 w-full" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-4 w-1/2" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No stories for this filter yet"
              description="Try another city or year. New stories are added after both families give consent."
            >
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                onClick={() => {
                  setCity("all");
                  setYear("all");
                }}
              >
                Reset filters
              </Button>
            </EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid list-none gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {filtered.map((story) => (
                <li key={story.id} id={story.id}>
                  <StoryCard story={story} className="h-full" />
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-amber-300/60 bg-amber-50 p-5">
          <p className="font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-amber-900">
            <span className="font-semibold">A note on honesty:</span> we do not publish fake
            testimonials, celebrity photographs or invented success rates. Everything on this page
            is visibly marked as a sample until the bureau supplies consented stories.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useVideos } from "@/hooks/useApiQueries";
import { useSeo } from "@/hooks/useSeo";
import { PageHero } from "@/components/common/PageHero";
import { VideoCard } from "@/components/common/VideoDialog";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState } from "@/components/common/DataState";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const categories = [
  { value: "all", label: "All videos" },
  { value: "guidance", label: "Guidance" },
  { value: "families", label: "For families" },
  { value: "bureau", label: "Our bureau" },
] as const;

export default function VideosPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useVideos();
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<(typeof categories)[number]["value"]>("all");

  useSeo({
    title: "Videos and guidance",
    description:
      "Short videos on preparing a rishta profile, meeting a family and staying safe. Playback starts only when you press play.",
    path: "/videos",
  });

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => (data ?? []).filter((video) => category === "all" || video.category === category),
    [data, category],
  );

  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        eyebrow="Watch"
        title="Videos and guidance"
        description="Practical advice from our consultants. Nothing loads from YouTube until you press play, and we use the privacy-enhanced player."
        breadcrumb={[{ label: "Home", href: "/" }, { label: "Videos" }]}
      />

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:py-14">
        <div
          className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5"
          role="group"
          aria-label="Video categories"
        >
          {categories.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.value}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCategory(item.value)}
              aria-pressed={category === item.value}
              className={cn(
                "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 font-sans text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-maroon-700 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none",
                category === item.value
                  ? "border-maroon-800 bg-maroon-800 text-cream-50"
                  : "border-khaki-300/70 bg-white text-maroon-800 hover:bg-cream-100",
              )}
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          {isError ? (
            <ErrorState onRetry={() => void refetch()} />
          ) : isLoading ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3" aria-busy="true">
              <span className="sr-only">Loading videos…</span>
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="rounded-3xl border border-border bg-white p-4">
                  <Skeleton className="aspect-video w-full" />
                  <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-5 w-3/4" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="No videos in this category yet"
              description="New videos are added each month."
            />
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid list-none gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {filtered.map((video, index) => (
                <li key={video.id}>
                  <VideoCard video={video} priority={index < 3} />
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>

        <p className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-border bg-cream-100/70 p-5 font-sans text-sm leading-7 text-maroon-900/80">
          Prefer reading? Our{" "}
          <a href="/blog" className="font-semibold underline underline-offset-4">
            guidance blog
          </a>{" "}
          covers the same topics in written form, and the{" "}
          <a href="/faq" className="font-semibold underline underline-offset-4">
            FAQ
          </a>{" "}
          answers the most common questions.
        </p>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\schemas\common.schema.ts

import { z } from "zod";

export const paginationSchema = z.object({
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  pageSize: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(48).default(12),
});

export interface Paginated<T> {
  items: T[];
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  total: number;
  totalPages: number;
}

export const paginatedSchema = <T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(item: T) =>
  z.object({
    items: z.array(item),
    page: z.number().int(),
    pageSize: z.number().int(),
    total: z.number().int(),
    totalPages: z.number().int(),
  });

/** Pakistani mobile: 03XXXXXXXXX or +923XXXXXXXXX (also accepts 0092 / spaces / dashes). */
export const pakistaniPhoneSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Phone number is required")
  .transform((value) => value.replace(/[\s()-]/g, ""))
  .refine((value) => /^(?:\+92|0092|92|0)3\d{9}$/.test(value), {
    message: "Enter a valid Pakistani mobile number, e.g. 0300 1234567",
  });

export const emailSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Email is required")
  .email("Enter a valid email address")
  .max(120, "Email is too long");

export const shortText = (label: string, min = 1, max = 120) =>
  z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(1, `${label} is required`)
    .max(max, `${label} must be under ${max} characters`)
    .refine((value) => value.length >= min, {
      message: `${label} must be at least ${min} characters`,
    });

/** Honeypot field: must stay empty (bots fill it). */
export const honeypotSchema = z.string().max(0, "Spam detected").optional().or(z.literal(""));


========================================
FILE: src\schemas\content.schema.ts
========================================
import { z } from "zod";

/* ---------------- Hero slides ---------------- */
export const heroSlideSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  image: z.string(),
  alt: z.string(),
  heading: z.string().optional(),
  subHeading: z.string().optional(),
  /** Optional accent word inside the heading, rendered in italic peach. */
  accentWord: z.string().optional(),
  ctaLabel: z.string().optional(),
  ctaHref: z.string().optional(),
});
export type HeroSlide = z.infer<typeof heroSlideSchema>;

/* ---------------- Stats ---------------- */
export const statSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  label: z.string(),
  value: z.string(),
  caption: z.string().optional(),
});
export type Stat = z.infer<typeof statSchema>;

/* ---------------- Plans ---------------- */
export const planFeatureSchema = z.object({
  label: z.string(),
  included: z.boolean(),
  note: z.string().optional(),
});
export type PlanFeature = z.infer<typeof planFeatureSchema>;

export const planSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  audience: z.string(),
  priceLabel: z.string(),
  priceNote: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  features: z.array(planFeatureSchema),
  highlight: z.boolean(),
  badge: z.string().optional(),
});
export type Plan = z.infer<typeof planSchema>;

/* ---------------- Consultants ---------------- */
export const consultantSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  role: z.string(),
  experience: z.string(),
  languages: z.array(z.string()),
  rating: z.number().min(0).max(5),
  reviews: z.number().int(),
  cities: z.array(z.string()),
  bio: z.string(),
  photoUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  initials: z.string(),
  focus: z.array(z.string()),
});
export type Consultant = z.infer<typeof consultantSchema>;

/* ---------------- Success stories ---------------- */
export const successStorySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  coupleLabel: z.string(),
  city: z.string(),
  year: z.number().int(),
  quote: z.string(),
  story: z.string(),
  serviceUsed: z.string(),
  isSample: z.boolean(),
});
export type SuccessStory = z.infer<typeof successStorySchema>;

/* ---------------- Videos ---------------- */
export const videoItemSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  title: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  youtubeId: z.string(),
  category: z.enum(["guidance", "bureau", "families"]),
  durationLabel: z.string(),
  publishedAt: z.string(),
});
export type VideoItem = z.infer<typeof videoItemSchema>;

});

  author: z.string(),

  readingMinutes: z.number().int(),
  imageUrl: z.string(),
  relatedIds: z.array(z.string()),
});

export const serviceSchema = z.object({

  title: z.string(),
  shortDescription: z.string(),

  icon: z.enum([
    "heart-handshake",
    "user-round-search",

    "globe",
    "messages-square",
    "users-round",
  ]),
  benefits: z.array(z.string()),
  process: z.array(z.object({ title: z.string(), description: z.string() })),
  faqs: z.array(z.object({ question: z.string(), answer: z.string() })),
  suitedFor: z.array(z.string()),
});
export type ServiceContent = z.infer<typeof serviceSchema>;

FILE: src\schemas\matchFilter.schema.ts

import { z } from "zod";

/** Quick filter used in the home page "Find your perfect match" card. */
export const matchFilterSchema = z
  .object({
    gender: z.string().optional(),
    caste: z.string().optional(),
    maritalStatus: z.string().optional(),
    city: z.string().optional(),
    country: z.string().optional(),
    religion: z.string().optional(),
    sect: z.string().optional(),
    education: z.string().optional(),
    ageFrom: z.coerce.number().int().min(18).max(80).optional(),
    ageTo: z.coerce.number().int().min(18).max(80).optional(),
    q: z.string().trim().max(80).optional(),
  })
  .refine((values) => !values.ageFrom || !values.ageTo || values.ageFrom <= values.ageTo, {
    message: "Minimum age must be less than maximum age",
    path: ["ageTo"],
  });

export type MatchFilterValues = z.infer<typeof matchFilterSchema>;

/** Builds a /profiles query string, dropping empty values. */
export function buildProfilesQuery(
  values: Record<string, string | number | undefined | null>,
): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) return;
    const stringValue = String(value).trim();
    if (!stringValue) return;
    params.set(key, stringValue);
  });
  const query = params.toString();
  return query ? `/profiles?${query}` : "/profiles";
}

import { z } from "zod";

export const GENDERS = ["male", "female"] as const;
export const MARITAL_STATUSES = ["never-married", "divorced", "widowed"] as const;
export const FAMILY_TYPES = ["nuclear", "joint"] as const;
export const RELIGIONS = ["Islam", "Christianity", "Hinduism", "Sikhism"] as const;
export const SECTS = [
  "Sunni",
  "Shia",
  "Deobandi",
  "Barelvi",
  "Ahl-e-Hadith",
  "Other",
  "Prefer not to say",
] as const;

export const genderSchema = z.enum(GENDERS);
export const maritalStatusSchema = z.enum(MARITAL_STATUSES);
export const familyTypeSchema = z.enum(FAMILY_TYPES);

export const partnerDemandsSchema = z.object({
  ageRange: z.string(),
  heightRange: z.string(),
  maritalStatus: z.string(),
  education: z.string(),
  occupation: z.string(),
  city: z.string(),
  notes: z.string(),
});

export type PartnerDemands = z.infer<typeof partnerDemandsSchema>;

export const profileSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  /** First name + last initial only (privacy by design). */
  displayName: z.string(),
  gender: genderSchema,
  age: z.number().int().min(18).max(80),
  /** ISO date — used for age, never rendered raw on public pages. */
  dateOfBirth: z.string(),
  heightCm: z.number().int().min(120).max(215),
  maritalStatus: maritalStatusSchema,
  hasChildren: z.boolean(),
  religion: z.string(),
  sect: z.string(),
  caste: z.string(),
  education: z.string(),
  occupation: z.string(),
  income: z.string(),
  city: z.string(),
  country: z.string(),
  languages: z.array(z.string()),
  familyType: familyTypeSchema,
  about: z.string(),
  partnerDemands: partnerDemandsSchema,
  /** Photos are only published when the family has given written consent. */
  photoConsent: z.boolean(),
  photoUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  verified: z.boolean(),
  packageName: z.string().nullable(),
  featured: z.boolean(),
  createdAt: z.string(),
});

export type Profile = z.infer<typeof profileSchema>;
export type Gender = z.infer<typeof genderSchema>;
export type MaritalStatus = z.infer<typeof maritalStatusSchema>;
export type FamilyType = z.infer<typeof familyTypeSchema>;

export const PROFILE_SORTS = [
  "newest",
  "age-asc",
  "age-desc",
  "height-asc",
  "height-desc",
] as const;
export type ProfileSort = (typeof PROFILE_SORTS)[number];

export const profileSortSchema = z.enum(PROFILE_SORTS).catch("newest");

export const profileFiltersSchema = z.object({
  gender: genderSchema.optional(),
  maritalStatus: maritalStatusSchema.optional(),
  ageFrom: z.coerce.number().int().min(18).max(80).optional(),
  ageTo: z.coerce.number().int().min(18).max(80).optional(),
  heightFrom: z.coerce.number().int().min(120).max(215).optional(),
  heightTo: z.coerce.number().int().min(120).max(215).optional(),
  religion: z.string().optional(),
  sect: z.string().optional(),
  caste: z.string().optional(),
  education: z.string().optional(),
  occupation: z.string().optional(),
  income: z.string().optional(),
  city: z.string().optional(),
  country: z.string().optional(),
  language: z.string().optional(),
  familyType: familyTypeSchema.optional(),
  q: z.string().trim().max(80).optional(),
  sort: profileSortSchema.optional(),
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).optional(),
  view: z.enum(["grid", "list"]).optional(),
  category: z.string().optional(),
});

export type ProfileFilters = z.infer<typeof profileFiltersSchema>;

export const PROFILE_FILTER_KEYS = [
  "gender",
  "maritalStatus",
  "ageFrom",
  "ageTo",
  "heightFrom",
  "heightTo",
  "religion",
  "sect",
  "caste",
  "education",
  "occupation",
  "income",
  "city",
  "country",
  "language",
  "familyType",
  "q",
  "sort",
  "page",
  "view",
  "category",
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof ProfileFilters)[];

export function profileHref(id: string): string {
  return `/profiles/${id}`;
}

export function formatHeight(cm: number): string {
  const totalInches = Math.round(cm / 2.54);
  const feet = Math.floor(totalInches / 12);
  const inches = totalInches % 12;
  return `${feet}' ${inches}"`;
}

export function genderLabel(gender: Gender): string {
  return gender === "male" ? "Groom" : "Bride";
}

export function maritalLabel(status: MaritalStatus): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "divorced":
      return "Divorced";
    case "widowed":
      return "Widowed";
    default:
      return "Never married";
  }
}


========================================
FILE: src\schemas\register.schema.ts
========================================
import { z } from "zod";
import {
  emailSchema,
  honeypotSchema,
  pakistaniPhoneSchema,
  shortText,
} from "@/schemas/common.schema";
import {
  FAMILY_TYPES,
  GENDERS,
  MARITAL_STATUSES,
  RELIGIONS,
  SECTS,
} from "@/schemas/profile.schema";

export const MIN_AGE = 18;
export const MAX_AGE = 70;
/** 4'0" */
export const MIN_HEIGHT_CM = 122;
/** 7'0" */
export const MAX_HEIGHT_CM = 213;

export function ageFromDate(dateOfBirth: string, today = new Date()): number {
  const dob = new Date(dateOfBirth);
  if (Number.isNaN(dob.getTime())) return Number.NaN;
  let age = today.getFullYear() - dob.getFullYear();
  const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - dob.getMonth();
  if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < dob.getDate())) age -= 1;
  return age;
}

export function isoDateYearsAgo(years: number): string {
  const date = new Date();
  date.setFullYear(date.getFullYear() - years);
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

export const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    /* Step 1 — About the proposal */
    registeredBy: z.enum(["self", "parent", "sibling", "relative"], {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please tell us who is registering" }),
    }),
    gender: z.enum(GENDERS, { errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please select bride or groom" }) }),
    maritalStatus: z.enum(MARITAL_STATUSES, {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please select marital status" }),
    }),
    dateOfBirth: z
      .string()
      .min(1, "Date of birth is required")
      .refine((value) => !Number.isNaN(new Date(value).getTime()), "Enter a valid date")
      .refine((value) => new Date(value) <= new Date(), "Date of birth cannot be in the future")
      .refine(
        (value) => ageFromDate(value) >= MIN_AGE,
        `The proposal must be at least ${MIN_AGE} years old`,
      )
      .refine(
        (value) => ageFromDate(value) <= MAX_AGE,
        `Please contact the office for ages above ${MAX_AGE}`,
      ),
    heightCm: z.coerce
      .number({ invalid_type_error: "Please select a height" })
      .int()
      .min(MIN_HEIGHT_CM, "Height must be at least 4'0\"")
      .max(MAX_HEIGHT_CM, "Height must be at most 7'0\""),

    /* Step 2 — Deen & family */
    religion: z.enum(RELIGIONS, { errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please select religion" }) }),
    sect: z.enum(SECTS, {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please select sect or prefer not to say" }),
    }),
    caste: shortText("Caste or biradari", 2, 60),
    familyType: z.enum(FAMILY_TYPES, {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please select family type" }),
    }),
    languages: z
      .array(z.string())
      .min(1, "Select at least one language")
      .max(6, "Select up to 6 languages"),
    siblingsTotal: z.coerce.number().int().min(0, "Cannot be negative").max(20, "Maximum 20"),
    siblingsMarried: z.coerce.number().int().min(0, "Cannot be negative").max(20, "Maximum 20"),
    hasChildren: z.boolean(),

    /* Step 3 — Education & work */
    education: shortText("Education", 2, 80),
    occupation: shortText("Occupation", 2, 80),
    income: shortText("Monthly income range", 2, 40),
    city: shortText("City", 2, 60),
    country: shortText("Country", 2, 60),

    /* Step 4 — Partner preferences */
    partnerAgeFrom: z.coerce.number().int().min(MIN_AGE).max(MAX_AGE),
    partnerAgeTo: z.coerce.number().int().min(MIN_AGE).max(MAX_AGE),
    partnerHeightFrom: z.coerce.number().int().min(MIN_HEIGHT_CM).max(MAX_HEIGHT_CM),
    partnerHeightTo: z.coerce.number().int().min(MIN_HEIGHT_CM).max(MAX_HEIGHT_CM),
    partnerMaritalStatus: z.enum([...MARITAL_STATUSES, "any"] as const),
    partnerEducation: shortText("Preferred education", 2, 80),
    partnerCity: shortText("Preferred city or country", 2, 60),
    partnerNotes: z.string().trim().max(600, "Please keep this under 600 characters").optional(),

    /* Step 5 — Contact */
    contactName: shortText("Your name", 2, 80),
    relation: shortText("Your relation to the proposal", 2, 40),
    phone: pakistaniPhoneSchema,
    email: z.union([emailSchema, z.literal("")]).optional(),
    bestTimeToCall: z.enum(["morning", "afternoon", "evening", "anytime"]),

    /* Step 6 — Consent */
    consentBureau: z.literal(true, {
      errorMap: () => ({
        message: "Please allow the bureau to review and share your details with suitable families",
      }),
    }),
    confirmTruth: z.literal(true, {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please confirm the information is accurate" }),
    }),
    agreeTerms: z.literal(true, {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: "Please accept the terms and privacy policy" }),
    }),

    /* Honeypot */
    company: honeypotSchema,
  })
  .superRefine((values, ctx) => {
    if (values.siblingsMarried > values.siblingsTotal) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
        path: ["siblingsMarried"],
        message: "Married siblings cannot be more than total siblings",
      });
    }
    if (values.partnerAgeFrom > values.partnerAgeTo) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
        path: ["partnerAgeTo"],
        message: "Maximum age must be greater than minimum age",
      });
    }
    if (values.partnerHeightFrom > values.partnerHeightTo) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
        path: ["partnerHeightTo"],
        message: "Maximum height must be greater than minimum height",
      });
    }
    if (values.partnerAgeTo - values.partnerAgeFrom > 30) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,
        path: ["partnerAgeFrom"],
        message: "Please keep the preferred age range within 30 years",
      });
    }
  });

export type RegistrationFormValues = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;
export type RegistrationSubmission = RegistrationFormValues;
